    60→def _is_subjective_queue_item(item: dict) -> bool:
    61→    """Return True for queue items that represent subjective work."""
    62→    return item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
    63→
    64→
    65→def _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(
    66→    items: list[dict],
    67→    *,
    68→    objective_burst: int = 3,
    69→    subjective_burst: int = 1,
    70→) -> list[dict]:
    71→    """Interleave subjective work with objective work to avoid starvation."""
    72→    if objective_burst < 1 or subjective_burst < 1:
    73→        return items
    74→
    75→    subjective_items = deque(
    76→        item for item in items if _is_subjective_queue_item(item)
    77→    )
    78→    objective_items = deque(
    79→        item for item in items if not _is_subjective_queue_item(item)
    80→    )
    81→    if not subjective_items or not objective_items:
    82→        return items
    83→
    84→    interleaved: list[dict] = []
    85→    while objective_items and subjective_items:
    86→        for _ in range(objective_burst):
    87→            if not objective_items:
    88→                break
    89→            interleaved.append(objective_items.popleft())
    90→        for _ in range(subjective_burst):
    91→            if not subjective_items:
    92→                break
    93→            interleaved.append(subjective_items.popleft())
    94→
    95→    interleaved.extend(objective_items)
    96→    interleaved.extend(subjective_items)
    97→    return interleaved
    98→

   100→def _new_item_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   101→    """Return finding IDs added in the most recent scan."""
   102→    scan_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
   103→    if not scan_history:
   104→        return set()
   105→    threshold = scan_history[-1].get("timestamp", "")
   106→    if not threshold:
   107→        return set()
   108→    return {
   109→        fid
   110→        for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   111→        if f.get("first_seen", "") >= threshold
   112→    }
   113→
   114→
   115→def _apply_plan_order(
   116→    items: list[dict],
   117→    plan: dict,
   118→    *,
   119→    include_skipped: bool = False,
   120→    cluster: str | None = None,
   121→    new_ids: set[str] | None = None,
   122→) -> list[dict]:
   123→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
   124→
   125→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
   126→    2. Existing remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
   127→    3. New items (from latest scan) sort after existing remaining items.
   128→    4. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
   129→    5. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
   130→    """
   131→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   132→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   133→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
   134→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
   135→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   136→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
   137→
   138→    # Build lookup
   139→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
   140→    for item in items:
   141→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
   142→
   143→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
   144→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
   145→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
   146→        if override.get("description"):
   147→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
   148→        if override.get("note"):
   149→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
   150→        if override.get("cluster"):
   151→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
   152→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   153→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
   154→                "name": cluster_name,
   155→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
   156→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
   157→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
   158→            }
   159→
   160→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   161→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   162→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   163→    for fid in queue_order:
   164→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   165→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   166→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   167→
   168→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   169→    remaining_existing: list[dict] = []
   170→    remaining_new: list[dict] = []
   171→    _new = new_ids or set()
   172→    for item in items:
   173→        item_id = item["id"]
   174→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   175→            continue
   176→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   177→            skipped_items.append(item)
   178→        elif item_id in _new:
   179→            remaining_new.append(item)
   180→        else:
   181→            remaining_existing.append(item)
   182→
   183→    # Assign queue positions — new items after existing ones
   184→    result = ordered + remaining_existing + remaining_new
   185→    if include_skipped:
   186→        result = result + skipped_items
   187→
   188→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   189→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   190→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   191→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   192→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   193→            if skip_entry:
   194→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   195→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   196→                if skip_reason:
   197→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   198→
   199→    # Filter to cluster if requested
   200→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   201→    if effective_cluster:
   202→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   203→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   204→        if cluster_member_ids:
   205→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   206→
   207→    return result
   208→
   209→
   210→def _item_matches_tier(item: dict, tier: int) -> bool:
   211→    """Check if an item (or any of its members for clusters) matches a tier."""
   212→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   213→        return any(
   214→            int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   215→            for m in item.get("members", [])
   216→        )
   217→    return int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   218→
   219→
   220→def _action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   221→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   222→    try:
   223→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   224→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   225→        if meta:
   226→            return meta.action_type
   227→    except ImportError as exc:
   228→        _ = exc
   229→    return "manual_fix"
   230→
   231→
   232→_ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   233→
   234→
   235→def _collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   236→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items."""
   237→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   238→    if not clusters:
   239→        return items
   240→
   241→    # Build mapping: finding_id → auto-cluster name
   242→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   243→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   244→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   245→            continue
   246→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   247→            fid_to_cluster[fid] = name
   248→
   249→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   250→        return items
   251→
   252→    # Collect members for each cluster, preserving order
   253→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   254→    non_cluster_items: list[dict] = []
   255→
   256→    for item in items:
   257→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   258→        if cname:
   259→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   260→        else:
   261→            non_cluster_items.append(item)
   262→
   263→    # Build cluster meta-items
   264→    result: list[dict] = list(non_cluster_items)
   265→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   266→        # Don't collapse singletons — show them as individual findings
   267→        if len(members) < 2:
   268→            result.extend(members)
   269→            continue
   270→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cname, {})
   271→        detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   272→        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   273→        # Derive action_type from the actual cluster action, not just the detector
   274→        if "desloppify fix" in action:
   275→            action_type = "auto_fix"
   276→        elif "desloppify move" in action:
   277→            action_type = "reorganize"
   278→        else:
   279→            action_type = _action_type_for_detector(detector)
   280→            # If detector says auto_fix but cluster has no fix command, it's really refactor
   281→            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
   282→                action_type = "refactor"
   283→
   284→        # Use stored description but patch the count if it differs from visible members
   285→        stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   286→        total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   287→        if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   288→            # Replace the stored count with the visible count
   289→            summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   290→        else:
   291→            summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   292→
   293→        primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   294→        if not primary_command:
   295→            primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cname} --count 10"
   296→
   297→        meta_item: dict = {
   298→            "id": cname,
   299→            "kind": "cluster",
   300→            "action_type": action_type,
   301→            "summary": summary,
   302→            "members": members,
   303→            "member_count": len(members),
   304→            "primary_command": primary_command,
   305→            "cluster_name": cname,
   306→            "cluster_auto": True,
   307→            "confidence": "high",
   308→            "detector": detector,
   309→            "file": "",
   310→        }
   311→        result.append(meta_item)
   312→
   313→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   314→    return result
   315→
   316→
   317→def build_work_queue(
   318→    state: StateModel,
   319→    *,
   320→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   321→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   322→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   323→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   324→
   325→    status = resolved_options.status
   326→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   327→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   328→    try:
   329→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   330→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   331→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   332→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   333→
   334→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   335→        state,
   336→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   337→        status_filter=status,
   338→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   339→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   340→    )
   341→
   342→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   343→    if (
   344→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   345→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   346→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   347→    ):
   348→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   349→            state,
   350→            state.get("findings", {}),
   351→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   352→        )
   353→        for item in subjective_items:
   354→            if scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   355→                all_items.append(item)
   356→
   357→    # Inject synthesis::pending synthetic item when plan requires it
   358→    if resolved_options.plan and status in {"open", "all"}:
   359→        synth_item = build_synthesis_item(resolved_options.plan, state)
   360→        if synth_item is not None:
   361→            all_items.append(synth_item)
   362→
   363→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   364→
   365→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   366→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
   367→    if resolved_options.plan:
   368→        new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   369→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   370→            all_items,
   371→            resolved_options.plan,
   372→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   373→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   374→            new_ids=new_ids,
   375→        )
   376→
   377→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   378→    should_collapse = (
   379→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   380→        and resolved_options.plan
   381→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   382→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   383→    )
   384→    if should_collapse:
   385→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   386→
   387→    should_interleave = (
   388→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   389→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   390→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   391→        and resolved_options.tier is None
   392→        and not resolved_options.scope
   393→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   394→    )
   395→    if should_interleave:
   396→        all_items = _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(all_items)
   397→
   398→    counts = tier_counts(all_items)
   399→
   400→    requested_tier = (
   401→        int(resolved_options.tier) if resolved_options.tier is not None else None
   402→    )
   403→    selected_tier = requested_tier
   404→    fallback_reason = None
   405→    filtered = all_items
   406→
   407→    if requested_tier is not None:
   408→        filtered = [
   409→            item
   410→            for item in all_items
   411→            if _item_matches_tier(item, requested_tier)
   412→        ]
   413→        if not filtered and not resolved_options.no_tier_fallback:
   414→            chosen = choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier, counts)
   415→            if chosen is not None:
   416→                selected_tier = chosen
   417→                filtered = [
   418→                    item
   419→                    for item in all_items
   420→                    if _item_matches_tier(item, chosen)
   421→                ]
   422→                fallback_reason = (
   423→                    f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open -> showing T{chosen} "
   424→                    "(nearest non-empty)."
   425→                )
   426→        elif not filtered:
   427→            fallback_reason = f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open."
   428→
   429→    total = len(filtered)
   430→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   431→        filtered = filtered[: resolved_options.count]
   432→
   433→    if resolved_options.explain:
   434→        for item in filtered:
   435→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   436→
   437→    available_tiers = [tier for tier, value in counts.items() if value > 0]
   438→    return {
   439→        "items": filtered,
   440→        "total": total,
   441→        "tier_counts": counts,
   442→        "requested_tier": requested_tier,
   443→        "selected_tier": selected_tier,
   444→        "fallback_reason": fallback_reason,
   445→        "available_tiers": available_tiers,
   446→        "grouped": group_queue_items(filtered, "item"),
   447→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   448→    }
   449→

1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    11→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    21→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    22→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    23→)
    24→
    25→
    26→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    27→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    28→    detail = item.get("detail")
    29→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    30→
    31→
    32→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    33→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    34→
    35→
    36→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    37→    detector = item.get("detector")
    38→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    39→        return True
    40→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    41→        return True
    42→    return False
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    47→
    48→
    49→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    50→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    51→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    52→    weight_by_confidence = {
    53→        "high": 1.0,
    54→        "medium": 0.7,
    55→        "low": 0.3,
    56→    }
    57→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    58→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    59→        weight *= 10.0
    60→    return float(weight)
    61→
    62→
    63→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    64→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    65→    if not scope:
    66→        return True
    67→
    68→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    69→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    70→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    71→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    72→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    73→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    74→
    75→    if "*" in scope:
    76→        return any(
    77→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    78→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    if "::" in scope:
    82→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    83→
    84→    lowered = scope.lower()
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    86→        return (
    87→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    88→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    89→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
    93→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    94→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    95→
    96→    return (
    97→        detector == scope
    98→        or filepath == scope
    99→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   100→    )
   101→
   102→
   103→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   104→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   105→
   106→
   107→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   108→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   109→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   110→    target = cleaned.lower()
   111→
   112→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   113→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   114→            return str(dim_key)
   115→    return slugify(cleaned)
   116→
   117→
   118→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   119→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   120→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   121→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   122→    return {
   123→        cleaned.lower(),
   124→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   125→        slugify(cleaned),
   126→        canonical.lower(),
   127→        slugify(canonical),
   128→    }
   129→
   130→
   131→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   132→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   133→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   134→    if not lang:
   135→        return None
   136→
   137→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   138→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   139→        return None
   140→
   141→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   142→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   143→        return None
   144→
   145→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   146→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   147→        return None
   148→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   149→
   150→
   151→def primary_command_for_finding(
   152→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   153→) -> str:
   154→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   155→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   156→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   157→        available_fixers = [
   158→            fixer
   159→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   160→            if supported_fixers is not None and fixer in supported_fixers
   161→        ]
   162→        if available_fixers:
   163→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   164→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   165→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   166→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   167→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   168→    return f'desloppify plan done "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --confirm'
   169→
   170→
   171→def build_synthesis_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> dict | None:
   172→    """Build a synthetic T1 work item for ``synthesis::pending`` if it's in the queue.
   173→
   174→    Returns ``None`` when synthesis is not pending.
   175→    """
   176→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SYNTHESIS_ID
   177→
   178→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   179→        return None
   180→
   181→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   182→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   183→
   184→    open_review_ids = {
   185→        fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   186→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   187→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   188→    }
   189→    synthesized_ids = set(meta.get("synthesized_ids", []))
   190→    new_since = len(open_review_ids - synthesized_ids)
   191→    resolved_since = len(synthesized_ids - open_review_ids)
   192→
   193→    return {
   194→        "id": SYNTHESIS_ID,
   195→        "tier": 1,
   196→        "confidence": "high",
   197→        "detector": "synthesis",
   198→        "file": ".",
   199→        "kind": "synthesis_needed",
   200→        "primary_command": "desloppify plan synthesize",
   201→        "summary": (
   202→            f"Synthesize {len(open_review_ids)} review findings into a coherent plan"
   203→        ),
   204→        "detail": {
   205→            "total_review_findings": len(open_review_ids),
   206→            "new_since_last": new_since,
   207→            "resolved_since_last": resolved_since,
   208→        },
   209→    }
   210→
   211→
   212→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   213→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   214→    if not dim_scores:
   215→        return {}
   216→
   217→    entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   218→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   219→    for entry in entries:
   220→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   221→        if not name:
   222→            continue
   223→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   224→        [REDACTED](name)
   225→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
   226→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   227→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   228→            if not key:
   229→                continue
   230→            aliases.add(key)
   231→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   232→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   233→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   234→        for alias in aliases:
   235→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   236→    return scores
   237→
   238→
   239→def build_subjective_items(
   240→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   241→) -> list[dict]:
   242→    """Create synthetic subjective work items (always tier 4)."""
   243→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   244→    if not dim_scores:
   245→        return []
   246→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   247→
   248→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   249→    if not subjective_entries:
   250→        return []
   251→    unassessed_dims = {
   252→        str(name).strip()
   253→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   254→            dim_scores
   255→        )
   256→    }
   257→
   258→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   259→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   260→    for finding in findings.values():
   261→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   262→            continue
   263→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   264→        if not dim_key:
   265→            continue
   266→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   267→
   268→    items: list[dict] = []
   269→    def _prepare_command(
   270→        cli_keys: list[str],
   271→        *,
   272→        force_review_rerun: bool = False,
   273→    ) -> str:
   274→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   275→        if cli_keys:
   276→            command += " --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   277→        if force_review_rerun:
   278→            command += " --force-review-rerun"
   279→        return command
   280→
   281→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   282→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   283→        if not name:
   284→            continue
   285→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   286→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   287→            continue
   288→
   289→        [REDACTED](name)
   290→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   291→        cli_keys = [
   292→            str(key).strip().lower()
   293→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   294→            if str(key).strip()
   295→        ]
   296→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   297→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   298→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   299→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   300→            name in unassessed_dims
   301→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   302→        )
   303→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   304→        # If review findings already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   305→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   306→        if open_review > 0:
   307→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   308→        elif is_unassessed:
   309→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   310→        elif is_stale:
   311→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   312→                cli_keys,
   313→                force_review_rerun=True,
   314→            )
   315→        elif cli_keys:
   316→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   317→                cli_keys,
   318→                force_review_rerun=True,
   319→            )
   320→        else:
   321→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   322→                cli_keys,
   323→                force_review_rerun=True,
   324→            )
   325→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   326→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   327→        items.append(
   328→            {
   329→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   330→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   331→                "file": ".",
   332→                "tier": 4,
   333→                "effective_tier": 4,
   334→                "confidence": "medium",
   335→                "summary": summary,
   336→                "detail": {
   337→                    "dimension_name": name,
   338→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   339→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   340→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   341→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   342→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   343→                },
   344→                "status": "open",
   345→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   346→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   347→            }
   348→        )
   349→    return items
   350→
   351→
   352→__all__ = [

   354→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",

1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import hashlib
    14→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    15→
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    17→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    18→
    19→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    20→SYNTHESIS_ID = "synthesis::pending"
    21→
    22→
    23→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    24→# Result dataclasses
    25→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26→
    27→@dataclass
    28→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    29→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    30→
    31→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    32→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    33→
    34→    @property
    35→    def changes(self) -> int:
    36→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    37→
    38→
    39→@dataclass
    40→class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    41→    """What changed during an unscored-dimension sync."""
    42→
    43→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    44→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    45→
    46→    @property
    47→    def changes(self) -> int:
    48→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    49→
    50→
    51→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    52→# ID helpers
    53→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    54→
    55→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    56→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    57→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    58→    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    59→        scorecard_subjective_entries,
    60→    )
    61→
    62→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    63→    if not dim_scores:
    64→        return set()
    65→
    66→    stale: set[str] = set()
    67→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    68→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    69→            continue
    70→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    71→        if dim_key:
    72→            stale.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    73→    return stale
    74→
    75→
    76→def _current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    77→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    78→
    79→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` directly because
    80→    ``scorecard_subjective_entries`` filters out placeholder dimensions
    81→    (they are hidden from the scorecard display).
    82→    """
    83→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    84→
    85→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    86→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    87→        return set()
    88→
    89→    unscored: set[str] = set()
    90→    for dim_key, payload in assessments.items():
    91→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    92→            continue
    93→        if not payload.get("placeholder"):
    94→            continue
    95→        if dim_key:
    96→            unscored.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    97→    return unscored
    98→
    99→
   100→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   101→# Promoted-aware insertion helper
   102→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   103→
   104→def _after_promoted(order: list[str], plan: PlanModel) -> int:
   105→    """Return the insertion index just after the last promoted item in *order*.
   106→
   107→    When no promoted items are present (or none are in *order*), returns 0
   108→    so callers fall back to existing front-of-queue behavior.
   109→    """
   110→    promoted = set(plan.get("promoted_ids", []))
   111→    if not promoted:
   112→        return 0
   113→    last_idx = -1
   114→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   115→        if fid in promoted:
   116→            last_idx = i
   117→    return last_idx + 1 if last_idx >= 0 else 0
   118→
   119→
   120→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   121→# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)
   122→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   123→
   124→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   125→    plan: PlanModel,
   126→    state: StateModel,
   127→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   128→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   129→
   130→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   131→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   132→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   133→    2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to the
   134→       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.
   135→    """
   136→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   137→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   138→    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   139→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   140→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   141→
   142→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   143→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   144→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   145→        fid for fid in order
   146→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   147→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   148→        and fid not in stale_ids
   149→    ]
   150→    for fid in to_remove:
   151→        order.remove(fid)
   152→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   153→
   154→    # --- Inject: prepend unscored IDs after any promoted items -------------
   155→    existing = set(order)
   156→    insert_at = _after_promoted(order, plan)
   157→    for uid in reversed(sorted(unscored_ids)):
   158→        if uid not in existing:
   159→            order.insert(insert_at, uid)
   160→            result.injected.append(uid)
   161→
   162→    return result
   163→
   164→
   165→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   166→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   167→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   168→
   169→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   170→    plan: PlanModel,
   171→    state: StateModel,
   172→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   173→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale subjective dimensions.
   174→
   175→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   176→       longer stale and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned by
   177→       ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   178→    2. If no objective items remain after cleanup, inject all currently-stale
   179→       dimension IDs so the plan surfaces them as actionable work.
   180→    """
   181→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   182→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   183→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   184→    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   185→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   186→
   187→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   188→    # Only prune IDs that are neither stale nor unscored.
   189→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   190→        fid for fid in order
   191→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   192→        and fid not in stale_ids
   193→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   194→    ]
   195→    for fid in to_remove:
   196→        order.remove(fid)
   197→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   198→
   199→    # --- Inject: populate when no objective items remain -----------------
   200→    has_real_items = any(fid for fid in order if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX))
   201→    if not has_real_items and stale_ids:
   202→        existing = set(order)
   203→        for sid in sorted(stale_ids):
   204→            if sid not in existing:
   205→                order.append(sid)
   206→                result.injected.append(sid)
   207→
   208→    return result
   209→
   210→
   211→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   212→# Synthesis snapshot hash + sync
   213→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   214→
   215→def review_finding_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   216→    """Hash open review finding IDs to detect changes.
   217→
   218→    Returns empty string when there are no open review findings.
   219→    """
   220→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   221→    review_ids = sorted(
   222→        fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   223→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   224→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   225→    )
   226→    if not review_ids:
   227→        return ""
   228→    return hashlib.sha256("|".join(review_ids).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
   229→
   230→
   231→@dataclass
   232→class SynthesisSyncResult:
   233→    """What changed during a synthesis sync."""
   234→
   235→    injected: bool = False
   236→    pruned: bool = False
   237→
   238→    @property
   239→    def changes(self) -> int:
   240→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   241→
   242→
   243→def sync_synthesis_needed(
   244→    plan: PlanModel,
   245→    state: StateModel,
   246→) -> SynthesisSyncResult:
   247→    """Inject ``synthesis::pending`` at front of queue when review findings change.
   248→
   249→    Never auto-prunes — only explicit completion (``_apply_completion``) removes it.
   250→    """
   251→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   252→    result = SynthesisSyncResult()
   253→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   254→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   255→    already_present = SYNTHESIS_ID in order
   256→
   257→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   258→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   259→
   260→    # If hash changed and not already present, inject after promoted items
   261→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash and not already_present:
   262→        order.insert(_after_promoted(order, plan), SYNTHESIS_ID)
   263→        result.injected = True
   264→
   265→    # Never auto-prune: synthesis::pending is only removed by explicit completion
   266→
   267→    return result
   268→
   269→
   270→def compute_new_finding_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   271→    """Return the set of open review/concerns finding IDs added since last synthesis.
   272→
   273→    Returns an empty set when no prior synthesis has recorded ``synthesized_ids``.
   274→    """
   275→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   276→    synthesized = set(meta.get("synthesized_ids", []))
   277→    current = {
   278→        fid for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   279→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   280→    }
   281→    return current - synthesized if synthesized else set()
   282→
   283→
   284→def is_synthesis_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   285→    """Side-effect-free check: is synthesis needed?
   286→
   287→    Returns True when ``synthesis::pending`` is in the queue OR the
   288→    review-finding snapshot hash has drifted from the last completed synthesis.
   289→    """
   290→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   291→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   292→    if SYNTHESIS_ID in order:
   293→        return True
   294→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   295→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   296→    if not last_hash:
   297→        return False
   298→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   299→    return bool(current_hash and current_hash != last_hash)
   300→
   301→
   302→__all__ = [
   303→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   304→    "SYNTHESIS_ID",
   305→    "StaleDimensionSyncResult",
   306→    "SynthesisSyncResult",
   307→    "UnscoredDimensionSyncResult",
   308→    "_current_unscored_ids",
   309→    "compute_new_finding_ids",
   310→    "is_synthesis_stale",
   311→    "review_finding_snapshot_hash",
   312→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
   313→    "sync_synthesis_needed",
   314→    "sync_unscored_dimensions",
   315→]
   316→

    29→def path_scoped_findings(

    33→    """Filter findings to those within the given scan path."""

    41→def finding_in_scan_scope(file_path: str, scan_path: str | None) -> bool:
    42→    """Return True when a file path belongs to the active scan scope."""
    43→    if not scan_path or scan_path == ".":
    44→        return True
    45→    prefix = scan_path.rstrip("/") + "/"
    46→    return (
    47→        file_path.startswith(prefix)
    48→        or file_path == scan_path
    49→        or file_path == "."
    50→    )
    51→
    52→
    53→def open_scope_breakdown(
    54→    findings: dict[str, Finding],

    56→    *,
    57→    detector: str | None = None,
    58→) -> dict[str, int]:

    61→    out_of_scope = 0
    62→
    63→    for finding in findings.values():
    64→        if finding.get("suppressed"):

    66→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    67→            continue
    68→        if detector is not None and finding.get("detector") != detector:
    69→            continue
    70→        file_path = str(finding.get("file", ""))
    71→        if finding_in_scan_scope(file_path, scan_path):
    72→            in_scope += 1
    73→        else:
    74→            out_of_scope += 1
    75→
    76→    return {
    77→        "in_scope": in_scope,
    78→        "out_of_scope": out_of_scope,

    83→def is_ignored(finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]) -> bool:

    85→    return matched_ignore_pattern(finding_id, file, ignore_patterns) is not None
    86→
    87→
    88→def matched_ignore_pattern(
    89→    finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]
    90→) -> str | None:
    91→    """Return the ignore pattern that matched, if any."""
    92→    for pattern in ignore_patterns:
    93→        if "*" in pattern:
    94→            target = finding_id if "::" in pattern else file
    95→            if fnmatch.fnmatch(target, pattern):
    96→                return pattern
    97→            continue
    98→
    99→        if "::" in pattern:
   100→            if finding_id.startswith(pattern):
   101→                return pattern
   102→            continue
   103→
   104→        if file == pattern or file == rel(pattern):
   105→            return pattern
   106→
   107→    return None
   108→
   109→
   110→def remove_ignored_findings(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   111→    """Suppress findings matching an ignore pattern. Returns count affected."""
   112→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   113→    matched_ids = [
   114→        finding_id
   115→        for finding_id, finding in state["findings"].items()
   116→        if is_ignored(finding_id, finding["file"], [pattern])
   117→    ]
   118→    now = utc_now()
   119→    for finding_id in matched_ids:
   120→        finding = state["findings"][finding_id]
   121→        finding["suppressed"] = True
   122→        finding["suppressed_at"] = now
   123→        finding["suppression_pattern"] = pattern
   124→    # Deferred import to avoid circular dependency with engine._state.scoring
   125→    from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
   126→
   127→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   128→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   129→    return len(matched_ids)
   130→
   131→
   132→def add_ignore(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   133→    """Add an ignore pattern and remove existing matching findings."""
   134→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   135→    config = state.setdefault("config", {})
   136→    ignores = config.setdefault("ignore", [])
   137→    if pattern not in ignores:
   138→        ignores.append(pattern)
   139→    return remove_ignored_findings(state, pattern)

   149→    summary: str,

   154→    finding_id = f"{detector}::{rfile}::{name}" if name else f"{detector}::{rfile}"
   155→    now = utc_now()

   170→    }

   172→
   173→_HEX8_RE = re.compile(r'^[0-9a-f]{8}$')
   174→
   175→
   176→def _matches_pattern(finding_id: str, finding: dict[str, str], pattern: str) -> bool:
   177→    """Check if a finding matches by ID, glob, prefix, detector, suffix, or path."""
   178→    if finding_id == pattern:
   179→        return True
   180→    if "*" in pattern and fnmatch.fnmatch(finding_id, pattern):
   181→        return True
   182→    if "::" in pattern and finding_id.startswith(pattern):
   183→        return True
   184→    if _HEX8_RE.match(pattern) and finding_id.endswith("::" + pattern):
   185→        return True
   186→    return (
   187→        finding.get("detector") == pattern
   188→        or finding["file"] == pattern
   189→        or finding["file"].startswith(pattern.rstrip("/") + "/")
   190→    )

## Comprehensive Analysis: Stale Subjective Dimensions and the Work Queue in the `next` Command

### 1. **How Subjective Work Items Get Into the Queue**

#### Primary Source: `build_subjective_items()` in `helpers.py` (lines 239-349)

**Key Flow:**
1. Reads `dimension_scores` from state
2. Calls `scorecard_subjective_entries()` to get dimension entries
3. Filters entries below a `threshold` (default 100.0, overridable)
4. For each dimension below threshold, creates a synthetic queue item with:
   - `id`: `subjective::<slugified-dimension-name>`
   - `kind`: `subjective_dimension`
   - `detector`: `subjective_assessment`
   - `tier`: 4 (always tier 4)
   - `status`: `open`

**Conditional Injection into Queue (in `core.py`, lines 342-355):**
The work queue only includes subjective items when ALL of these are true:
- `include_subjective=True` (default in QueueBuildOptions)
- `status` is "open" or "all"
- NOT in chronic mode (`chronic=False`)

```python
if (
    resolved_options.include_subjective
    and status in {"open", "all"}
    and not resolved_options.chronic
):
    subjective_items = build_subjective_items(...)
    for item in subjective_items:
        if scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
            all_items.append(item)
```

### 2. **Ordering of Subjective Items Relative to Objective Items**

#### Two-Stage Ordering Process:

**Stage 1: Initial Mechanical Sort (line 363)**
```python
all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
```
This sorts ALL items (objective + subjective) by a mechanical sort key.

**Stage 2: Interleave Guardrail (lines 387-396)**
After plan ordering is applied, if conditions are met, subjective items are **interleaved** with objective items to prevent starvation:

```python
should_interleave = (
    resolved_options.include_subjective
    and status in {"open", "all"}
    and not resolved_options.chronic
    and resolved_options.tier is None          # ← No tier filtering
    and not resolved_options.scope             # ← No scope filtering
    and not resolved_options.cluster           # ← No cluster filtering
)
if should_interleave:
    all_items = _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(all_items)
```

**The Interleave Pattern (lines 65-97):**
- Objective burst: 3 items
- Subjective burst: 1 item
- Repeats until one category exhausts
- Remaining items appended in order

This means subjective items are NOT starved out, but also not prioritized over objective items.

### 3. **Gating Based on Objective Backlog Size**

#### Primary Gating: `sync_stale_dimensions()` in `stale_dimensions.py` (lines 169-208)

**Critical Logic (lines 199-206):**
```python
has_real_items = any(fid for fid in order if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX))
if not has_real_items and stale_ids:
    existing = set(order)
    for sid in sorted(stale_ids):
        if sid not in existing:
            order.append(sid)
            result.injected.append(sid)
```

**Key Insight:** Stale subjective dimensions are ONLY injected into the plan queue when:
1. **NO objective items remain** in the queue (`has_real_items == False`)
2. AND there are stale dimensions to inject (`stale_ids` is non-empty)

When objective items exist, stale subjective IDs are NOT in the queue, BUT they are still available via the subjective build process and get interleaved.

**Distinction Between Queue and Subjective Items:**
- **Plan Queue (`queue_order`):** Manual + objective items + unscored dimensions (front) + stale dimensions (ONLY if no objectives)
- **Subjective Items (from `build_subjective_items()`):** Always computed dynamically from scorecard, filtered by threshold, then interleaved with queue items

### 4. **The `include_subjective` Parameter Flow**

#### In `next.py` (lines 204-220):

```python
queue = build_work_queue(
    state,
    options=QueueBuildOptions(
        tier=tier,
        count=count,
        scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
        scope=scope,
        status=status,
        include_subjective=True,                    # ← Always True
        subjective_threshold=target_strict,         # ← From config or 95.0
        no_tier_fallback=no_tier_fallback,
        explain=explain,
        plan=plan_data,
        include_skipped=include_skipped,
        cluster=effective_cluster,
    ),
)
```

**Key Points:**
- `include_subjective` is **hardcoded to `True`** in the `next` command (line 212)
- `subjective_threshold` is derived from config's target_strict_score (default 95.0 fallback)
- This threshold filters dimensions: only dimensions with `strict < threshold` get queue items

#### Threshold Application in `build_subjective_items()` (lines 286-287):
```python
if strict_val >= threshold:
    continue  # Skip dimensions at or above threshold
```

### 5. **Stale Dimension Detection and Marking**

#### In `helpers.py`, `build_subjective_items()` (lines 303-314):

```python
is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
...
if open_review > 0:
    primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
elif is_unassessed:
    primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
elif is_stale:
    primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys, force_review_rerun=True)
```

The `stale` flag is set elsewhere in the scorecard computation, and when detected:
- The summary gets appended: ` [stale — re-review]`
- The primary command includes `--force-review-rerun`

### 6. **Plan Ordering Integration**

#### In `core.py`, `_apply_plan_order()` (lines 115-207):

After subjective items are built and mechanically sorted:

1. **Annotates items with plan metadata** (lines 143-158):
   - Plan descriptions, notes, cluster assignments

2. **Reorders by plan queue** (lines 160-166):
   - Items in `queue_order` appear first, in order
   - Creates three buckets: ordered, remaining_existing, remaining_new

3. **Preserves new items last** (lines 171-181):
   - Items from latest scan sorted after existing remaining items

4. **Does NOT distinguish subjective in reordering:**
   - Subjective IDs in `queue_order` are treated the same as objective IDs
   - They follow the same bucketing rules

### 7. **Complete Flow in `next` Command**

```
cmd_next()
  ↓
_get_items()
  ↓
build_work_queue(
  options: include_subjective=True, threshold=95.0
)
  ├─ build_finding_items()       ← Objective work
  │  └─ scan_path filter
  │
  ├─ if include_subjective: build_subjective_items()
  │  ├─ Read dimension_scores
  │  ├─ Filter by threshold (95.0)
  │  └─ Create tier-4 queue items
  │
  ├─ build_synthesis_item()       ← If synthesis::pending in queue
  │
  ├─ Sort all_items by item_sort_key
  │
  ├─ if plan_data: _apply_plan_order()
  │  └─ Reorder per queue_order, annotate metadata
  │
  ├─ if should_collapse: _collapse_clusters()
  │
  ├─ if should_interleave: _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail()
  │  └─ 3 objective : 1 subjective pattern
  │
  └─ Return filtered items (with count limit)
```

### 8. **Critical Interactions**

| Aspect | Details |
|--------|---------|
| **Stale detection** | `entry.get("stale")` flag set by scorecard computation |
| **Sync timing** | `sync_stale_dimensions()` runs after scan, only injects stale IDs if no objectives remain |
| **Threshold behavior** | Dimensions ≥ threshold are filtered OUT; stale dimensions BELOW threshold show as items |
| **Plan queue gating** | Stale IDs in `queue_order` only when no objectives present |
| **Subjective items** | Built fresh each command, independent of plan queue — always available if below threshold |
| **Interleave** | Applied AFTER plan ordering, prevents subjective starvation but doesn't prioritize them |
| **Cluster focus** | Disables interleaving; cluster members sorted normally |
| **Tier filtering** | Disables interleaving; only tier-4 subjective items shown |

### 9. **Summary**

**Stale subjective dimensions interact with the work queue through a dual-path system:**

1. **Plan Queue Path**: Stale IDs are synced INTO `queue_order` ONLY when objective backlog is empty — this is a "last resort" mechanism to surface work when there's nothing else to do.

2. **Subjective Items Path**: Every `next` command computes subjective items from dimension_scores, filters by threshold, then **interleaves** them 3:1 with objective work — this ensures they're always visible and actionable, regardless of plan state.

3. **The Threshold Acts as Primary Gate**: Only dimensions with `strict < target_strict` generate queue items. Stale dimensions below threshold get marked with `[stale — re-review]` and get `--force-review-rerun` in their command, but they're still subject to the same threshold filtering as any other subjective dimension.

The key insight is that **stale subjective work is never starved** — it appears either in the plan queue (if objectives are gone) or in the interleaved subjective items (if objectives remain).

    24→def coerce_assessment_score(value: object) -> float | None:
    25→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float.
    26→
    27→    Returns ``None`` when the value cannot be interpreted as a numeric score
    28→    (e.g. bools, non-numeric strings, missing keys).
    29→    """
    30→    if is_numeric(value):
    31→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))), 1)
    32→    if isinstance(value, dict):
    33→        raw = value.get("score")
    34→        if not is_numeric(raw):
    35→            return None
    36→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(raw))), 1)
    37→    return None
    38→

    40→def _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
    41→    state: StateModel,
    42→    *,
    43→    status: str,
    44→    resolved_findings: list[dict],
    45→    now: str,
    46→) -> None:
    47→    """Mark subjective assessments as stale when review findings are resolved.
    48→
    49→    The assessment score is preserved (not zeroed) — only a fresh review import
    50→    should change dimension scores.  The stale marker tells the UI to prompt
    51→    for a re-review.
    52→    """
    53→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    54→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    55→        return
    56→
    57→    touched_dimensions: set[str] = set()
    58→    for finding in resolved_findings:
    59→        if finding.get("detector") != "review":
    60→            continue
    61→        dimension = str(finding.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")).strip()
    62→        if dimension:
    63→            touched_dimensions.add(dimension)
    64→
    65→    for dimension in sorted(touched_dimensions):
    66→        if dimension not in assessments:
    67→            continue
    68→
    69→        payload = assessments.get(dimension)
    70→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
    71→            payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    72→            payload["refresh_reason"] = f"review_finding_{status}"
    73→            payload["stale_since"] = now
    74→        else:
    75→            assessments[dimension] = {
    76→                "score": coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0,
    77→                "needs_review_refresh": True,
    78→                "refresh_reason": f"review_finding_{status}",
    79→                "stale_since": now,
    80→            }
    81→
    82→
    83→def match_findings(
    84→    state: StateModel, pattern: str, status_filter: str = "open"
    85→) -> list[dict]:
    86→    """Return findings matching *pattern* with the given status."""
    87→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
    88→    return [
    89→        finding
    90→        for finding_id, finding in state["findings"].items()
    91→        if not finding.get("suppressed")
    92→        if (status_filter == "all" or finding["status"] == status_filter)
    93→        and _matches_pattern(finding_id, finding, pattern)
    94→    ]
    95→
    96→
    97→def resolve_findings(
    98→    state: StateModel,
    99→    pattern: str,
   100→    status: str,
   101→    note: str | None = None,
   102→    attestation: str | None = None,
   103→) -> list[str]:
   104→    """Set finding status for matches and return affected finding IDs."""
   105→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   106→    now = utc_now()
   107→    resolved: list[str] = []
   108→    resolved_findings: list[dict] = []
   109→    status_filter = "all" if status == "open" else "open"
   110→    for finding in match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter):
   111→        previous_status = str(finding.get("status", "open")).strip() or "open"
   112→        if status == "open" and previous_status == "open":
   113→            continue
   114→
   115→        extra_updates: dict[str, object] = {}
   116→        if status == "wontfix":
   117→            snapshot_scan_count = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   118→            extra_updates["wontfix_scan_count"] = snapshot_scan_count
   119→            extra_updates["wontfix_snapshot"] = {
   120→                "captured_at": now,
   121→                "scan_count": snapshot_scan_count,
   122→                "tier": finding.get("tier"),
   123→                "confidence": finding.get("confidence"),
   124→                "detail": copy.deepcopy(finding.get("detail", {})),
   125→            }
   126→        if status == "open":
   127→            finding["reopen_count"] = int(finding.get("reopen_count", 0) or 0) + 1
   128→            finding.pop("wontfix_scan_count", None)
   129→            finding.pop("wontfix_snapshot", None)
   130→            previous_note = finding.get("note")
   131→            next_note = note if note is not None else previous_note
   132→            extra_updates["resolved_at"] = None
   133→            extra_updates["note"] = next_note
   134→            extra_updates["resolution_attestation"] = {
   135→                "kind": "manual_reopen",
   136→                "text": attestation or note,
   137→                "attested_at": now,
   138→                "scan_verified": False,
   139→                "previous_status": previous_status,
   140→            }
   141→
   142→        updates: dict[str, object] = {
   143→            "status": status,
   144→            "note": note,
   145→            "resolved_at": now,
   146→            "suppressed": False,
   147→            "suppressed_at": None,
   148→            "suppression_pattern": None,
   149→            "resolution_attestation": {
   150→                "kind": "manual",
   151→                "text": attestation,
   152→                "attested_at": now,
   153→                "scan_verified": False,
   154→            },
   155→        }
   156→        updates.update(extra_updates)
   157→        finding.update(updates)
   158→        resolved.append(finding["id"])
   159→        resolved_findings.append(finding)
   160→
   161→    _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
   162→        state,
   163→        status=status,
   164→        resolved_findings=resolved_findings,
   165→        now=now,
   166→    )
   167→
   168→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   169→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   170→    return resolved
   171→

1→"""Preflight guards for subjective review reruns.
     2→
     3→Blocks reruns while backlog remains (objective findings and/or subjective queue
     4→work), unless ``--force-review-rerun`` is set. Also clears stale subjective
     5→markers before a new cycle.

     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→import re
    11→import sys

    13→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    14→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue

    18→
    19→
    20→def clear_stale_subjective_entries(
    21→    state: StateModel,
    22→    *,
    23→    dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    24→) -> list[str]:
    25→    """Clear ``needs_review_refresh`` markers from subjective assessments.
    26→
    27→    When *dimensions* is provided, only those dimensions are cleared;
    28→    otherwise all stale dimensions are cleared.
    29→
    30→    Returns the list of dimension keys that were cleared.
    31→    """
    32→    assessments: dict = state.get("subjective_assessments", {})
    33→    cleared: list[str] = []
    34→    for dim_key, assessment in assessments.items():
    35→        if not isinstance(assessment, dict):
    36→            continue
    37→        if dimensions is not None and dim_key not in dimensions:
    38→            continue
    39→        if assessment.get("needs_review_refresh"):
    40→            assessment.pop("needs_review_refresh", None)
    41→            assessment.pop("stale_since", None)
    42→            assessment.pop("refresh_reason", None)
    43→            cleared.append(dim_key)
    44→    return cleared
    45→
    46→
    47→def _scored_dimensions(state: StateModel) -> list[str]:
    48→    """Return dimension keys that already have a nonzero subjective score."""
    49→    assessments: dict = state.get("subjective_assessments", {})
    50→    scored: list[str] = []
    51→    for dim_key, assessment in assessments.items():
    52→        if isinstance(assessment, dict):
    53→            if assessment.get("score", 0):
    54→                scored.append(dim_key)
    55→        elif isinstance(assessment, (int, float)) and assessment:
    56→            scored.append(dim_key)
    57→    return sorted(scored)
    58→
    59→
    60→def _normalize_dimension_key(raw: object) -> str:
    61→    """Normalize a dimension key/name to canonical snake_case."""
    62→    text = str(raw or "").strip().lower().replace(" ", "_")
    63→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text).strip("_")
    64→
    65→
    66→def _count_open_subjective_queue_items(
    67→    state: StateModel,
    68→    *,
    69→    dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    70→) -> int:
    71→    """Count open synthetic subjective queue items for rerun gating."""
    72→    normalized_dims = (
    73→        {_normalize_dimension_key(dim) for dim in dimensions}
    74→        if dimensions is not None
    75→        else None
    76→    )
    77→    queue = build_work_queue(
    78→        state,
    79→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    80→            status="open",
    81→            include_subjective=True,
    82→            count=None,
    83→        ),
    84→    )
    85→    count = 0
    86→    for item in queue.get("items", []):
    87→        if item.get("kind") != "subjective_dimension":
    88→            continue
    89→        if normalized_dims is None:
    90→            count += 1
    91→            continue
    92→        detail = item.get("detail")
    93→        raw_dim = detail.get("dimension", "") if isinstance(detail, dict) else ""
    94→        item_dim = _normalize_dimension_key(raw_dim)
    95→        if item_dim and item_dim in normalized_dims:
    96→            count += 1
    97→    return count
    98→
    99→
   100→def _blocking_scored_dimensions(
   101→    state: StateModel,
   102→    *,
   103→    dimensions: set[str] | None,
   104→    normalized_dimensions: set[str] | None,
   105→) -> list[str]:
   106→    scored_dims = _scored_dimensions(state)
   107→    if dimensions is None:
   108→        return scored_dims
   109→    normalized = normalized_dimensions or set()
   110→    return sorted(
   111→        dim
   112→        for dim in scored_dims
   113→        if _normalize_dimension_key(dim) in normalized
   114→    )
   115→
   116→
   117→def _objective_and_subjective_backlog(
   118→    state: StateModel,
   119→    *,
   120→    blocking_dims: list[str],
   121→) -> tuple[int, int]:
   122→    objective_result = build_work_queue(
   123→        state,
   124→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   125→            status="open",
   126→            include_subjective=False,
   127→            count=None,
   128→        ),
   129→    )
   130→    objective_total = objective_result["total"]
   131→    subjective_total = _count_open_subjective_queue_items(
   132→        state,
   133→        dimensions=set(blocking_dims),
   134→    )
   135→    return objective_total, subjective_total
   136→
   137→

   179→        if unscored:

   196→
   197→def review_rerun_preflight(
   198→    state: StateModel,
   199→    args,
   200→    *,
   201→    state_file=None,
   202→    save_fn=save_state,
   203→) -> None:
   204→    """Single entry point: gate check -> clear stale -> save.
   205→
   206→    Exits with code 1 when open backlog exists and ``--force-review-rerun`` is
   207→    not set. On success, clears stale subjective markers for targeted
   208→    dimensions and persists state.

   210→    dimensions = parse_dimensions(args)
   211→    normalized_dimensions = (
   212→        {_normalize_dimension_key(dim) for dim in dimensions}
   213→        if dimensions is not None
   214→        else None
   215→    )
   216→
   217→    # --force-review-rerun bypasses the gate
   218→    if getattr(args, "force_review_rerun", False):
   219→        print(
   220→            colorize(
   221→                "  --force-review-rerun: bypassing rerun backlog checks.",
   222→                "yellow",
   223→            )
   224→        )
   225→    else:
   226→        # Only gate dimensions that are actually targeted by this review run.
   227→        blocking_dims = _blocking_scored_dimensions(
   228→            state,
   229→            dimensions=dimensions,
   230→            normalized_dimensions=normalized_dimensions,
   231→        )
   232→
   233→        # No gate when none of the targeted dimensions have prior scores —
   234→        # this is a first run for these dimensions, not a rerun.
   235→        if blocking_dims:
   236→            objective_total, subjective_total = _objective_and_subjective_backlog(
   237→                state,
   238→                blocking_dims=blocking_dims,
   239→            )
   240→            if objective_total > 0 or subjective_total > 0:
   241→                _print_backlog_blocked_message(
   242→                    blocking_dims=blocking_dims,
   243→                    objective_total=objective_total,
   244→                    subjective_total=subjective_total,
   245→                    dimensions=dimensions,
   246→                )
   247→                sys.exit(1)
   248→
   249→    # Gate passed — clear stale for targeted dims
   250→    cleared = clear_stale_subjective_entries(state, dimensions=dimensions)
   251→    if cleared and state_file:
   252→        save_fn(state, state_file)
   253→        print(
   254→            colorize(
   255→                f"  Cleared stale review markers: {', '.join(sorted(cleared))}",

   257→            )
   258→        )
