1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    11→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    21→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    22→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    23→)
    24→
    25→
    26→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    27→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    28→    detail = item.get("detail")
    29→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    30→
    31→
    32→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    33→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    34→
    35→
    36→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    37→    detector = item.get("detector")
    38→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    39→        return True
    40→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    41→        return True
    42→    return False
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    47→
    48→
    49→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    50→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    51→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    52→    weight_by_confidence = {
    53→        "high": 1.0,
    54→        "medium": 0.7,
    55→        "low": 0.3,
    56→    }
    57→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    58→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    59→        weight *= 10.0
    60→    return float(weight)
    61→
    62→
    63→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    64→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    65→    if not scope:
    66→        return True
    67→
    68→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    69→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    70→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    71→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    72→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    73→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    74→
    75→    if "*" in scope:
    76→        return any(
    77→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    78→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    if "::" in scope:
    82→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    83→
    84→    lowered = scope.lower()
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    86→        return (
    87→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    88→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    89→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
    93→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    94→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    95→
    96→    return (
    97→        detector == scope
    98→        or filepath == scope
    99→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   100→    )
   101→
   102→
   103→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   104→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   105→
   106→
   107→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   108→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   109→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   110→    target = cleaned.lower()
   111→
   112→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   113→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   114→            return str(dim_key)
   115→    return slugify(cleaned)
   116→
   117→
   118→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   119→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   120→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   121→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   122→    return {
   123→        cleaned.lower(),
   124→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   125→        slugify(cleaned),
   126→        canonical.lower(),
   127→        slugify(canonical),
   128→    }
   129→
   130→
   131→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   132→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   133→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   134→    if not lang:
   135→        return None
   136→
   137→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   138→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   139→        return None
   140→
   141→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   142→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   143→        return None
   144→
   145→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   146→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   147→        return None
   148→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   149→
   150→
   151→def primary_command_for_finding(
   152→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   153→) -> str:
   154→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   155→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   156→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   157→        available_fixers = [
   158→            fixer
   159→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   160→            if supported_fixers is not None and fixer in supported_fixers
   161→        ]
   162→        if available_fixers:
   163→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   164→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   165→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   166→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   167→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   168→    return f'desloppify plan done "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --confirm'
   169→
   170→
   171→def build_synthesis_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> dict | None:
   172→    """Build a synthetic T1 work item for ``synthesis::pending`` if it's in the queue.
   173→
   174→    Returns ``None`` when synthesis is not pending.
   175→    """
   176→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SYNTHESIS_ID
   177→
   178→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   179→        return None
   180→
   181→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   182→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   183→
   184→    open_review_ids = {
   185→        fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   186→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   187→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   188→    }
   189→    synthesized_ids = set(meta.get("synthesized_ids", []))
   190→    new_since = len(open_review_ids - synthesized_ids)
   191→    resolved_since = len(synthesized_ids - open_review_ids)
   192→
   193→    return {
   194→        "id": SYNTHESIS_ID,
   195→        "tier": 1,
   196→        "confidence": "high",
   197→        "detector": "synthesis",
   198→        "file": ".",
   199→        "kind": "synthesis_needed",
   200→        "primary_command": "desloppify plan synthesize",
   201→        "summary": (
   202→            f"Synthesize {len(open_review_ids)} review findings into a coherent plan"
   203→        ),
   204→        "detail": {
   205→            "total_review_findings": len(open_review_ids),
   206→            "new_since_last": new_since,
   207→            "resolved_since_last": resolved_since,
   208→        },
   209→    }
   210→
   211→
   212→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   213→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   214→    if not dim_scores:
   215→        return {}
   216→
   217→    entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   218→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   219→    for entry in entries:
   220→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   221→        if not name:
   222→            continue
   223→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   224→        [REDACTED](name)
   225→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
   226→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   227→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   228→            if not key:
   229→                continue
   230→            aliases.add(key)
   231→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   232→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   233→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   234→        for alias in aliases:
   235→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   236→    return scores
   237→
   238→
   239→def build_subjective_items(
   240→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   241→) -> list[dict]:
   242→    """Create synthetic subjective work items (always tier 4)."""
   243→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   244→    if not dim_scores:
   245→        return []
   246→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   247→
   248→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   249→    if not subjective_entries:
   250→        return []
   251→    unassessed_dims = {
   252→        str(name).strip()
   253→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   254→            dim_scores
   255→        )
   256→    }
   257→
   258→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   259→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   260→    for finding in findings.values():
   261→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   262→            continue
   263→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   264→        if not dim_key:
   265→            continue
   266→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   267→
   268→    items: list[dict] = []
   269→    def _prepare_command(
   270→        cli_keys: list[str],
   271→        *,
   272→        force_review_rerun: bool = False,
   273→    ) -> str:
   274→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   275→        if cli_keys:
   276→            command += " --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   277→        if force_review_rerun:
   278→            command += " --force-review-rerun"
   279→        return command
   280→
   281→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   282→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   283→        if not name:
   284→            continue
   285→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   286→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   287→            continue
   288→
   289→        [REDACTED](name)
   290→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   291→        cli_keys = [
   292→            str(key).strip().lower()
   293→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   294→            if str(key).strip()
   295→        ]
   296→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   297→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   298→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   299→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   300→            name in unassessed_dims
   301→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   302→        )
   303→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   304→        # Stale items with no open review findings can only be actioned via
   305→        # --force-review-rerun which is gated behind an empty objective
   306→        # backlog.  Flag them so the interleave guardrail can demote them.
   307→        is_stale_rerun = is_stale and open_review == 0 and not is_unassessed
   308→        # If review findings already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   309→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   310→        if open_review > 0:
   311→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   312→        elif is_unassessed:
   313→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   314→        elif is_stale:
   315→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   316→                cli_keys,
   317→                force_review_rerun=True,
   318→            )
   319→        elif cli_keys:
   320→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   321→                cli_keys,
   322→                force_review_rerun=True,
   323→            )
   324→        else:
   325→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   326→                cli_keys,
   327→                force_review_rerun=True,
   328→            )
   329→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   330→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   331→        items.append(
   332→            {
   333→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   334→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   335→                "file": ".",
   336→                "tier": 4,
   337→                "effective_tier": 4,
   338→                "confidence": "medium",
   339→                "summary": summary,
   340→                "detail": {
   341→                    "dimension_name": name,
   342→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   343→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   344→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   345→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   346→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   347→                },
   348→                "status": "open",
   349→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   350→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   351→                "stale_rerun": is_stale_rerun,
   352→            }
   353→        )
   354→    return items
   355→
   356→
   357→__all__ = [
   358→    "ALL_STATUSES",
   359→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   360→    "build_subjective_items",

     2→

    11→
    12→_COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION: dict[str, str] = {
    13→    "abstraction leverage": "abstraction_fitness",
    14→    "indirection cost": "abstraction_fitness",

    19→def dimension_cli_key(dimension_name: str) -> str:
    20→    """Map a display name to a review --dimensions CLI key."""
    21→    cleaned = dimension_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    22→    lowered = cleaned.lower()
    23→    if lowered in _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION:
    24→        return _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION[lowered]
    25→    for key, display in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    26→        if display.lower() == lowered:
    27→            return key
    28→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", lowered).strip("_")
    29→
    30→
    31→def scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name: str, data: dict) -> list[str]:
    32→    """Map scorecard subjective display names to review --dimensions keys."""
    33→    if name in ("Elegance", "Elegance (combined)"):
    34→        components = (
    35→            data.get("detectors", {})
    36→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    37→            .get("components", [])
    38→        )
    39→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    40→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    41→        else:
    42→            keys = [
    43→                "high_level_elegance",
    44→                "mid_level_elegance",
    45→                "low_level_elegance",
    46→            ]
    47→    elif name in ("Abstraction fit", "Abstraction fit (combined)"):
    48→        components = (
    49→            data.get("detectors", {})
    50→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    51→            .get("components", [])
    52→        )
    53→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    54→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    55→        else:
    56→            keys = ["abstraction_fitness"]
    57→    else:
    58→        keys = [dimension_cli_key(name)]
    59→
    60→    seen: set[str] = set()
    61→    ordered: list[str] = []
    62→    for key in keys:
    63→        if not key or key in seen:
    64→            continue
    65→        ordered.append(key)
    66→        seen.add(key)
    67→    return ordered
    68→
    69→
    70→def scorecard_subjective_entries(
    71→    state: dict,
    72→    *,
    73→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    74→) -> list[dict]:
    75→    """Return scorecard-subjective entries with score/strict/placeholder metadata."""
    76→    rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    77→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}
    78→    subjective_display_names = {display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()}
    79→    subjective_display_names.update({"elegance", "elegance (combined)"})
    80→
    81→    entries: list[dict] = []
    82→    for name, data in rows:
    83→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    84→        lowered_name = str(name).strip().lower()
    85→        is_subjective = (
    86→            "subjective_assessment" in detectors
    87→            or lowered_name in subjective_display_names
    88→        )
    89→        if not is_subjective:
    90→            continue
    91→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
    92→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
    93→        assessment_meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment", {})
    94→        placeholder = bool(
    95→            assessment_meta.get("placeholder")
    96→            or (
    97→                data.get("score", 0) == 0
    98→                and data.get("issues", 0) == 0
    99→                and data.get("checks", 0) == 0
   100→            )
   101→        )
   102→        [REDACTED]("dimension_key", "")
   103→        stale = bool(
   104→            dim_key
   105→            and isinstance(assessments.get(dim_key), dict)
   106→            and assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")
   107→        )
   108→        entries.append(
   109→            {
   110→                "name": name,
   111→                "score": score,
   112→                "strict": strict,
   113→                "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   114→                "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   115→                "tier": int(data.get("tier", 4) or 4),
   116→                "placeholder": placeholder,
   117→                "stale": stale,
   118→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
   119→                "cli_keys": scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name, data),
   120→            }
   121→        )
   122→    return entries
   123→
   124→
   125→def scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   126→    state: dict,
   127→    *,
   128→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
   129→) -> list[dict]:
   130→    """Serialize scorecard rows for JSON/query outputs."""
   131→    subjective_by_name = {
   132→        entry["name"]: entry
   133→        for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   134→    }
   135→    payload: list[dict] = []
   136→    for name, data in scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
   137→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
   138→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
   139→        subjective = name in subjective_by_name
   140→        entry = {
   141→            "name": name,
   142→            "score": score,
   143→            "strict": strict,
   144→            "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   145→            "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   146→            "tier": int(
   147→                data.get("tier", 4 if subjective else 3) or (4 if subjective else 3)
   148→            ),
   149→            "subjective": subjective,
   150→        }
   151→        if subjective:
   152→            sub = subjective_by_name[name]
   153→            entry["placeholder"] = bool(sub.get("placeholder"))
   154→            entry["cli_keys"] = list(sub.get("cli_keys", []))
   155→        payload.append(entry)
   156→    return payload
   157→

1→"""Lightweight integrity helpers for subjective/review scoring.
     2→
     3→This module intentionally lives outside ``desloppify.intelligence.review`` so command/state
     4→paths can import it without loading the heavier review package.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→from collections.abc import Iterable, Mapping
    10→
    11→from desloppify.scoring import (
    12→    SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_MATCH_TOLERANCE,
    13→    matches_target_score,
    14→)
    15→
    16→__all__ = [
    17→    "SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_MATCH_TOLERANCE",
    18→    "is_holistic_subjective_finding",
    19→    "is_subjective_review_open",
    20→    "matches_target_score",
    21→    "subjective_review_open_breakdown",
    22→    "unassessed_subjective_dimensions",
    23→]
    24→
    25→
    26→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    27→# Internal iteration helper
    28→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    29→
    30→
    31→def _iter_findings(
    32→    findings: Mapping[str, dict] | Iterable[dict],
    33→) -> Iterable[tuple[str, dict]]:
    34→    """Yield (finding_id, finding) pairs from mapping or iterable inputs."""
    35→    if isinstance(findings, Mapping):
    36→        for finding_id, finding in findings.items():
    37→            if isinstance(finding, dict):
    38→                yield str(finding_id), finding
    39→        return
    40→
    41→    for index, finding in enumerate(findings):
    42→        if isinstance(finding, dict):
    43→            yield str(index), finding
    44→
    45→
    46→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    47→# Public helpers (formerly in integrity/review.py)
    48→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    49→
    50→
    51→def is_subjective_review_open(finding: dict) -> bool:
    52→    """Return True when a finding is an open subjective-review signal."""
    53→    return (
    54→        finding.get("status") == "open"
    55→        and finding.get("detector") == "subjective_review"
    56→    )
    57→
    58→
    59→def is_holistic_subjective_finding(finding: dict, *, finding_id: str = "") -> bool:
    60→    """Best-effort check for holistic subjective-review coverage findings."""
    61→    candidate_id = str(finding.get("id") or finding_id or "")
    62→    if "::holistic_unreviewed" in candidate_id or "::holistic_stale" in candidate_id:
    63→        return True
    64→
    65→    summary = str(finding.get("summary", "") or "").lower()
    66→    if "holistic" in summary and "review" in summary:
    67→        return True
    68→
    69→    detail = finding.get("detail", {})
    70→    return bool(detail.get("holistic"))
    71→
    72→
    73→def subjective_review_open_breakdown(
    74→    findings: Mapping[str, dict] | Iterable[dict],
    75→) -> tuple[int, dict[str, int], dict[str, int]]:
    76→    """Return open subjective count plus reason and holistic-reason breakdowns."""
    77→    reason_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    78→    holistic_reason_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    79→    total = 0
    80→
    81→    for finding_id, finding in _iter_findings(findings):
    82→        if not is_subjective_review_open(finding):
    83→            continue
    84→
    85→        total += 1
    86→        reason = str(finding.get("detail", {}).get("reason", "other") or "other")
    87→        reason_counts[reason] = reason_counts.get(reason, 0) + 1
    88→
    89→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(finding, finding_id=finding_id):
    90→            holistic_reason_counts[reason] = holistic_reason_counts.get(reason, 0) + 1
    91→
    92→    return total, reason_counts, holistic_reason_counts
    93→
    94→
    95→def unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores: dict | None) -> list[str]:
    96→    """Return subjective dimension display names that are still 0% placeholders."""
    97→    if not dim_scores:
    98→        return []
    99→
   100→    unassessed: list[str] = []
   101→    for name, info in dim_scores.items():
   102→        detectors = info.get("detectors", {})
   103→        if "subjective_assessment" not in detectors:
   104→            continue
   105→        assessment_meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment", {})
   106→        if isinstance(assessment_meta, dict) and assessment_meta.get("placeholder"):
   107→            unassessed.append(name)
   108→            continue
   109→        strict_val = float(info.get("strict", info.get("score", 100.0)))
   110→        issues = int(info.get("issues", 0))
   111→        if strict_val <= 0.0 and issues == 0:
   112→            unassessed.append(name)
   113→
   114→    unassessed.sort()
   115→    return unassessed
   116→

    24→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    25→

    27→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    28→class QueueBuildOptions:
    29→    """Configuration for queue construction and tier selection behavior."""
    30→
    31→    tier: int | None = None
    32→    count: int | None = 1
    33→    scan_path: str | None = None
    34→    scope: str | None = None
    35→    status: str = "open"
    36→    include_subjective: bool = True
    37→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    38→    chronic: bool = False
    39→    no_tier_fallback: bool = False
    40→    explain: bool = False
    41→    plan: dict | None = None
    42→    include_skipped: bool = False
    43→    cluster: str | None = None
    44→    collapse_clusters: bool = True
    45→
    46→
    47→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    48→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    49→
    50→    items: list[dict]
    51→    total: int
    52→    tier_counts: dict[int, int]
    53→    requested_tier: int | None
    54→    selected_tier: int | None
    55→    fallback_reason: str | None
    56→    available_tiers: list[int]
    57→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    58→
    59→
    60→def _is_subjective_queue_item(item: dict) -> bool:
    61→    """Return True for queue items that represent subjective work."""
    62→    return item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
    63→
    64→
    65→def _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(
    66→    items: list[dict],
    67→    *,
    68→    objective_burst: int = 3,
    69→    subjective_burst: int = 1,
    70→) -> list[dict]:
    71→    """Interleave subjective work with objective work to avoid starvation.
    72→
    73→    Stale-rerun subjective items (whose only action is ``--force-review-rerun``,
    74→    which is blocked while an objective backlog exists) are demoted behind all
    75→    objective work so they only surface once the backlog drains.
    76→    """
    77→    if objective_burst < 1 or subjective_burst < 1:
    78→        return items
    79→
    80→    # Split subjective items into active (interleave) vs stale-rerun (demote).
    81→    active_subjective: deque[dict] = deque()
    82→    stale_rerun: list[dict] = []
    83→    for item in items:
    84→        if not _is_subjective_queue_item(item):
    85→            continue
    86→        if item.get("stale_rerun"):
    87→            stale_rerun.append(item)
    88→        else:
    89→            active_subjective.append(item)
    90→
    91→    objective_items = deque(
    92→        item for item in items if not _is_subjective_queue_item(item)
    93→    )
    94→
    95→    has_objective = bool(objective_items)
    96→    if not active_subjective and not stale_rerun:
    97→        return items
    98→    if not objective_items and not stale_rerun:
    99→        return items
   100→

100→
   101→    # Interleave only active subjective items with objective work.
   102→    interleaved: list[dict] = []
   103→    if active_subjective and objective_items:
   104→        while objective_items and active_subjective:
   105→            for _ in range(objective_burst):
   106→                if not objective_items:
   107→                    break
   108→                interleaved.append(objective_items.popleft())
   109→            for _ in range(subjective_burst):
   110→                if not active_subjective:
   111→                    break
   112→                interleaved.append(active_subjective.popleft())
   113→    interleaved.extend(objective_items)
   114→    interleaved.extend(active_subjective)
   115→
   116→    # Stale-rerun items go after all objective work.
   117→    interleaved.extend(stale_rerun)
   118→
   119→    return interleaved
   120→
   121→
   122→def _new_item_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   123→    """Return finding IDs added in the most recent scan."""
   124→    scan_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
   125→    if not scan_history:
   126→        return set()
   127→    threshold = scan_history[-1].get("timestamp", "")
   128→    if not threshold:
   129→        return set()
   130→    return {
   131→        fid
   132→        for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   133→        if f.get("first_seen", "") >= threshold
   134→    }
   135→
   136→
   137→def _apply_plan_order(
   138→    items: list[dict],
   139→    plan: dict,
   140→    *,
   141→    include_skipped: bool = False,
   142→    cluster: str | None = None,
   143→    new_ids: set[str] | None = None,
   144→) -> list[dict]:
   145→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
   146→
   147→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
   148→    2. Existing remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
   149→    3. New items (from latest scan) sort after existing remaining items.
   150→    4. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
   151→    5. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
   152→    """
   153→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   154→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   155→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
   156→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
   157→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   158→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
   159→
   160→    # Build lookup
   161→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
   162→    for item in items:
   163→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
   164→
   165→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
   166→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
   167→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
   168→        if override.get("description"):
   169→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
   170→        if override.get("note"):
   171→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
   172→        if override.get("cluster"):
   173→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
   174→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   175→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
   176→                "name": cluster_name,
   177→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
   178→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
   179→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
   180→            }
   181→
   182→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   183→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   184→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   185→    for fid in queue_order:
   186→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   187→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   188→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   189→
   190→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   191→    remaining_existing: list[dict] = []
   192→    remaining_new: list[dict] = []
   193→    _new = new_ids or set()
   194→    for item in items:
   195→        item_id = item["id"]
   196→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   197→            continue
   198→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   199→            skipped_items.append(item)
   200→        elif item_id in _new:
   201→            remaining_new.append(item)
   202→        else:
   203→            remaining_existing.append(item)
   204→
   205→    # Assign queue positions — new items after existing ones
   206→    result = ordered + remaining_existing + remaining_new
   207→    if include_skipped:
   208→        result = result + skipped_items
   209→
   210→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   211→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   212→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   213→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   214→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   215→            if skip_entry:
   216→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   217→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   218→                if skip_reason:
   219→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   220→
   221→    # Filter to cluster if requested
   222→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   223→    if effective_cluster:
   224→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   225→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   226→        if cluster_member_ids:
   227→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   228→
   229→    return result
   230→
   231→
   232→def _item_matches_tier(item: dict, tier: int) -> bool:
   233→    """Check if an item (or any of its members for clusters) matches a tier."""
   234→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   235→        return any(
   236→            int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   237→            for m in item.get("members", [])
   238→        )
   239→    return int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   240→
   241→
   242→def _action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   243→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   244→    try:
   245→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   246→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   247→        if meta:
   248→            return meta.action_type
   249→    except ImportError as exc:
   250→        _ = exc
   251→    return "manual_fix"
   252→
   253→
   254→_ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   255→
   256→
   257→def _collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   258→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items."""
   259→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   260→    if not clusters:
   261→        return items
   262→
   263→    # Build mapping: finding_id → auto-cluster name
   264→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   265→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   266→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   267→            continue
   268→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   269→            fid_to_cluster[fid] = name
   270→
   271→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   272→        return items
   273→
   274→    # Collect members for each cluster, preserving order
   275→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   276→    non_cluster_items: list[dict] = []
   277→
   278→    for item in items:
   279→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   280→        if cname:
   281→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   282→        else:
   283→            non_cluster_items.append(item)
   284→
   285→    # Build cluster meta-items
   286→    result: list[dict] = list(non_cluster_items)
   287→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   288→        # Don't collapse singletons — show them as individual findings
   289→        if len(members) < 2:
   290→            result.extend(members)
   291→            continue
   292→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cname, {})
   293→        detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   294→        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   295→        # Derive action_type from the actual cluster action, not just the detector
   296→        if "desloppify fix" in action:
   297→            action_type = "auto_fix"
   298→        elif "desloppify move" in action:
   299→            action_type = "reorganize"
   300→        else:
   301→            action_type = _action_type_for_detector(detector)
   302→            # If detector says auto_fix but cluster has no fix command, it's really refactor
   303→            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
   304→                action_type = "refactor"
   305→
   306→        # Use stored description but patch the count if it differs from visible members
   307→        stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   308→        total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   309→        if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   310→            # Replace the stored count with the visible count
   311→            summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   312→        else:
   313→            summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   314→
   315→        primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   316→        if not primary_command:
   317→            primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cname} --count 10"
   318→
   319→        meta_item: dict = {
   320→            "id": cname,
   321→            "kind": "cluster",
   322→            "action_type": action_type,
   323→            "summary": summary,
   324→            "members": members,
   325→            "member_count": len(members),
   326→            "primary_command": primary_command,
   327→            "cluster_name": cname,
   328→            "cluster_auto": True,
   329→            "confidence": "high",
   330→            "detector": detector,
   331→            "file": "",
   332→        }
   333→        result.append(meta_item)
   334→
   335→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   336→    return result
   337→
   338→
   339→def build_work_queue(
   340→    state: StateModel,
   341→    *,
   342→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   343→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   344→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   345→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   346→
   347→    status = resolved_options.status
   348→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   349→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   350→    try:
   351→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   352→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   353→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   354→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   355→
   356→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   357→        state,
   358→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   359→        status_filter=status,
   360→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   361→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   362→    )
   363→
   364→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   365→    if (
   366→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   367→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   368→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   369→    ):
   370→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   371→            state,
   372→            state.get("findings", {}),
   373→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   374→        )
   375→        for item in subjective_items:
   376→            if scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   377→                all_items.append(item)
   378→
   379→    # Inject synthesis::pending synthetic item when plan requires it
   380→    if resolved_options.plan and status in {"open", "all"}:
   381→        synth_item = build_synthesis_item(resolved_options.plan, state)
   382→        if synth_item is not None:
   383→            all_items.append(synth_item)
   384→
   385→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   386→
   387→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   388→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
   389→    if resolved_options.plan:
   390→        new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   391→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   392→            all_items,
   393→            resolved_options.plan,
   394→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   395→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   396→            new_ids=new_ids,
   397→        )
   398→
   399→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   400→    should_collapse = (
   401→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   402→        and resolved_options.plan
   403→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   404→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   405→    )
   406→    if should_collapse:
   407→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   408→
   409→    should_interleave = (
   410→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   411→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   412→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   413→        and resolved_options.tier is None
   414→        and not resolved_options.scope
   415→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   416→    )
   417→    if should_interleave:
   418→        all_items = _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(all_items)
   419→
   420→    counts = tier_counts(all_items)
   421→
   422→    requested_tier = (
   423→        int(resolved_options.tier) if resolved_options.tier is not None else None
   424→    )
   425→    selected_tier = requested_tier
   426→    fallback_reason = None
   427→    filtered = all_items
   428→
   429→    if requested_tier is not None:
   430→        filtered = [
   431→            item
   432→            for item in all_items
   433→            if _item_matches_tier(item, requested_tier)
   434→        ]
   435→        if not filtered and not resolved_options.no_tier_fallback:
   436→            chosen = choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier, counts)
   437→            if chosen is not None:
   438→                selected_tier = chosen
   439→                filtered = [
   440→                    item
   441→                    for item in all_items
   442→                    if _item_matches_tier(item, chosen)
   443→                ]
   444→                fallback_reason = (
   445→                    f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open -> showing T{chosen} "
   446→                    "(nearest non-empty)."
   447→                )
   448→        elif not filtered:
   449→            fallback_reason = f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open."
   450→
   451→    total = len(filtered)
   452→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   453→        filtered = filtered[: resolved_options.count]
   454→
   455→    if resolved_options.explain:
   456→        for item in filtered:
   457→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   458→
   459→    available_tiers = [tier for tier, value in counts.items() if value > 0]
   460→    return {
   461→        "items": filtered,
   462→        "total": total,
   463→        "tier_counts": counts,
   464→        "requested_tier": requested_tier,
   465→        "selected_tier": selected_tier,
   466→        "fallback_reason": fallback_reason,
   467→        "available_tiers": available_tiers,
   468→        "grouped": group_queue_items(filtered, "item"),
   469→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   470→    }
   471→
   472→
   473→__all__ = [
   474→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   475→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   476→    "WorkQueueResult",
   477→    "build_work_queue",
   478→    "group_queue_items",
   479→]
   480→

1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import hashlib
    14→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    15→
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    17→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    18→
    19→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    20→SYNTHESIS_ID = "synthesis::pending"
    21→
    22→
    23→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    24→# Result dataclasses
    25→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26→
    27→@dataclass
    28→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    29→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    30→
    31→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    32→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    33→
    34→    @property
    35→    def changes(self) -> int:
    36→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    37→
    38→
    39→@dataclass
    40→class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    41→    """What changed during an unscored-dimension sync."""
    42→
    43→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    44→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    45→
    46→    @property
    47→    def changes(self) -> int:
    48→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    49→
    50→
    51→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    52→# ID helpers
    53→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    54→
    55→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    56→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    57→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    58→    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    59→        scorecard_subjective_entries,
    60→    )
    61→
    62→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    63→    if not dim_scores:
    64→        return set()
    65→
    66→    stale: set[str] = set()
    67→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    68→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    69→            continue
    70→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    71→        if dim_key:
    72→            stale.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    73→    return stale
    74→
    75→
    76→def _current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    77→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    78→
    79→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` directly because
    80→    ``scorecard_subjective_entries`` filters out placeholder dimensions
    81→    (they are hidden from the scorecard display).
    82→    """
    83→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    84→
    85→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    86→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    87→        return set()
    88→
    89→    unscored: set[str] = set()
    90→    for dim_key, payload in assessments.items():
    91→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    92→            continue
    93→        if not payload.get("placeholder"):
    94→            continue
    95→        if dim_key:
    96→            unscored.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    97→    return unscored
    98→
    99→
   100→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   101→# Promoted-aware insertion helper
   102→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   103→
   104→def _after_promoted(order: list[str], plan: PlanModel) -> int:
   105→    """Return the insertion index just after the last promoted item in *order*.
   106→
   107→    When no promoted items are present (or none are in *order*), returns 0
   108→    so callers fall back to existing front-of-queue behavior.
   109→    """
   110→    promoted = set(plan.get("promoted_ids", []))
   111→    if not promoted:
   112→        return 0
   113→    last_idx = -1
   114→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   115→        if fid in promoted:
   116→            last_idx = i
   117→    return last_idx + 1 if last_idx >= 0 else 0
   118→
   119→
   120→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   121→# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)
   122→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   123→
   124→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   125→    plan: PlanModel,
   126→    state: StateModel,
   127→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   128→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   129→
   130→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   131→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   132→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   133→    2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to the
   134→       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.
   135→    """
   136→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   137→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   138→    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   139→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   140→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   141→
   142→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   143→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   144→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   145→        fid for fid in order
   146→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   147→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   148→        and fid not in stale_ids
   149→    ]
   150→    for fid in to_remove:
   151→        order.remove(fid)
   152→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   153→
   154→    # --- Inject: prepend unscored IDs after any promoted items -------------
   155→    existing = set(order)
   156→    insert_at = _after_promoted(order, plan)
   157→    for uid in reversed(sorted(unscored_ids)):
   158→        if uid not in existing:
   159→            order.insert(insert_at, uid)
   160→            result.injected.append(uid)
   161→
   162→    return result
   163→
   164→
   165→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   166→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   167→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   168→
   169→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   170→    plan: PlanModel,
   171→    state: StateModel,
   172→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   173→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale subjective dimensions.
   174→
   175→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   176→       longer stale and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned by
   177→       ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   178→    2. If no objective items remain after cleanup, inject all currently-stale
   179→       dimension IDs so the plan surfaces them as actionable work.
   180→    """
   181→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   182→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   183→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   184→    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   185→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   186→
   187→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   188→    # Only prune IDs that are neither stale nor unscored.
   189→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   190→        fid for fid in order
   191→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   192→        and fid not in stale_ids
   193→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   194→    ]
   195→    for fid in to_remove:
   196→        order.remove(fid)
   197→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   198→
   199→    # --- Inject: populate when no objective items remain -----------------
   200→    has_real_items = any(fid for fid in order if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX))
   201→    if not has_real_items and stale_ids:
   202→        existing = set(order)
   203→        for sid in sorted(stale_ids):
   204→            if sid not in existing:
   205→                order.append(sid)
   206→                result.injected.append(sid)
   207→
   208→    return result
   209→
   210→
   211→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   212→# Synthesis snapshot hash + sync
   213→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   214→
   215→def review_finding_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   216→    """Hash open review finding IDs to detect changes.
   217→
   218→    Returns empty string when there are no open review findings.
   219→    """
   220→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   221→    review_ids = sorted(
   222→        fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   223→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   224→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   225→    )
   226→    if not review_ids:
   227→        return ""
   228→    return hashlib.sha256("|".join(review_ids).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
   229→
   230→
   231→@dataclass
   232→class SynthesisSyncResult:
   233→    """What changed during a synthesis sync."""
   234→
   235→    injected: bool = False
   236→    pruned: bool = False
   237→
   238→    @property
   239→    def changes(self) -> int:
   240→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   241→
   242→
   243→def sync_synthesis_needed(
   244→    plan: PlanModel,
   245→    state: StateModel,
   246→) -> SynthesisSyncResult:
   247→    """Inject ``synthesis::pending`` at front of queue when review findings change.
   248→
   249→    Never auto-prunes — only explicit completion (``_apply_completion``) removes it.
   250→    """
   251→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   252→    result = SynthesisSyncResult()
   253→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   254→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   255→    already_present = SYNTHESIS_ID in order
   256→
   257→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   258→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   259→
   260→    # If hash changed and not already present, inject after promoted items
   261→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash and not already_present:
   262→        order.insert(_after_promoted(order, plan), SYNTHESIS_ID)
   263→        result.injected = True
   264→
   265→    # Never auto-prune: synthesis::pending is only removed by explicit completion
   266→
   267→    return result
   268→
   269→
   270→def compute_new_finding_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   271→    """Return the set of open review/concerns finding IDs added since last synthesis.
   272→
   273→    Returns an empty set when no prior synthesis has recorded ``synthesized_ids``.
   274→    """
   275→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   276→    synthesized = set(meta.get("synthesized_ids", []))
   277→    current = {
   278→        fid for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   279→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   280→    }
   281→    return current - synthesized if synthesized else set()
   282→
   283→
   284→def is_synthesis_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   285→    """Side-effect-free check: is synthesis needed?
   286→
   287→    Returns True when ``synthesis::pending`` is in the queue OR the
   288→    review-finding snapshot hash has drifted from the last completed synthesis.
   289→    """
   290→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   291→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   292→    if SYNTHESIS_ID in order:
   293→        return True
   294→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   295→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   296→    if not last_hash:
   297→        return False
   298→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   299→    return bool(current_hash and current_hash != last_hash)
   300→
   301→
   302→__all__ = [
   303→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   304→    "SYNTHESIS_ID",
   305→    "StaleDimensionSyncResult",
   306→    "SynthesisSyncResult",
   307→    "UnscoredDimensionSyncResult",
   308→    "_current_unscored_ids",
   309→    "compute_new_finding_ids",
   310→    "is_synthesis_stale",
   311→    "review_finding_snapshot_hash",
   312→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
   313→    "sync_synthesis_needed",
   314→    "sync_unscored_dimensions",
   315→]
   316→

   105→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
   106→        payload["provisional_override"] = True
   107→        payload["provisional_until_scan"] = expires_scan
   108→        payload.pop("placeholder", None)
   109→        marked += 1
   110→    return marked
   111→
   112→
   113→def _clear_provisional_override_flags(
   114→    state: dict,
   115→    *,
   116→    assessment_keys: set[str],
   117→) -> int:
   118→    """Clear provisional override flags when trusted internal assessments replace them."""
   119→    if not assessment_keys:
   120→        return 0
   121→    store = state.get("subjective_assessments")
   122→    if not isinstance(store, dict):
   123→        return 0
   124→
   125→    cleared = 0
   126→    for key in sorted(assessment_keys):
   127→        payload = store.get(key)
   128→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   129→            continue
   130→        if payload.pop("provisional_override", None) is not None:
   131→            cleared += 1
   132→        payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   133→        if payload.get("source") == "manual_override":
   134→            payload["source"] = "holistic"
   135→    return cleared
   136→
   137→
   138→
   139→def _print_review_import_sync(state: dict, result: ReviewImportSyncResult) -> None:
   140→    """Print summary of plan changes after review import sync."""
   141→    new_ids = result.new_ids
   142→    print(colorize(
   143→        f"\n  Plan updated: {len(new_ids)} new review finding(s) added to queue.",
   144→        "bold",
   145→    ))
   146→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   147→    for fid in sorted(new_ids)[:10]:
   148→        f = findings.get(fid, {})
   149→        print(f"    * [{short_finding_id(fid)}] {f.get('summary', '')}")
   150→    if len(new_ids) > 10:
   151→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(new_ids) - 10} more", "dim"))
   152→    print()
   153→    print(colorize("  New items added to end of queue.", "dim"))
   154→    print()
   155→    print(colorize("  View queue:            desloppify plan queue", "dim"))
   156→    print(colorize("  View newest first:     desloppify plan queue --sort recent", "dim"))
   157→    print()
   158→    print(colorize("  NEXT STEP:  desloppify plan synthesize", "yellow"))
   159→    print(colorize(
   160→        "  (Review new findings and decide whether to re-plan or accept current queue.)",
   161→        "dim",
   162→    ))
   163→
   164→
   165→def _sync_plan_after_review_change(state: dict, diff: dict) -> None:
   166→    """Best-effort: sync living plan after review import introduces new findings."""
   167→    try:
   168→        from desloppify.engine.plan import (
   169→            append_log_entry,
   170→            has_living_plan,
   171→            load_plan,
   172→            save_plan,
   173→            sync_plan_after_review_import,
   174→        )
   175→
   176→        if not has_living_plan():
   177→            return
   178→
   179→        plan = load_plan()
   180→        result = sync_plan_after_review_import(plan, state)
   181→        if result is None:
   182→            return
   183→
   184→        append_log_entry(
   185→            plan,
   186→            "review_import_sync",
   187→            actor="system",
   188→            detail={
   189→                "trigger": "review_import",
   190→                "new_ids": sorted(result.new_ids),
   191→                "added_to_queue": result.added_to_queue,
   192→                "diff_new": diff.get("new", 0),
   193→                "diff_reopened": diff.get("reopened", 0),
   194→            },
   195→        )
   196→        save_plan(plan)
   197→        _print_review_import_sync(state, result)
   198→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   199→        pass  # best-effort — don't break import on plan errors
   200→
   201→
   202→def do_import(
   203→    import_file,
   204→    state,
   205→    lang,
   206→    state_file,
   207→    *,
   208→    config: dict | None = None,
   209→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   210→    trusted_assessment_source: bool = False,
   211→    trusted_assessment_label: str | None = None,
   212→    attested_external: bool = False,
   213→    manual_override: bool = False,
   214→    manual_attest: str | None = None,
   215→    assessment_override: bool = False,
   216→    assessment_note: str | None = None,
   217→) -> None:
   218→    """Import mode: ingest agent-produced findings."""
   219→    override_enabled, override_attest = import_helpers_mod.resolve_override_context(
   220→        manual_override=manual_override,
   221→        manual_attest=manual_attest,
   222→        assessment_override=assessment_override,
   223→        assessment_note=assessment_note,
   224→    )
   225→    try:
   226→        _validate_import_flag_combos(
   227→            attested_external=attested_external,
   228→            allow_partial=allow_partial,
   229→            override_enabled=override_enabled,
   230→            override_attest=override_attest,
   231→        )
   232→    except ImportFlagValidationError as exc:
   233→        print(colorize(f"  Error: {exc}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   234→        sys.exit(1)
   235→
   236→    try:
   237→        findings_data = import_helpers_mod.load_import_findings_data(
   238→            import_file,
   239→            lang_name=lang.name,
   240→            allow_partial=allow_partial,
   241→            trusted_assessment_source=trusted_assessment_source,
   242→            trusted_assessment_label=trusted_assessment_label,
   243→            attested_external=attested_external,
   244→            manual_override=override_enabled,
   245→            manual_attest=override_attest,
   246→            assessment_override=assessment_override,
   247→            assessment_note=assessment_note,
   248→        )
   249→    except import_helpers_mod.ImportPayloadLoadError as exc:
   250→        import_helpers_mod.print_import_load_errors(
   251→            exc.errors,
   252→            import_file=str(import_file),
   253→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   254→        )
   255→        sys.exit(1)
   256→    assessment_policy: AssessmentImportPolicyModel = (
   257→        import_helpers_mod.assessment_policy_model_from_payload(findings_data)
   258→    )
   259→    import_helpers_mod.print_assessment_mode_banner(
   260→        assessment_policy.to_dict(),
   261→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   262→    )
   263→    import_helpers_mod.print_assessment_policy_notice(
   264→        assessment_policy.to_dict(),
   265→        import_file=str(import_file),
   266→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   267→    )
   268→
   269→    prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   270→
   271→    # Transactional import: only persist if all post-import guards pass.
   272→    # Rebase on the latest on-disk state when available so long-running review
   273→    # sessions don't clobber newer imports/scans that completed while batches ran.
   274→    state_path = Path(state_file) if state_file is not None else None
   275→    if state_path is not None and state_path.exists():
   276→        working_state = copy.deepcopy(state_mod.load_state(state_path))
   277→    else:
   278→        working_state = copy.deepcopy(state)
   279→    diff = review_mod.import_holistic_findings(findings_data, working_state, lang.name)
   280→    label = "Holistic review"
   281→    imported_assessment_keys = _imported_assessment_keys(findings_data)
   282→    provisional_count = 0
   283→    if assessment_policy.mode == "manual_override":
   284→        provisional_count = _mark_manual_override_assessments_provisional(

    67→    "nice ",
    68→    "solid ",

    72→def _validate_and_build_findings(
    73→    findings_list: list[ReviewFindingPayload],
    74→    holistic_prompts: dict[str, Any],
    75→    lang_name: str,
    76→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], list[dict[str, Any]], list[dict[str, Any]]]:
    77→    """Validate raw holistic findings and build state-ready finding dicts.
    78→
    79→    Returns (review_findings, skipped, dismissed_concerns).
    80→    """
    81→    review_findings: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    82→    skipped: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    83→    dismissed_concerns: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    84→    allowed_dimensions = {
    85→        dim for dim in holistic_prompts if isinstance(dim, str) and dim.strip()
    86→    }
    87→
    88→    for idx, raw_finding in enumerate(findings_list):
    89→        finding, finding_errors = validate_review_finding_payload(
    90→            raw_finding,
    91→            label=f"findings[{idx}]",
    92→            allowed_dimensions=allowed_dimensions,
    93→            allow_dismissed=True,
    94→        )
    95→        if finding_errors:
    96→            skipped.append(
    97→                {
    98→                    "index": idx,
    99→                    "missing": finding_errors,
   100→                    "identifier": (
   101→                        raw_finding.get("identifier", "<none>")
   102→                        if isinstance(raw_finding, dict)
   103→                        else "<none>"
   104→                    ),
   105→                }
   106→            )
   107→            continue
   108→        assert finding is not None
   109→
   110→        # Handle dismissed concern verdicts (no dimension/summary required).
   111→        if finding.get("concern_verdict") == "dismissed":
   112→            fp = finding.get("concern_fingerprint", "")
   113→            if fp:
   114→                dismissed_concerns.append(
   115→                    {
   116→                        "fingerprint": fp,
   117→                        "concern_type": finding.get("concern_type", ""),
   118→                        "concern_file": finding.get("concern_file", ""),
   119→                        "reasoning": finding.get("reasoning", ""),
   120→                    }
   121→                )
   122→            continue
   123→
   124→        # Safety net: skip positive observations that slipped past the prompt.
   125→        summary_text = str(finding.get("summary", ""))
   126→        if summary_text.lower().startswith(_POSITIVE_PREFIXES):
   127→            skipped.append(
   128→                {
   129→                    "index": idx,
   130→                    "missing": ["positive observation (not a defect)"],
   131→                    "identifier": finding.get("identifier", "<none>"),
   132→                }
   133→            )
   134→            continue
   135→
   136→        dimension = finding["dimension"]
   137→
   138→        # Confirmed concern verdicts become "concerns" detector findings.
   139→        is_confirmed_concern = finding.get("concern_verdict") == "confirmed"
   140→        detector = "concerns" if is_confirmed_concern else "review"
   141→
   142→        content_hash = hashlib.sha256(summary_text.encode()).hexdigest()[:8]
   143→        detail: dict[str, Any] = {
   144→            "holistic": True,
   145→            "dimension": dimension,
   146→            "related_files": finding["related_files"],
   147→            "evidence": finding["evidence"],
   148→            "suggestion": finding.get("suggestion", ""),
   149→            "reasoning": finding.get("reasoning", ""),
   150→        }

   256→def import_holistic_findings(
   257→    findings_data: ReviewImportPayload,
   258→    state: dict[str, Any],
   259→    lang_name: str,
   260→    *,
   261→    project_root: Path | str | None = None,
   262→    utc_now_fn=utc_now,
   263→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   264→    """Import holistic (codebase-wide) findings into state."""
   265→    payload: ReviewImportEnvelope = parse_review_import_payload(
   266→        findings_data,
   267→        mode_name="Holistic",
   268→    )
   269→    findings_list = payload.findings
   270→    assessments = payload.assessments
   271→    reviewed_files = payload.reviewed_files
   272→    review_scope = findings_data.get("review_scope", {})
   273→    if not isinstance(review_scope, dict):
   274→        review_scope = {}
   275→    scope_full_sweep = review_scope.get("full_sweep_included")
   276→    if not isinstance(scope_full_sweep, bool):
   277→        scope_full_sweep = None
   278→    if assessments:
   279→        store_assessments(
   280→            state,
   281→            assessments,
   282→            source="holistic",
   283→            utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn,
   284→        )
   285→
   286→    _, holistic_prompts, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name)
   287→    valid_dimensions = {
   288→        normalize_dimension_name(dim)
   289→        for dim in holistic_prompts
   290→        if isinstance(dim, str)
   291→    }
   292→    review_findings, skipped, dismissed_concerns = _validate_and_build_findings(
   293→        findings_list, holistic_prompts, lang_name
   294→    )
   295→    imported_dimensions = _collect_imported_dimensions(
   296→        findings_list=findings_list,
   297→        review_findings=review_findings,
   298→        assessments=assessments if isinstance(assessments, dict) else None,
   299→        review_scope=review_scope,
   300→        valid_dimensions=valid_dimensions,
   301→    )
   302→
   303→    # Store dismissed concern verdicts for suppression in future concern generation.
   304→    if dismissed_concerns:
   305→        from desloppify.engine.concerns import generate_concerns
   306→
   307→        store = state.setdefault("concern_dismissals", {})
   308→        now = utc_now_fn()
   309→        # Compute current concerns to get source_finding_ids for each fingerprint.
   310→        current_concerns = generate_concerns(state, lang_name=lang_name)
   311→        concern_sources = {
   312→            c.fingerprint: list(c.source_findings) for c in current_concerns
   313→        }
   314→        for dc in dismissed_concerns:
   315→            fp = dc["fingerprint"]
   316→            store[fp] = {
   317→                "dismissed_at": now,
   318→                "reasoning": dc.get("reasoning", ""),
   319→                "concern_type": dc.get("concern_type", ""),
   320→                "concern_file": dc.get("concern_file", ""),
   321→                "source_finding_ids": concern_sources.get(fp, []),
   322→            }
   323→
   324→    potentials = _lang_potentials(state, lang_name)
   325→    existing_review = potentials.get("review", 0)
   326→    potentials["review"] = max(existing_review, HOLISTIC_POTENTIAL)
   327→
   328→    concern_count = sum(1 for f in review_findings if f.get("detector") == "concerns")
   329→    if concern_count:
   330→        potentials["concerns"] = max(potentials.get("concerns", 0), concern_count)
   331→
   332→    merge_potentials_dict: dict[str, int] = {"review": potentials.get("review", 0)}
   333→    if potentials.get("concerns", 0) > 0:
   334→        merge_potentials_dict["concerns"] = potentials["concerns"]
   335→
   336→    diff = merge_scan(
   337→        state,
   338→        review_findings,
   339→        options=MergeScanOptions(
   340→            lang=lang_name,
   341→            potentials=merge_potentials_dict,
   342→            merge_potentials=True,
   343→        ),
   344→    )
   345→
   346→    new_ids = {finding["id"] for finding in review_findings}
   347→    _auto_resolve_stale_holistic(
   348→        state,
   349→        new_ids,
   350→        diff,
   351→        utc_now_fn,
   352→        imported_dimensions=imported_dimensions,
   353→        full_sweep_included=scope_full_sweep,
   354→    )
   355→
   356→    if skipped:
   357→        diff["skipped"] = len(skipped)
   358→        diff["skipped_details"] = skipped
   359→
   360→    update_reviewed_file_cache(
   361→        state,
   362→        reviewed_files,
   363→        project_root=project_root,
   364→        utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn,
   365→    )
   366→    resolve_reviewed_file_coverage_findings(
   367→        state,
   368→        diff,
   369→        reviewed_files,
   370→        utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn,
   371→    )
   372→    update_holistic_review_cache(
   373→        state,
   374→        findings_list,
   375→        lang_name=lang_name,
   376→        review_scope=review_scope,
   377→        utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn,
   378→    )
   379→    resolve_holistic_coverage_findings(state, diff, utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn)
   380→
   381→    # Clean up dismissals whose source findings were all resolved — runs after
   382→    # all finding mutations (merge_scan, auto_resolve, coverage resolve) so it
   383→    # sees the final state.
   384→    from desloppify.engine.concerns import cleanup_stale_dismissals
   385→
   386→    cleanup_stale_dismissals(state)
   387→
   388→    return diff
   389→
   390→
   391→def _resolve_total_files(state: dict[str, Any], lang_name: str | None) -> int:
   392→    """Best-effort total file count from codebase_metrics or review cache."""
   393→    review_cache = state.get("review_cache", {})
   394→    fallback = len(review_cache.get("files", {}))
   395→
   396→    codebase_metrics = state.get("codebase_metrics", {})
   397→    if not isinstance(codebase_metrics, dict):
   398→        return fallback
   399→

39→def store_assessments(
    40→    state: dict[str, Any],
    41→    assessments: dict[str, Any],
    42→    source: str,
    43→    *,
    44→    utc_now_fn=utc_now,
    45→) -> None:
    46→    """Store dimension assessments in state.
    47→
    48→    *assessments*: ``{dim_name: score}`` or ``{dim_name: {score, ...}}``.
    49→    *source*: ``"per_file"`` or ``"holistic"``.
    50→
    51→    Holistic assessments overwrite per-file for the same dimension.
    52→    Per-file assessments don't overwrite holistic.
    53→    """
    54→    store = state.setdefault("subjective_assessments", {})
    55→    now = utc_now_fn()
    56→
    57→    for dimension_name, value in assessments.items():
    58→        value_obj = value if isinstance(value, dict) else {}
    59→        score = value if is_numeric(value) else value_obj.get("score", 0)
    60→        score = max(0, min(100, score))
    61→        [REDACTED](str(dimension_name))
    62→        if not dimension_key:
    63→            continue
    64→
    65→        existing = store.get(dimension_key)
    66→        if existing and existing.get("source") == "holistic" and source == "per_file":
    67→            continue
    68→
    69→        cleaned_components: list[str] = []
    70→        components = value_obj.get("components")
    71→        if isinstance(components, list):
    72→            cleaned_components = [
    73→                str(item).strip()
    74→                for item in components
    75→                if isinstance(item, str) and item.strip()
    76→            ]
    77→
    78→        component_scores = value_obj.get("component_scores")
    79→        cleaned_scores: dict[str, float] = {}
    80→        if isinstance(component_scores, dict):
    81→            for key, raw in component_scores.items():
    82→                if not isinstance(key, str) or not key.strip():
    83→                    continue
    84→                if not is_numeric(raw):
    85→                    continue
    86→                cleaned_scores[key.strip()] = round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(raw))), 1)
    87→
    88→        store[dimension_key] = {
    89→            "score": score,
    90→            "source": source,
    91→            "assessed_at": now,
    92→            **({"components": cleaned_components} if cleaned_components else {}),
    93→            **({"component_scores": cleaned_scores} if cleaned_scores else {}),
    94→        }
    95→
    96→
    97→def extract_reviewed_files(data: list[dict] | dict) -> list[str]:
    98→    """Parse optional reviewed-file list from import payload."""
    99→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
   100→        return []
   101→    raw = data.get("reviewed_files")
   102→    if not isinstance(raw, list):
   103→        return []
   104→
   105→    reviewed: list[str] = []
   106→    seen: set[str] = set()
   107→    for item in raw:
   108→        if not isinstance(item, str):
   109→            continue
   110→        path = item.strip()
   111→        if not path or path in seen:
   112→            continue
   113→        seen.add(path)
   114→        reviewed.append(path)
   115→    return reviewed
   116→
   117→
   118→def parse_review_import_payload(
   119→    data: ReviewImportPayload | dict[str, Any],
   120→    *,
   121→    mode_name: str,
   122→) -> ReviewImportEnvelope:
   123→    """Parse shared review import payload shape for per-file/holistic flows."""
   124→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
   125→        raise ValueError(f"{mode_name} review import payload must be a JSON object")
   126→
   127→    if "findings" not in data:
   128→        raise ValueError(f"{mode_name} review import payload must contain 'findings'")
   129→    findings = data["findings"]
   130→    if not isinstance(findings, list):
   131→        raise ValueError(f"{mode_name} review import payload 'findings' must be a list")
   132→    for idx, finding in enumerate(findings):
   133→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   134→            raise ValueError(
   135→                f"{mode_name} review import payload 'findings[{idx}]' must be an object"
   136→            )
   137→
   138→    assessments = data.get("assessments")
