```
186→def _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: "ScanRuntime") -> None:
   187→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
   188→    try:
   189→        plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
   190→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   191→        dirty = False
   192→
   193→        if _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan, runtime.state, reconcile_plan_after_scan):
   194→            dirty = True
   195→
   196→        unscored_changed = _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, runtime.state, sync_unscored_dimensions)
   197→        if unscored_changed:
   198→            dirty = True
   199→            append_log_entry(plan, "sync_unscored", actor="system",
   200→                             detail={"changes": True})
   201→
   202→        stale_changed = _sync_stale_dimensions(plan, runtime.state, sync_stale_dimensions)
   203→        if stale_changed:
   204→            dirty = True
   205→            append_log_entry(plan, "sync_stale", actor="system",
   206→                             detail={"changes": True})
   207→
   208→        auto_changed = _sync_auto_clusters(plan, runtime.state)
   209→        if auto_changed:
   210→            dirty = True
   211→            append_log_entry(plan, "auto_cluster", actor="system",
   212→                             detail={"changes": True})
   213→
   214→        synth_sync = sync_synthesis_needed(plan, runtime.state)
   215→        if synth_sync.changes:
   216→            dirty = True
   217→            if synth_sync.injected:
   218→                print(
   219→                    colorize(
   220→                        "  Plan: synthesis needed — review findings changed since last synthesis.",
   221→                        "cyan",
   222→                    )
   223→                )
   224→                append_log_entry(plan, "sync_synthesis", actor="system",
   225→                                 detail={"injected": True})
   226→
   227→        seeded = _seed_plan_start_scores(plan, runtime.state)
   228→        if seeded:
   229→            dirty = True
   230→            append_log_entry(plan, "seed_start_scores", actor="system",
   231→                             detail={})
   232→        # Only clear scores that existed before this reconcile pass —
   233→        # never clear scores we just seeded in the same scan.
   234→        if not seeded and _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(runtime.state, plan):
   235→            dirty = True
   236→            append_log_entry(plan, "clear_start_scores", actor="system",
   237→                             detail={})
   238→
   239→        if dirty:
   240→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   241→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   242→        logging.debug("Plan operation skipped: %s", exc)
   243→
   244→
   245→def _state_subjective_assessments(
   246→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   247→) -> dict[str, object]:
   248→    """Return normalized subjective assessment store from state."""
   249→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
   250→    if isinstance(assessments, dict):
   251→        return assessments
   252→    raise ScanStateContractError(
   253→        "state.subjective_assessments must be an object; rerun with a valid state file"
   254→    )
   255→
```

```
    23→def _state_with_stale_dimensions(*dim_keys: str, score: float = 50.0) -> dict:
    24→    """Build a minimal state with stale subjective dimensions."""
    25→    dim_scores: dict = {}
    26→    assessments: dict = {}
    27→    for dim_key in dim_keys:
    28→        dim_scores[dim_key] = {
    29→            "score": score,
    30→            "strict": score,
    31→            "checks": 1,
    32→            "issues": 0,
    33→            "detectors": {
    34→                "subjective_assessment": {
    35→                    "dimension_key": dim_key,
    36→                    "placeholder": False,
    37→                }
    38→            },
    39→        }
    40→        assessments[dim_key] = {
    41→            "score": score,
    42→            "needs_review_refresh": True,
    43→            "refresh_reason": "mechanical_findings_changed",
    44→            "stale_since": "2025-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
    45→        }
    46→    return {
    47→        "findings": {},
    48→        "scan_count": 5,
    49→        "dimension_scores": dim_scores,
    50→        "subjective_assessments": assessments,
    51→    }
    52→

    54→def _state_with_unscored_dimensions(*dim_keys: str) -> dict:
    55→    """Build a minimal state with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions."""
    56→    dim_scores: dict = {}
    57→    assessments: dict = {}
    58→    for dim_key in dim_keys:
    59→        dim_scores[dim_key] = {
    60→            "score": 0,
    61→            "strict": 0,
    62→            "checks": 1,
    63→            "issues": 0,
    64→            "detectors": {
    65→                "subjective_assessment": {
    66→                    "dimension_key": dim_key,
    67→                    "placeholder": True,

    69→            },
    70→        }
    71→        assessments[dim_key] = {

    76→    return {
    77→        "findings": {},
    78→        "scan_count": 1,

    82→
    83→
    84→def _state_with_mixed_dimensions(
    85→    unscored: list[str],
    86→    stale: list[str],
    87→) -> dict:

    89→    state = _state_with_unscored_dimensions(*unscored)
    90→    stale_state = _state_with_stale_dimensions(*stale)
    91→    state["dimension_scores"].update(stale_state["dimension_scores"])
    92→    state["subjective_assessments"].update(stale_state["subjective_assessments"])
    93→    return state

   100→def test_unscored_injected_at_front():
   101→    """Unscored IDs are prepended before existing items."""
   102→    plan = _plan_with_queue("some_finding::file.py::abc123")
   103→    state = _state_with_unscored_dimensions("design_coherence", "error_consistency")
   104→
   105→    result = sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state)
   106→    assert len(result.injected) == 2
   107→    # Unscored dims should be at the front, before the real finding
   108→    assert plan["queue_order"][-1] == "some_finding::file.py::abc123"
   109→    assert plan["queue_order"][0].startswith("subjective::")
   110→    assert plan["queue_order"][1].startswith("subjective::")
   111→
   112→

   122→    assert len(result.injected) == 1
   123→    assert plan["queue_order"][0] == "subjective::design_coherence"
   124→    assert len(plan["queue_order"]) == 3  # 1 unscored + 2 existing
   125→
```

```
    68→    objective_burst: int = 3,
    69→    subjective_burst: int = 1,
    70→) -> list[dict]:
    71→    """Interleave subjective work with objective work to avoid starvation.
    72→
    73→    Stale-rerun subjective items (whose only action is ``--force-review-rerun``,
    74→    which is blocked while an objective backlog exists) are demoted behind all
    75→    objective work so they only surface once the backlog drains.
    76→    """
    77→    if objective_burst < 1 or subjective_burst < 1:
    78→        return items
    79→
    80→    # Split subjective items into active (interleave) vs stale-rerun (demote).
    81→    active_subjective: deque[dict] = deque()
    82→    stale_rerun: list[dict] = []
    83→    for item in items:
    84→        if not _is_subjective_queue_item(item):
    85→            continue
    86→        if item.get("stale_rerun"):
    87→            stale_rerun.append(item)
    88→        else:
    89→            active_subjective.append(item)
    90→
    91→    objective_items = deque(
    92→        item for item in items if not _is_subjective_queue_item(item)
    93→    )
    94→
    95→    has_objective = bool(objective_items)
    96→    if not active_subjective and not stale_rerun:
    97→        return items
    98→    if not objective_items and not stale_rerun:
    99→        return items
   100→
   101→    # Interleave only active subjective items with objective work.
   102→    interleaved: list[dict] = []
   103→    if active_subjective and objective_items:
   104→        while objective_items and active_subjective:
   105→            for _ in range(objective_burst):
   106→                if not objective_items:
   107→                    break
   108→                interleaved.append(objective_items.popleft())
   109→            for _ in range(subjective_burst):
   110→                if not active_subjective:
   111→                    break
   112→                interleaved.append(active_subjective.popleft())
   113→    interleaved.extend(objective_items)
   114→    interleaved.extend(active_subjective)
   115→
   116→    # Stale-rerun items go after all objective work.
   117→    interleaved.extend(stale_rerun)
   118→
   119→    return interleaved
   120→
   121→
   122→def _new_item_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   123→    """Return finding IDs added in the most recent scan."""
   124→    scan_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
```

---

## **Subjective Dimension Item Lifecycle Analysis**

Based on my thorough examination of the codebase, here is the complete lifecycle and design of subjective dimension items in the work queue:

### **1. CREATION: When Are Subjective Dimensions First Created/Scored?**

**Initial Creation Point: `desloppify review --prepare`**
- Triggered by user running `desloppify review --prepare [--dimensions <keys>]`
- Creates "unscored" (placeholder) subjective dimension entries in `state.subjective_assessments`
- Entry structure (from `store_assessments()` in `/desloppify/intelligence/review/importing/shared.py:39-94`):
  ```python
  {
    "score": <0-100>,
    "source": "holistic" or "per_file",
    "assessed_at": <timestamp>,
    "components": [...],
    "component_scores": {...},
  }
  ```

**Score Persistence:**
- Scores are written to `state["subjective_assessments"][dimension_key]` during import via `store_assessments()` 
- Called from `import_holistic_findings()` (`/desloppify/intelligence/review/importing/holistic.py:279-284`)
- Holistic (user-reviewed) assessments **overwrite** per-file assessments for the same dimension
- Per-file assessments **do not overwrite** holistic assessments (line 66-67 in shared.py)

**First-Time vs. Re-review Distinction:**
- Preflight gate in `/desloppify/app/commands/review/preflight.py` distinguishes these:
  - **First run** (unscored dimensions): No backlog gate, allows immediate review
  - **Rerun** (previously-scored dimensions): Blocks until objective backlog is empty (line 235)

---

### **2. STALENESS: When Do Dimensions Become Stale?**

**Stale Marker Set: `needs_review_refresh` Flag**

Dimensions are marked stale in **two ways**:

**A. Scan Mechanical Change (in scoring pipeline)**
- When objective findings change that impact a dimension's metrics
- Automatically detected during dimension re-scoring in the plan reconciliation phase

**B. Manual Resolution of Review Findings**
- Triggered in `_mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve()` (`/desloppify/engine/_state/resolution.py:40-81`)
- Called when user resolves a `review` detector finding via `resolve_findings()`
- Sets three fields on the assessment:
  ```python
  payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
  payload["refresh_reason"] = f"review_finding_{status}"  # e.g., "review_finding_wontfix"
  payload["stale_since"] = now
  ```

**Stale Detection in Queue Items** (`/desloppify/engine/planning/scorecard_projection.py:103-107`):
```python
stale = bool(
    dim_key
    and isinstance(assessments.get(dim_key), dict)
    and assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")
)
```

---

### **3. INTENDED FLOW: Scan → Review → Resolve → Rereview**

The canonical lifecycle is:

```
SCAN
  ↓
Build dimension_scores (dimension health metrics)
Build subjective_items from those scores
  ↓
[REVIEW] User runs: desloppify review --prepare --dimensions <key>
  ↓
Import findings + assessments → store_assessments() populates subjective_assessments
  ↓
[RESOLVE] User triages review findings: plan done "<finding>" --note "..."
  ↓
_mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve() marks dimension needs_review_refresh=True
  ↓
[NEXT SCAN] Reconciliation syncs stale markers into plan queue
  ↓
If objective queue empty → stale items injected to back of queue
If objective queue has items → stale items demoted (only surface when backlog drains)
  ↓
[REREVIEW] User can re-run review: desloppify review --prepare --dimensions <key> --force-review-rerun
```

---

### **4. SUBJECTIVE ITEM CREATION: All Cases in `build_subjective_items()`**

File: `/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/helpers.py:239-354`

Items are created when `strict_score < threshold` (default 100.0). For each entry, the code determines the state:

| **Case** | **Condition** | **Description** | **Primary Command** | **Notes** |
|----------|-------------|-----------------|-------------------|---------|
| **Unassessed** | `placeholder=True` OR (`strict ≤ 0%` AND `issues=0`) | Dimension never scored | `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions <key>` | First-time onboarding |
| **Has Open Review** | `open_review > 0` | Review findings exist for this dimension | `desloppify show review --status open` | Triage existing findings first |
| **Stale + No Review** | `is_stale=True` AND `open_review=0` AND NOT unassessed | Previously scored, now stale, no active findings | `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions <key> --force-review-rerun` | Marked with `stale_rerun=True` for demotion |
| **Below Threshold** | `strict_score < threshold` AND NOT above cases | Just below target score | `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions <key> --force-review-rerun` | Normal reruns require empty backlog |

**Key Flag: `is_stale_rerun`** (line 307):
```python
is_stale_rerun = is_stale and open_review == 0 and not is_unassessed
```
This flag enables the **interleave guardrail** to demote these items when objective backlog exists.

---

### **5. REVIEW PREFLIGHT GATING: `review_rerun_preflight()`**

File: `/desloppify/app/commands/review/preflight.py:197-259`

**Gate Logic:**
1. Parse `--dimensions` argument to identify targeted dimensions (if provided)
2. Check if any targeted dimensions have **prior scores** (are actually reruns, not first-time):
   ```python
   blocking_dims = _blocking_scored_dimensions(
       state,
       dimensions=dimensions,
       normalized_dimensions=normalized_dimensions,
   )
   ```
3. **Only if** blocking_dims is non-empty (i.e., at least one targeted dimension was previously scored):
   - Build both queues: objective (with `include_subjective=False`) and subjective (for those dims)
   - Count items in each: `_objective_and_subjective_backlog()`
   - **Fail if either count > 0** (line 240)
4. On success, clear `needs_review_refresh` markers (line 250):
   ```python
   cleared = clear_stale_subjective_entries(state, dimensions=dimensions)
   ```

**Critical:** First-time dimension reviews bypass this gate entirely (no blocking_dims).

---

### **6. PLAN QUEUE SYNCHRONIZATION: Two Independent Syncs**

Files: `/desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py:124-208` and called from `/desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_workflow.py:196-206`

**A. Unscored Dimension Sync** (`sync_unscored_dimensions()`, line 124-162)
- **When:** After every scan
- **Action:** Unconditionally prepend unscored (placeholder) dimensions to front of queue
- **Cleanup:** Prune `subjective::*` IDs that are no longer unscored **and** not stale
- **Key:** Even with objective backlog, unscored items are front-of-queue (onboarding priority)

**B. Stale Dimension Sync** (`sync_stale_dimensions()`, line 169-208)
- **When:** After every scan
- **Action:** Only inject stale dimensions when queue has NO real (non-subjective) items
- **Condition** (line 200-206):
  ```python
  has_real_items = any(fid for fid in order if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX))
  if not has_real_items and stale_ids:
      # inject all stale_ids to queue_order
  ```
- **Cleanup:** Prune `subjective::*` IDs that are no longer stale **and** not unscored

**Queue Position Rules (from test lifecycle test_full_lifecycle):**
1. Unscored always front (unconditional)
2. Stale only injected when objective queue empty
3. Once injected, stale dimensions persist in queue until explicitly cleared by re-review
4. Pruning happens lazily: only when dimension no longer meets the stale condition

---

### **7. DISPLAY LAYER: Interleave Guardrail in `_apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail()`**

File: `/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py:65-119`

**Design:**
- Splits subjective items into **active** (can proceed now) vs. **stale_rerun** (requires empty backlog)
- **Active items:** Interleaved 3:1 with objective work (3 objective findings, 1 subjective item)
- **Stale-rerun items:** Demoted to appear AFTER all objective work and active subjective items
- This prevents stale items from blocking vision when objective backlog exists

**Trigger condition** (line 87):
```python
if item.get("stale_rerun"):
    stale_rerun.append(item)
```

The `stale_rerun` flag is set in `build_subjective_items()` line 351.

---

### **8. DESIGN CORRECTNESS ANALYSIS**

**Question:** Should stale subjective items ONLY appear when objective queue is empty, or can they appear while backlog exists?

**Answer: The current design is CORRECT and INTENTIONAL.**

**Evidence:**

1. **Two-Level Gating System:**
   - **Plan level** (`sync_stale_dimensions`): Only injects stale IDs to `queue_order` when objective queue empty
   - **Display level** (`_apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail`): Demotes stale items if objective items exist at display time

2. **Why Two Gates?**
   - **Plan persistence** ensures stale items are queued for when backlog drains
   - **Display interleaving** handles dynamic situations where new objective items arrive after plan was saved
   - Example: Plan was saved with empty queue (stale items injected), then user imports new review findings

3. **Preflight Gating** is the enforcement mechanism:
   - Users cannot re-run a stale dimension review while objective/subjective backlog exists
   - Exception: `--force-review-rerun` flag bypasses this (for manual overrides)
   - This prevents "stale review blocking objective work"

4. **Test Cases Confirm** (test_stale_dimensions.py:200-227):
   - `test_injects_when_queue_empty()`: Stale dims injected ONLY when queue empty
   - `test_no_injection_when_queue_has_real_items()`: No injection if objective items exist

**The design allows stale items in the queue_order because:**
- They are only INJECTED when queue is empty
- They are DEMOTED in display if objective items exist
- They cannot be RE-REVIEWED (actioned) while backlog exists (preflight gate)
- This is a "patient queuing" design: wait for backlog drain rather than lose plan state

---

### **Summary Table: Subjective Item Lifecycle**

| **Stage** | **Location** | **Key Fields** | **Trigger** |
|-----------|-------------|----------------|-----------|
| **Create** | `store_assessments()` in import flow | `score`, `source`, `assessed_at` | `desloppify review --prepare` → import |
| **Mark Stale** | `_mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve()` | `needs_review_refresh=True`, `stale_since` | User resolves review finding |
| **Build Items** | `build_subjective_items()` | `id`, `summary`, `stale_rerun` flag | Work queue building |
| **Sync Plan** | `sync_stale_dimensions()` | Injects/prunes `subjective::*` IDs | Post-scan reconciliation |
| **Display** | `_apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail()` | Item ordering, item removal | Queue rendering for `next`/`show`/`plan` |
| **Clear Stale** | `clear_stale_subjective_entries()` | Remove `needs_review_refresh` flag | After successful `review --prepare` import |

This is a well-designed, multi-level system that balances user guidance (show stale items), work priority (demote when backlog exists), and queue persistence (plan durability).

```
    38→def subjective_coverage_breakdown(
    39→    findings_scoped: dict,
    40→) -> tuple[int, dict[str, int], dict[str, int]]:
    41→    """Return open subjective-review count plus reason and holistic-reason breakdowns."""
    42→    return subjective_review_open_breakdown(findings_scoped)
    43→
    44→
    45→def _tier_label(tier: int) -> str:
    46→    return f"T{tier}"
    47→
    48→
    49→def _render_tier_navigator(queue: dict) -> None:
    50→    counts = queue.get("tier_counts", {})
    51→    print(colorize("\n  Tier Navigator", "bold"))
    52→    print(
    53→        colorize(
    54→            f"    Open: T1:{counts.get(1, 0)} T2:{counts.get(2, 0)} "
    55→            f"T3:{counts.get(3, 0)} T4:{counts.get(4, 0)}",
    56→            "dim",
    57→        )
    58→    )
    59→    print(
    60→        colorize(
    61→            "    Switch: `desloppify next --tier 1` | `desloppify next --tier 2` | "
    62→            "`desloppify next --tier 3` | `desloppify next --tier 4`",
    63→            "dim",
    64→        )
    65→    )
    66→
    67→
    68→def _render_grouped(items: list[dict], group: str) -> None:
    69→    grouped = group_queue_items(items, group)
    70→    for key, grouped_items in grouped.items():
    71→        print(colorize(f"\n  {key} ({len(grouped_items)})", "cyan"))
    72→        for item in grouped_items:
    73→            tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
    74→            tag = _effort_tag(item)
    75→            tag_str = f" {tag}" if tag else ""
    76→            print(
    77→                f"    {_tier_label(tier)} [{item.get('confidence', 'medium')}]{tag_str} {item.get('summary', '')}"
    78→            )
    79→
    80→
    81→def is_auto_fix_command(command: str | None) -> bool:
    82→    cmd = (command or "").strip()
    83→    return cmd.startswith("desloppify fix ") and "--dry-run" in cmd
    84→
    85→
    86→def _effort_tag(item: dict) -> str:
    87→    """Return a short effort/type tag for a queue item."""
    88→    if item.get("detector") == "review":
    89→        return "[review]"
    90→    if is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
    91→        return "[auto]"
    92→    return ""
    93→
    94→
    95→_ACTION_TYPE_LABELS = {
    96→    "auto_fix": "Auto-fixable batch",
    97→    "reorganize": "Reorganize batch",
    98→    "refactor": "Refactor batch",
    99→    "manual_fix": "Grouped task",
   100→}
   101→
   102→
   103→def _render_cluster_item(item: dict) -> None:
   104→    """Render an auto-cluster task card."""
   105→    member_count = int(item.get("member_count", 0))
   106→    action_type = item.get("action_type", "manual_fix")
   107→    cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   108→    if cluster_name == "auto/initial-review":
   109→        type_label = "Initial subjective review"
   110→    elif cluster_name == "auto/stale-review":
   111→        type_label = "Stale subjective review"
   112→    else:
   113→        type_label = _ACTION_TYPE_LABELS.get(action_type, "Grouped task")
   114→    print(colorize(f"  ({type_label}, {member_count} findings)", "bold"))
   115→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   116→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   117→
   118→    # Show breakdown by file (compact) + representative members
   119→    members = item.get("members", [])
   120→    if members:
   121→        # File distribution
   122→        file_counts = Counter(m.get("file", "?") for m in members)
   123→        if len(file_counts) <= 5:
   124→            print(colorize("\n  Files:", "dim"))
   125→            for f, c in file_counts.most_common():
   126→                print(f"    {f} ({c})")
   127→        else:
   128→            print(colorize(f"\n  Spread across {len(file_counts)} files:", "dim"))
   129→            for f, c in file_counts.most_common(3):
   130→                print(f"    {f} ({c})")
   131→            remaining = len(file_counts) - 3
   132→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {remaining} more files", "dim"))
   133→
   134→        # Sample IDs
   135→        print(colorize("\n  Sample:", "dim"))
   136→        for m in members[:3]:
   137→            print(f"    - {m.get('id', '')}")
   138→        if len(members) > 3:
   139→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(members) - 3} more", "dim"))
   140→
   141→    # Action
   142→    cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   143→    primary_command = item.get("primary_command")
   144→    if primary_command:
   145→        print(colorize(f"\n  Action: {primary_command}", "cyan"))
   146→
   147→    # Resolution hints
   148→    print(colorize(f'\n  Resolve all:   desloppify plan done "{cluster_name}" '
   149→                   f'--note "<what>" --confirm', "dim"))
   150→    print(colorize(f"  Drill in:      desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10",
   151→                   "dim"))
   152→    print(colorize(f"  Skip cluster:  desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}", "dim"))
   153→
   154→
   155→def _render_item(
   156→    item: dict, dim_scores: dict, findings_scoped: dict, explain: bool,
   157→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   158→) -> None:
   159→    # Delegate to cluster renderer for cluster meta-items
   160→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   161→        _render_cluster_item(item)
   162→        return
   163→
   164→    # Synthesis-needed item — dedicated compact renderer
   165→    if item.get("kind") == "synthesis_needed":
   166→        print(colorize("  (Tier 1, blocking)", "bold"))
   167→        print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   168→        print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   169→        detail = item.get("detail", {})
   170→        new_since = detail.get("new_since_last", 0)
   171→        resolved_since = detail.get("resolved_since_last", 0)
   172→        if new_since or resolved_since:
   173→            parts = []
   174→            if new_since:
   175→                parts.append(f"{new_since} new")
   176→            if resolved_since:
   177→                parts.append(f"{resolved_since} resolved")
   178→            print(colorize(f"  Changes since last synthesis: {', '.join(parts)}", "dim"))
   179→        print(colorize(f"\n  Action: {item.get('primary_command', 'desloppify plan synthesize')}", "cyan"))
   180→        print(colorize("  Complete all 4 stages (observe → reflect → organize → commit) before fixing.", "dim"))
   181→        return
   182→
   183→    tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   184→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   185→    print(colorize(f"  (Tier {tier}, {confidence} confidence)", "bold"))
   186→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   187→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   188→
   189→    # Effort/type label
   190→    if item.get("detector") == "review":
   191→        print(colorize("  Type: Design review (requires judgment)", "dim"))
   192→    elif is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
   193→        print(colorize("  Type: Auto-fixable", "dim"))
   194→
   195→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   196→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
   197→        detail = item.get("detail", {})
   198→        subjective_score = float(
   199→            detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
   200→        )
   201→        print(f"  Dimension: {detail.get('dimension_name', 'unknown')}")
   202→        print(f"  Score: {subjective_score:.1f}% (always queued as T4)")
   203→        print(
   204→            colorize(
   205→                f"  Action: {item.get('primary_command', 'desloppify review --prepare')}",
   206→                "cyan",
   207→            )
   208→        )
   209→        print(colorize(
   210→            "  Note: re-review scores what it finds — scores can go down if issues are discovered.",
   211→            "dim",
   212→        ))
   213→        if explain:
   214→            reason = item.get("explain", {}).get(
   215→                "policy",
   216→                "subjective items are fixed at T4 and do not outrank mechanical T1/T2/T3.",
   217→            )
   218→            print(colorize(f"  explain: {reason}", "dim"))
   219→        return
   220→
   221→    # Plan overrides: description, cluster, note
   222→    if item.get("plan_description"):
   223→        print(colorize(f"  → {item['plan_description']}", "cyan"))
   224→    plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   225→    if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict):
   226→        cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "")
   227→        cluster_desc = plan_cluster.get("description") or ""
   228→        total = plan_cluster.get("total_items", 0)
   229→        desc_str = f' — "{cluster_desc}"' if cluster_desc else ""
   230→        print(colorize(f"  Cluster: {cluster_name}{desc_str} ({total} items)", "dim"))
   231→    if item.get("plan_note"):
   232→        print(colorize(f"  Note: {item['plan_note']}", "dim"))
   233→
   234→    print(f"  File: {item.get('file', '')}")
   235→    print(colorize(f"  ID:   {item.get('id', '')}", "dim"))
   236→
   237→    detail = item.get("detail", {})
   238→    if detail.get("lines"):
   239→        print(f"  Lines: {', '.join(str(line_no) for line_no in detail['lines'][:8])}")
   240→    if detail.get("category"):
   241→        print(f"  Category: {detail['category']}")
   242→    if detail.get("importers") is not None:
   243→        print(f"  Active importers: {detail['importers']}")
   244→    if detail.get("suggestion"):
   245→        print(colorize(f"\n  Suggestion: {detail['suggestion']}", "dim"))
   246→
   247→    target_line = detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
   248→    if target_line and item.get("file") not in (".", ""):
   249→        snippet = read_code_snippet(item["file"], target_line)
   250→        if snippet:
   251→            print(colorize("\n  Code:", "dim"))
   252→            print(snippet)
   253→
   254→    if dim_scores:
   255→        detector = item.get("detector", "")
   256→        dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   257→        if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   258→            dimension_score = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   259→            strict_val = dimension_score.get("strict", dimension_score["score"])
   260→            print(
   261→                colorize(
   262→                    f"\n  Dimension: {dimension.name} — {dimension_score['score']:.1f}% "
   263→                    f"(strict: {strict_val:.1f}%) "
   264→                    f"({dimension_score['issues']} of {dimension_score['checks']:,} checks failing)",
   265→                    "dim",
   266→                )
   267→            )
   268→
   269→    # Score impact estimate
   270→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   271→    if potentials and detector and dim_scores:
   272→        try:
   273→            impact = compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issues_to_fix=1)
   274→            if impact > 0:
   275→                print(colorize(f"  Impact: fixing this is worth ~+{impact:.1f} pts on overall score", "cyan"))
   276→            else:
   277→                # Single-item impact rounds to 0 — try bulk impact for all issues in detector
   278→                dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   279→                if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   280→                    issues = dim_scores[dimension.name].get("issues", 0)
   281→                    if issues > 1:
   282→                        bulk = compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issues_to_fix=issues)
   283→                        if bulk > 0:
   284→                            print(colorize(
   285→                                f"  Impact: fixing all {issues} {detector} issues → ~+{bulk:.1f} pts",
   286→                                "cyan",
   287→                            ))
   288→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
   289→            log(f"  score impact estimate skipped: {exc}")
   290→    elif detector == "review" and dim_scores:
   291→        # Review findings: show dimension drag from breakdown
   292→        try:
   293→            dim_key = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   294→            if dim_key:
   295→                breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores)
   296→                for entry in breakdown.get("entries", []):
   297→                    if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   298→                        continue
   299→                    entry_key = str(entry.get("name", "")).lower().replace(" ", "_")
   300→                    if entry_key == dim_key.lower().replace(" ", "_"):
   301→                        drag = float(entry.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0)
   302→                        if drag > 0.01:
   303→                            print(colorize(
   304→                                f"  Dimension drag: {entry['name']} costs -{drag:.2f} pts on overall score",
   305→                                "cyan",
   306→                            ))
   307→                        break
   308→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
   309→            log(f"  dimension drag estimate skipped: {exc}")
   310→
   311→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   312→    auto_fix_command = item.get("primary_command")
   313→    if is_auto_fix_command(auto_fix_command):
   314→        similar_count = sum(
   315→            1
   316→            for finding in findings_scoped.values()
   317→            if finding.get("detector") == detector_name and finding["status"] == "open"
   318→        )
   319→        if similar_count > 1:
   320→            print(
   321→                colorize(
   322→                    f"\n  Auto-fixable: {similar_count} similar findings. "
   323→                    f"Run `{auto_fix_command}` to fix all at once.",
   324→                    "cyan",
   325→                )
   326→            )
   327→    if explain:
   328→        explanation = item.get("explain", {})
   329→        count_weight = explanation.get("count", int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0))
   330→        base = (
   331→            f"ranked by tier={tier}, confidence={confidence}, "
   332→            f"count={count_weight}, id={item.get('id', '')}"
   333→        )
   334→
   335→        # Add dimension context for mechanical detectors
   336→        if dim_scores and detector:
   337→            dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   338→            if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   339→                ds = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   340→                base += (
   341→                    f". Dimension: {dimension.name} at {ds['score']:.1f}% "
   342→                    f"({ds['issues']} open issues)"
   343→                )
   344→
   345→        # For review findings, show subjective dimension context
   346→        if item.get("detector") == "review" and dim_scores:
   347→            dim_key = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   348→            if dim_key:
   349→                for ds_name, ds_data in dim_scores.items():
   350→                    if ds_name.lower().replace(" ", "_") == dim_key.lower().replace(" ", "_"):
   351→                        score_val = ds_data.get("score", "?")
   352→                        score_str = f"{score_val:.1f}" if isinstance(score_val, (int, float)) else str(score_val)
   353→                        base += f". Subjective dimension: {ds_name} at {score_str}%"
   354→                        break
   355→
   356→        policy = explanation.get("policy")
   357→        if policy:
   358→            base = f"{base}. {policy}"
   359→        print(colorize(f"  explain: {base}", "dim"))
   360→
   361→
   362→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   363→    _render_tier_navigator(queue)
   364→    if not queue.get("fallback_reason"):
   365→        return
   366→    print(colorize(f"  {queue['fallback_reason']}", "yellow"))
   367→    if not explain:
   368→        return
   369→    available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   370→    if available:
   371→        tiers = ", ".join(f"T{tier_num}" for tier_num in available)
   372→        print(colorize(f"  explain: available tiers are {tiers}", "dim"))
   373→
   374→
   375→def show_empty_queue(
   376→    queue: dict,
   377→    tier: int | None,
   378→    strict: float | None,
   379→    *,
   380→    plan_start_strict: float | None = None,
   381→    target_strict: float | None = None,
   382→) -> bool:
   383→    if queue.get("items"):
   384→        return False
   385→    # Show before/after comparison when plan_start_strict is available
   386→    if plan_start_strict is not None and strict is not None:
   387→        delta = round(strict - plan_start_strict, 1)
   388→        delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if abs(delta) >= 0.05 else ""
   389→        print(colorize("\n  Queue cleared!", "green"))
   390→        print(colorize(
   391→            f"  Frozen plan-start: strict {plan_start_strict:.1f} → Live estimate: strict {strict:.1f}{delta_str}",
   392→            "cyan",
   393→        ))
   394→        print(colorize(
   395→            "  Run `desloppify scan` now to finalize and reveal your updated score.",
   396→            "dim",
   397→        ))
   398→    else:
   399→        suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   400→        print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   401→    if tier is not None:
   402→        print(colorize(f"  Requested tier: T{tier}", "dim"))
   403→        available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   404→        if available:
   405→            commands = " | ".join(
   406→                f"desloppify next --tier {tier_num}" for tier_num in available
   407→            )
   408→            print(colorize(f"  Try: {commands}", "dim"))
   409→    return True
   410→
   411→
   412→def _render_compact_item(item: dict, idx: int, total: int) -> None:
   413→    """One-line summary for cluster drill-in items after the first."""
   414→    tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   415→    tag = _effort_tag(item)
   416→    tag_str = f" {tag}" if tag else ""
   417→    fid = item.get("id", "")
   418→    short = fid.rsplit("::", 1)[-1][:8] if "::" in fid else fid
   419→    print(f"  [{idx + 1}/{total}] {_tier_label(tier)}{tag_str} {item.get('summary', '')}")
   420→    print(colorize(f"         {item.get('file', '')}  [{short}]", "dim"))
   421→
   422→
   423→def render_terminal_items(
   424→    items: list[dict],
   425→    dim_scores: dict,
   426→    findings_scoped: dict,
   427→    *,
   428→    group: str,
   429→    explain: bool,
   430→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   431→    plan: dict | None = None,
   432→    cluster_filter: str | None = None,
   433→) -> None:
   434→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   435→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   436→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   437→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   438→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   439→        total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   440→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({len(items)} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   441→
   442→    if group != "item":
   443→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   444→        return
   445→
   446→    # Detect cluster drill-in: multiple items with cluster focus active
   447→    is_cluster_drill = len(items) > 1 and (
   448→        cluster_filter or (plan and plan.get("active_cluster"))
   449→    )
   450→
   451→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   452→        if idx > 0:
   453→            print()
   454→        # Full card for first item, compact for rest in cluster drill-in
   455→        if is_cluster_drill and idx > 0:
   456→            _render_compact_item(item, idx, len(items))
   457→            continue
   458→        queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   459→        if queue_pos and len(items) > 1:
   460→            label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   461→        elif len(items) > 1:
   462→            label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]"
   463→        else:
   464→            pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   465→            label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   466→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   467→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials)
   468→
   469→
   470→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   471→    if len(items) != 1:
   472→        return
   473→    if items[0].get("kind") == "cluster":
   474→        return  # Cluster card already includes resolution hints
   475→    if items[0].get("kind") != "finding":
   476→        return
   477→    item = items[0]
   478→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   479→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   480→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   481→        primary = item.get(
   482→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   483→        )
   484→        print(f"    {primary}")
   485→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   486→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   487→        return
   488→
   489→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   490→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   491→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   492→        print(f"    {primary}")
   493→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   494→    else:
   495→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   496→
   497→    print(
   498→        f'    desloppify plan done "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" --confirm'
   499→    )
   500→    print(
   501→        f'    desloppify plan skip --permanent "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   502→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   503→    )
   504→
   505→
   506→def render_followup_nudges(
   507→    state: dict,
   508→    dim_scores: dict,
   509→    findings_scoped: dict,
   510→    *,
   511→    strict_score: float | None,
   512→    target_strict_score: float,
   513→    queue_total: int = 0,
   514→    plan_start_strict: float | None = None,
   515→) -> None:
   516→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   517→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   518→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   519→        state,
   520→        subjective_entries,
   521→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   522→        max_quality_items=3,
   523→        max_integrity_items=5,
   524→    )
   525→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   526→    # Show frozen plan-start score + queue remaining when in an active cycle
   527→    if queue_total > 0 and plan_start_strict is not None:
   528→        print(
   529→            colorize(
   530→                f"\n  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {plan_start_strict:.1f}/100",
   531→                "cyan",
   532→            )
   533→        )
   534→        print(
   535→            colorize(
   536→                f"  Queue: {queue_total} item{'s' if queue_total != 1 else ''}"
   537→                " remaining. Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   538→                "dim",
   539→            )
   540→        )
   541→    elif strict_score is not None:
   542→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   543→        if gap > 0:
   544→            print(
   545→                colorize(
   546→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   547→                    "cyan",
   548→                )
   549→            )
   550→        else:
   551→            print(
   552→                colorize(
   553→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   554→                    "green",
   555→                )
   556→            )
   557→    # Subjective bottleneck banner
   558→    if strict_score is not None and dim_scores:
   559→        try:
   560→            breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores)
   561→            subjective_drag = sum(
   562→                float(e.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0)
   563→                for e in breakdown.get("entries", [])
   564→                if isinstance(e, dict) and e.get("component") == "subjective"
   565→            )
   566→            mechanical_drag = sum(
   567→                float(e.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0)
   568→                for e in breakdown.get("entries", [])
   569→                if isinstance(e, dict) and e.get("component") != "subjective"
   570→            )
   571→            if subjective_drag > mechanical_drag and subjective_drag > 5.0:
   572→                print(colorize(
   573→                    f"\n  Subjective dimensions are the main bottleneck "
   574→                    f"(-{subjective_drag:.0f} pts vs -{mechanical_drag:.0f} pts mechanical).",
   575→                    "yellow",
   576→                ))
   577→                print(colorize(
   578→                    "  Code fixes alone won't close the gap — run "
   579→                    "`desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` "
   580→                    "to re-score.",
   581→                    "yellow",
   582→                ))
   583→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
   584→            log(f"  subjective bottleneck banner skipped: {exc}")
   585→
   586→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   587→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   588→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   589→
   590→    # Rescan nudge after structural work
   591→    if queue_total > 10:
   592→        print(colorize(
   593→            "\n  Tip: after structural fixes (splitting files, moving code), rescan to "
   594→            "let cascade effects settle: `desloppify scan --path .`",
   595→            "dim",
   596→        ))
   597→
   598→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   599→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   600→        findings_scoped
   601→    )
   602→    parts: list[str] = []
   603→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   604→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   605→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   606→    open_review = [
   607→        f for f in findings_scoped.values()
   608→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   609→    ]
   610→    if low_dims:
   611→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   612→    if stale_count:
   613→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   614→    if unassessed_count:
   615→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   616→    if len(open_review):
   617→        parts.append(f"{len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open")
   618→    if coverage_open > 0:
   619→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   620→
   621→    if parts:
   622→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   623→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   624→
```

```
1→"""Unified work-queue selection for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from collections import deque
     7→from typing import TypedDict
     8→
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
    10→    ALL_STATUSES,
    11→    ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    12→    build_subjective_items,
    13→    build_synthesis_item,
    14→    scope_matches,
    15→)
    16→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import (
    17→    build_finding_items,
    18→    choose_fallback_tier,
    19→    group_queue_items,
    20→    item_explain,
    21→    item_sort_key,
    22→    tier_counts,
    23→)
    24→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    25→
    26→
    27→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    28→class QueueBuildOptions:
    29→    """Configuration for queue construction and tier selection behavior."""
    30→
    31→    tier: int | None = None
    32→    count: int | None = 1
    33→    scan_path: str | None = None
    34→    scope: str | None = None
    35→    status: str = "open"
    36→    include_subjective: bool = True
    37→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    38→    chronic: bool = False
    39→    no_tier_fallback: bool = False
    40→    explain: bool = False
    41→    plan: dict | None = None
    42→    include_skipped: bool = False
    43→    cluster: str | None = None
    44→    collapse_clusters: bool = True
    45→
    46→
    47→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    48→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    49→
    50→    items: list[dict]
    51→    total: int
    52→    tier_counts: dict[int, int]
    53→    requested_tier: int | None
    54→    selected_tier: int | None
    55→    fallback_reason: str | None
    56→    available_tiers: list[int]
    57→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    58→
    59→
    60→def _is_subjective_queue_item(item: dict) -> bool:
    61→    """Return True for queue items that represent subjective work."""
    62→    return item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
    63→
    64→
    65→def _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(
    66→    items: list[dict],
    67→    *,
    68→    objective_burst: int = 3,
    69→    subjective_burst: int = 1,
    70→) -> list[dict]:
    71→    """Interleave subjective work with objective work to avoid starvation."""
    72→    if objective_burst < 1 or subjective_burst < 1:
    73→        return items
    74→
    75→    subjective_items = deque(
    76→        item for item in items if _is_subjective_queue_item(item)
    77→    )
    78→    objective_items = deque(
    79→        item for item in items if not _is_subjective_queue_item(item)
    80→    )
    81→    if not subjective_items or not objective_items:
    82→        return items
    83→
    84→    interleaved: list[dict] = []
    85→    while objective_items and subjective_items:
    86→        for _ in range(objective_burst):
    87→            if not objective_items:
    88→                break
    89→            interleaved.append(objective_items.popleft())
    90→        for _ in range(subjective_burst):
    91→            if not subjective_items:
    92→                break
    93→            interleaved.append(subjective_items.popleft())
    94→
    95→    interleaved.extend(objective_items)
    96→    interleaved.extend(subjective_items)
    97→    return interleaved
    98→
    99→
   100→def _new_item_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   101→    """Return finding IDs added in the most recent scan."""
   102→    scan_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
   103→    if not scan_history:
   104→        return set()
   105→    threshold = scan_history[-1].get("timestamp", "")
   106→    if not threshold:
   107→        return set()
   108→    return {
   109→        fid
   110→        for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   111→        if f.get("first_seen", "") >= threshold
   112→    }
   113→
   114→
   115→def _apply_plan_order(
   116→    items: list[dict],
   117→    plan: dict,
   118→    *,
   119→    include_skipped: bool = False,
   120→    cluster: str | None = None,
   121→    new_ids: set[str] | None = None,
   122→) -> list[dict]:
   123→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
   124→
   125→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
   126→    2. Existing remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
   127→    3. New items (from latest scan) sort after existing remaining items.
   128→    4. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
   129→    5. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
   130→    """
   131→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   132→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   133→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
   134→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
   135→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   136→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
   137→
   138→    # Build lookup
   139→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
   140→    for item in items:
   141→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
   142→
   143→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
   144→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
   145→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
   146→        if override.get("description"):
   147→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
   148→        if override.get("note"):
   149→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
   150→        if override.get("cluster"):
   151→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
   152→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   153→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
   154→                "name": cluster_name,
   155→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
   156→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
   157→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
   158→            }
   159→
   160→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   161→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   162→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   163→    for fid in queue_order:
   164→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   165→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   166→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   167→
   168→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   169→    remaining_existing: list[dict] = []
   170→    remaining_new: list[dict] = []
   171→    _new = new_ids or set()
   172→    for item in items:
   173→        item_id = item["id"]
   174→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   175→            continue
   176→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   177→            skipped_items.append(item)
   178→        elif item_id in _new:
   179→            remaining_new.append(item)
   180→        else:
   181→            remaining_existing.append(item)
   182→
   183→    # Assign queue positions — new items after existing ones
   184→    result = ordered + remaining_existing + remaining_new
   185→    if include_skipped:
   186→        result = result + skipped_items
   187→
   188→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   189→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   190→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   191→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   192→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   193→            if skip_entry:
   194→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   195→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   196→                if skip_reason:
   197→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   198→
   199→    # Filter to cluster if requested
   200→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   201→    if effective_cluster:
   202→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   203→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   204→        if cluster_member_ids:
   205→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   206→
   207→    return result
   208→
   209→
   210→def _item_matches_tier(item: dict, tier: int) -> bool:
   211→    """Check if an item (or any of its members for clusters) matches a tier."""
   212→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   213→        return any(
   214→            int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   215→            for m in item.get("members", [])
   216→        )
   217→    return int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   218→
   219→
   220→def _action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   221→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   222→    try:
   223→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   224→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   225→        if meta:
   226→            return meta.action_type
   227→    except ImportError as exc:
   228→        _ = exc
   229→    return "manual_fix"
   230→
   231→
   232→_ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   233→
   234→
   235→def _collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   236→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items."""
   237→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   238→    if not clusters:
   239→        return items
   240→
   241→    # Build mapping: finding_id → auto-cluster name
   242→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   243→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   244→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   245→            continue
   246→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   247→            fid_to_cluster[fid] = name
   248→
   249→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   250→        return items
   251→
   252→    # Collect members for each cluster, preserving order
   253→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   254→    non_cluster_items: list[dict] = []
   255→
   256→    for item in items:
   257→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   258→        if cname:
   259→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   260→        else:
   261→            non_cluster_items.append(item)
   262→
   263→    # Build cluster meta-items
   264→    result: list[dict] = list(non_cluster_items)
   265→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   266→        # Don't collapse singletons — show them as individual findings
   267→        if len(members) < 2:
   268→            result.extend(members)
   269→            continue
   270→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cname, {})
   271→        detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   272→        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   273→        # Derive action_type from the actual cluster action, not just the detector
   274→        if "desloppify fix" in action:
   275→            action_type = "auto_fix"
   276→        elif "desloppify move" in action:
   277→            action_type = "reorganize"
   278→        else:
   279→            action_type = _action_type_for_detector(detector)
   280→            # If detector says auto_fix but cluster has no fix command, it's really refactor
   281→            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
   282→                action_type = "refactor"
   283→
   284→        # Use stored description but patch the count if it differs from visible members
   285→        stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   286→        total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   287→        if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   288→            # Replace the stored count with the visible count
   289→            summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   290→        else:
   291→            summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   292→
   293→        primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   294→        if not primary_command:
   295→            primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cname} --count 10"
   296→
   297→        meta_item: dict = {
   298→            "id": cname,
   299→            "kind": "cluster",
   300→            "action_type": action_type,
   301→            "summary": summary,
   302→            "members": members,
   303→            "member_count": len(members),
   304→            "primary_command": primary_command,
   305→            "cluster_name": cname,
   306→            "cluster_auto": True,
   307→            "confidence": "high",
   308→            "detector": detector,
   309→            "file": "",
   310→        }
   311→        result.append(meta_item)
   312→
   313→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   314→    return result
   315→
   316→
   317→def build_work_queue(
   318→    state: StateModel,
   319→    *,
   320→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   321→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   322→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   323→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   324→
   325→    status = resolved_options.status
   326→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   327→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   328→    try:
   329→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   330→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   331→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   332→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   333→
   334→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   335→        state,
   336→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   337→        status_filter=status,
   338→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   339→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   340→    )
   341→
   342→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   343→    has_objective_backlog = bool(finding_items)
   344→    if (
   345→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   346→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   347→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   348→    ):
   349→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   350→            state,
   351→            state.get("findings", {}),
   352→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   353→        )
   354→        for item in subjective_items:
   355→            if not scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   356→                continue
   357→            # Suppress subjective items whose only action is a review rerun
   358→            # (--force-review-rerun) while objective backlog exists — the
   359→            # review preflight blocks reruns until the backlog drains.
   360→            if has_objective_backlog and item.get("backlog_gated"):
   361→                continue
   362→            all_items.append(item)
   363→
   364→    # Inject synthesis::pending synthetic item when plan requires it
   365→    if resolved_options.plan and status in {"open", "all"}:
   366→        synth_item = build_synthesis_item(resolved_options.plan, state)
   367→        if synth_item is not None:
   368→            all_items.append(synth_item)
   369→
   370→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   371→
   372→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   373→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
   374→    if resolved_options.plan:
   375→        new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   376→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   377→            all_items,
   378→            resolved_options.plan,
   379→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   380→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   381→            new_ids=new_ids,
   382→        )
   383→
   384→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   385→    should_collapse = (
   386→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   387→        and resolved_options.plan
   388→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   389→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   390→    )
   391→    if should_collapse:
   392→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   393→
   394→    should_interleave = (
   395→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   396→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   397→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   398→        and resolved_options.tier is None
   399→        and not resolved_options.scope
   400→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   401→    )
   402→    if should_interleave:
   403→        all_items = _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(all_items)
   404→
   405→    counts = tier_counts(all_items)
   406→
   407→    requested_tier = (
   408→        int(resolved_options.tier) if resolved_options.tier is not None else None
   409→    )
   410→    selected_tier = requested_tier
   411→    fallback_reason = None
   412→    filtered = all_items
   413→
   414→    if requested_tier is not None:
   415→        filtered = [
   416→            item
   417→            for item in all_items
   418→            if _item_matches_tier(item, requested_tier)
   419→        ]
   420→        if not filtered and not resolved_options.no_tier_fallback:
   421→            chosen = choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier, counts)
   422→            if chosen is not None:
   423→                selected_tier = chosen
   424→                filtered = [
   425→                    item
   426→                    for item in all_items
   427→                    if _item_matches_tier(item, chosen)
   428→                ]
   429→                fallback_reason = (
   430→                    f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open -> showing T{chosen} "
   431→                    "(nearest non-empty)."
   432→                )
   433→        elif not filtered:
   434→            fallback_reason = f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open."
   435→
   436→    total = len(filtered)
   437→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   438→        filtered = filtered[: resolved_options.count]
   439→
   440→    if resolved_options.explain:
   441→        for item in filtered:
   442→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   443→
   444→    available_tiers = [tier for tier, value in counts.items() if value > 0]
   445→    return {
   446→        "items": filtered,
   447→        "total": total,
   448→        "tier_counts": counts,
   449→        "requested_tier": requested_tier,
   450→        "selected_tier": selected_tier,
   451→        "fallback_reason": fallback_reason,
   452→        "available_tiers": available_tiers,
   453→        "grouped": group_queue_items(filtered, "item"),
   454→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   455→    }
   456→
   457→
   458→__all__ = [
   459→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   460→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   461→    "WorkQueueResult",
   462→    "build_work_queue",
   463→    "group_queue_items",
   464→]
   465→
```

```
    45→def _low_subjective_dimensions(

    49→    threshold: float = 95.0,

    65→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
```

```
1→"""Output helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_write_error
    10→
    11→
    12→def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    13→    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    14→    # Cluster meta-items get their own serialization
    15→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    16→        members_raw = item.get("members", [])
    17→        serialized_members = [serialize_item(m) for m in members_raw]
    18→        return {
    19→            "id": item.get("id"),
    20→            "kind": "cluster",
    21→            "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
    22→            "summary": item.get("summary"),
    23→            "member_count": item.get("member_count", len(serialized_members)),
    24→            "members": serialized_members,
    25→            "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    26→            "cluster_name": item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id")),
    27→            "cluster_auto": item.get("cluster_auto", True),
    28→            "detector": item.get("detector"),
    29→        }
    30→
    31→    serialized: dict[str, Any] = {
    32→        "id": item.get("id"),
    33→        "kind": item.get("kind", "finding"),
    34→        "tier": item.get("tier"),
    35→        "effective_tier": item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier")),
    36→        "confidence": item.get("confidence"),
    37→        "detector": item.get("detector"),
    38→        "file": item.get("file"),
    39→        "summary": item.get("summary"),
    40→        "detail": item.get("detail", {}),
    41→        "status": item.get("status"),
    42→        "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    43→    }
    44→    explain = item.get("explain")
    45→    if explain is not None:
    46→        serialized["explain"] = explain
    47→
    48→    # Plan metadata
    49→    if item.get("queue_position"):
    50→        serialized["queue_position"] = item["queue_position"]
    51→    if item.get("plan_description"):
    52→        serialized["plan_description"] = item["plan_description"]
    53→    if item.get("plan_note"):
    54→        serialized["plan_note"] = item["plan_note"]
    55→    if item.get("plan_cluster"):
    56→        serialized["plan_cluster"] = item["plan_cluster"]
    57→    if item.get("plan_skipped"):
    58→        serialized["plan_skipped"] = True
    59→        serialized["plan_skip_kind"] = item.get("plan_skip_kind", "temporary")
    60→        if item.get("plan_skip_reason"):
    61→            serialized["plan_skip_reason"] = item["plan_skip_reason"]
    62→
    63→    return serialized
    64→
    65→
    66→def build_query_payload(
    67→    queue: Mapping[str, Any],
    68→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    69→    *,
    70→    command: str,
    71→    narrative: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    72→    plan: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    73→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    74→    """Build JSON payload for query.json and non-terminal output modes."""
    75→    serialized = [serialize_item(item) for item in items]
    76→    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
    77→        "command": command,
    78→        "items": serialized,
    79→        "queue": {
    80→            "tier_counts": queue.get("tier_counts", {}),
    81→            "requested_tier": queue.get("requested_tier"),
    82→            "selected_tier": queue.get("selected_tier"),
    83→            "fallback_reason": queue.get("fallback_reason"),
    84→            "available_tiers": queue.get("available_tiers", []),
    85→            "total": queue.get("total", len(items)),
    86→        },
    87→        "narrative": narrative,
    88→    }
    89→
    90→    if plan and (
    91→        plan.get("queue_order")
    92→        or plan.get("skipped")
    93→        or plan.get("clusters")
    94→    ):
    95→        clusters_summary = []
    96→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    97→            member_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    98→            clusters_summary.append({
    99→                "name": name,
   100→                "description": cluster.get("description"),
   101→                "item_count": len(member_ids),
   102→            })
   103→        payload["plan"] = {
   104→            "active": True,
   105→            "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
   106→            "clusters": clusters_summary,
   107→            "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
   108→            "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
   109→            "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
   110→        }
   111→
   112→    return payload
   113→
   114→
   115→def render_markdown(items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
   116→    """Render queue items as markdown."""
   117→    lines = [
   118→        "# Desloppify Next Queue",
   119→        "",
   120→        "| Kind | Tier | Confidence | Summary | Command |",
   121→        "|------|------|------------|---------|---------|",
   122→    ]
   123→    for item in items:
   124→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   125→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   126→        conf = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   127→        summary = item.get("summary", "").replace("|", "\\|")
   128→        command = (item.get("primary_command", "") or "").replace("|", "\\|")
   129→        lines.append(f"| {kind} | T{tier} | {conf} | {summary} | {command} |")
   130→    lines.append("")
   131→    return "\n".join(lines)
   132→
   133→
   134→def write_output_file(
   135→    output_file: str,
   136→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   137→    item_count: int,
   138→    *,
   139→    safe_write_text_fn,
   140→    colorize_fn,
   141→) -> bool:
   142→    """Persist payload to file and print success/failure hints."""
   143→    try:
   144→        safe_write_text_fn(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   145→        print(colorize_fn(f"Wrote {item_count} items to {output_file}", "green"))
   146→    except OSError as exc:
   147→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
   148→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="next output")
   149→        return False
   150→    return True
   151→
   152→
   153→def emit_non_terminal_output(
   154→    output_format: str,
   155→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   156→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
   157→) -> bool:
   158→    """Render JSON/markdown output variants."""
   159→    renderers = {
   160→        "json": lambda: print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2)),
   161→        "md": lambda: print(render_markdown(items)),
   162→    }
   163→    renderer = renderers.get(output_format)
   164→    if renderer is None:
   165→        return False
   166→    renderer()
   167→    return True
   168→
   169→
   170→__all__ = [
   171→    "build_query_payload",
   172→    "emit_non_terminal_output",
   173→    "render_markdown",
   174→    "serialize_item",
   175→    "write_output_file",
   176→]
   177→
```

```
    34→
    35→def show_tier_progress_table(by_tier: dict) -> None:
    36→    """Fallback display when dimension scores are unavailable."""
    37→    rows = []
    38→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
    39→        ts = by_tier.get(str(tier_num), {})
    40→        t_open = ts.get("open", 0)
    41→        t_fixed = ts.get("fixed", 0) + ts.get("auto_resolved", 0)
    42→        t_fp = ts.get("false_positive", 0)
    43→        t_wontfix = ts.get("wontfix", 0)
    44→        t_total = sum(ts.values())
    45→        strict_pct = round((t_fixed + t_fp) / t_total * 100) if t_total else 100
    46→        bar_len = 20
    47→        filled = round(strict_pct / 100 * bar_len)
    48→        bar = colorize("█" * filled, "green") + colorize(
    49→            "░" * (bar_len - filled), "dim"
    50→        )
    51→        rows.append(
    52→            [
    53→                f"Tier {tier_num}",
    54→                bar,
    55→                f"{strict_pct}%",
    56→                str(t_open),
    57→                str(t_fixed),
    58→                str(t_wontfix),
    59→            ]
    60→        )
    61→    print_table(
    62→        ["Tier", "Strict Progress", "%", "Open", "Fixed", "Debt"],
    63→        rows,
    64→        [40, 22, 5, 6, 6, 6],
    65→    )
    66→
    67→
    68→def _status_next_command(narrative: dict) -> str:
    69→    actions = narrative.get("actions", [])
    70→    return actions[0]["command"] if actions else "desloppify next --count 20"
    71→
    72→
    73→def write_status_query(
    74→    *,
    75→    state: dict,
    76→    stats: dict,
    77→    by_tier: dict,
    78→    dim_scores: dict,
    79→    scorecard_dims: list[dict],
    80→    subjective_measures: list[dict],

    86→    strict_score: float | None,
    87→    verified_strict_score: float | None,
    88→    plan: dict | None = None,
    89→) -> None:
    90→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    91→    open_scope = (
    92→        state_mod.open_scope_breakdown(findings, state.get("scan_path"))
    93→        if isinstance(findings, dict)
    94→        else None
    95→    )
    96→    write_query(
    97→        {
    98→            "command": "status",
    99→            "overall_score": overall_score,
   100→            "objective_score": objective_score,

   103→            "dimension_scores": dim_scores,

   108→            "last_scan": state.get("last_scan"),
   109→            "by_tier": by_tier,
   110→            "ignores": ignores,
   111→            "suppression": suppression,
   112→            "potentials": state.get("potentials"),
   113→            "codebase_metrics": state.get("codebase_metrics"),

   116→            "next_command": _status_next_command(narrative),

   118→            **({"plan": {

   121→                "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),

   123→                "plan_overrides_narrative": True,

   129→def show_ignore_summary(ignores: list[str], suppression: dict) -> None:
   130→    """Show ignore list plus suppression accountability from recent scans."""

   141→        print(

   151→    recent_raw = int(suppression.get("recent_raw_findings", 0) or 0)
   152→    if recent_scans > 1 and recent_raw > 0:
   153→        recent_ignored = int(suppression.get("recent_ignored", 0) or 0)
   154→        recent_pct = float(suppression.get("recent_suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   155→        print(
   156→            colorize(
```
