   162→
   163→def _scorecard_subjective_entries(state: dict, dim_scores: dict) -> list[dict]:
   164→    """Return subjective entries aligned to scorecard labels and ordering."""
   165→    return scorecard_subjective_entries(
   166→        state,
   167→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
   168→    )
   169→
   170→
   171→def _find_lowest_dimension(
   172→    dim_scores: dict,
   173→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
   174→) -> str | None:
   175→    """Return the name of the dimension with the lowest strict score."""
   176→    lowest_name = None
   177→    lowest_score = 101.0
   178→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   179→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   180→        if not ds:
   181→            continue
   182→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", ds["score"])
   183→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
   184→            lowest_score = strict_val
   185→            lowest_name = dim.name
   186→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   187→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   188→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
   189→            lowest_score = strict_val
   190→            lowest_name = entry.get("name")
   191→    return lowest_name
   192→
   193→
   194→def _open_review_issue_counts(state: dict) -> dict[str, int]:
   195→    """Count open review findings grouped by subjective dimension key."""
   196→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   197→    if not isinstance(findings, dict):
   198→        return {}
   199→
   200→    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
   201→    for finding in findings.values():
   202→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   203→            continue
   204→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   205→            continue
   206→        detail = finding.get("detail", {})
   207→        dimension = ""
   208→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
   209→            dimension = str(detail.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   210→        if not dimension:
   211→            dimension = str(finding.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   212→        if not dimension:
   213→            continue
   214→        counts[dimension] = counts.get(dimension, 0) + 1
   215→    return counts
   216→
   217→
   218→def _render_objective_dimensions(
   219→    dim_scores: dict, *, lowest_name: str | None, bar_len: int
   220→) -> None:
   221→    """Print rows for objective (detector-based) dimensions."""
   222→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   223→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   224→        if not ds:
   225→            continue
   226→        score_val = ds["score"]
   227→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   228→        checks = ds["checks"]
   229→
   230→        bar = dimension_bar(score_val, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
   231→
   232→        focus = colorize(" ←", "yellow") if dim.name == lowest_name else "  "
   233→        checks_str = f"{checks:>7,}"
   234→        action = dimension_action_type(dim.name)
   235→        print(
   236→            f"  {dim.name:<22} {checks_str}  {score_val:5.1f}%  {strict_val:5.1f}%  {bar}  T{dim.tier}  {action}{focus}"
   237→        )
   238→
   239→
   240→def _render_subjective_dimensions(
   241→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
   242→    *,
   243→    lowest_name: str | None,
   244→    bar_len: int,
   245→    review_issue_counts: dict[str, int],
   246→) -> None:
   247→    """Print rows for subjective (review-based) dimensions."""
   248→    if not scorecard_subjective:
   249→        return
   250→    print(
   251→        colorize(
   252→            "  ── Subjective Measures (matches scorecard.png) ──────────────────────",
   253→            "dim",
   254→        )
   255→    )
   256→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   257→        name = str(entry.get("name", "Unknown"))
   258→        score_val = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
   259→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", score_val))
   260→        tier = 4
   261→
   262→        bar = dimension_bar(score_val, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
   263→
   264→        focus = colorize(" ←", "yellow") if name == lowest_name else "  "
   265→        checks_str = f"{'—':>7}"
   266→        stale_tag = colorize(" [stale]", "yellow") if entry.get("stale") else ""
   267→        placeholder_tag = (
   268→            colorize(" [unassessed]", "yellow") if entry.get("placeholder") else ""
   269→        )
   270→        dim_key = str(entry.get("dimension_key", "")).strip()
   271→        cli_keys = [
   272→            str(key).strip()
   273→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   274→            if isinstance(key, str) and str(key).strip()
   275→        ]
   276→        if dim_key:
   277→            issue_count = int(review_issue_counts.get(dim_key, 0))
   278→        elif cli_keys:
   279→            issue_count = int(sum(review_issue_counts.get(key, 0) for key in cli_keys))
   280→        else:
   281→            issue_count = 0
   282→        issue_style = "yellow" if strict_val < 95.0 and issue_count == 0 else "dim"
   283→        issue_tag = colorize(f" [open issues: {issue_count}]", issue_style)
   284→        print(
   285→            f"  {name:<22} {checks_str}  {score_val:5.1f}%  {strict_val:5.1f}%  {bar}  T{tier}  {'review'}{focus}{stale_tag}"
   286→            f"{placeholder_tag}{issue_tag}"
   287→        )
   288→
   289→
   290→def _render_dimension_legend(scorecard_subjective: list[dict], state: dict | None = None) -> None:
   291→    """Print the legend footer and stale-dimension re-review hint."""
   292→    print(
   293→        colorize("  Health = open penalized | Strict = open + wontfix penalized", "dim")
   294→    )
   295→    print(
   296→        colorize(
   297→            "  Action: fix=auto-fixer | move=reorganize | refactor=manual rewrite | manual=review & fix",
   298→            "dim",
   299→        )
   300→    )
   301→    stale_keys = [
   302→        str(e.get("dimension_key"))
   303→        for e in scorecard_subjective
   304→        if e.get("stale") and e.get("dimension_key")
   305→    ]
   306→    if stale_keys:
   307→        n = len(stale_keys)
   308→        dims_arg = ",".join(stale_keys)
   309→        print(
   310→            colorize(
   311→                f"  [stale] = subjective assessment outdated, not an unresolved finding"
   312→                f" — re-review to refresh",
   313→                "yellow",
   314→            )
   315→        )
   316→        print(
   317→            colorize(
   318→                f"  {n} stale dimension{'s' if n != 1 else ''}"
   319→                f": `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {dims_arg} --force-review-rerun`",
   320→                "yellow",
   321→            )
   322→        )
   323→
   324→
   325→def show_dimension_table(state: dict, dim_scores: dict) -> None:
   326→    """Show dimension health table with dual scores and progress bars."""
   327→    print()
   328→    bar_len = 20
   329→    print(
   330→        colorize(
   331→            f"  {'Dimension':<22} {'Checks':>7}  {'Health':>6}  {'Strict':>6}  {'Bar':<{bar_len + 2}} {'Tier'}  {'Action'}",
   332→            "dim",
   333→        )
   334→    )
   335→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 86, "dim"))
   336→
   337→    scorecard_subjective = _scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores)
   338→    lowest_name = _find_lowest_dimension(dim_scores, scorecard_subjective)
   339→    review_issue_counts = _open_review_issue_counts(state)
   340→
   341→    _render_objective_dimensions(dim_scores, lowest_name=lowest_name, bar_len=bar_len)
   342→    _render_subjective_dimensions(
   343→        scorecard_subjective,
   344→        lowest_name=lowest_name,
   345→        bar_len=bar_len,
   346→        review_issue_counts=review_issue_counts,
   347→    )
   348→    _render_dimension_legend(scorecard_subjective, state=state)
   349→    print()
   350→
   351→
   352→def show_focus_suggestion(
   353→    dim_scores: dict, state: dict, *, plan: dict | None = None
   354→) -> None:
   355→    """Show the lowest-scoring dimension as the focus area."""
   356→    # When plan has an active focus cluster, show that instead
   357→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   358→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   359→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name, {})
   360→        remaining = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   361→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   362→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   363→        print(
   364→            colorize(
   365→                f"  Focus: {cluster_name} ({remaining} items remaining){desc_str}",
   366→                "cyan",
   367→            )
   368→        )
   369→        print()
   370→        return
   371→
   372→    scorecard_subjective = _scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores)
   373→    lowest_name = _find_lowest_dimension(dim_scores, scorecard_subjective)
   374→    if not lowest_name:
   375→        return
   376→
   377→    # Determine kind, score, and issue count from the resolved name
   378→    lowest_kind = None
   379→    lowest_score = 101.0
   380→    lowest_issues = 0
   381→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   382→        if dim.name == lowest_name:
   383→            ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   384→            if ds:
   385→                lowest_score = float(ds.get("strict", ds["score"]))
   386→                lowest_kind = "mechanical"
   387→                lowest_issues = int(ds.get("issues", 0))
   388→            break
   389→    else:
   390→        for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   391→            if entry.get("name") == lowest_name:
   392→                lowest_score = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   393→                lowest_kind = "subjective"
   394→                lowest_issues = 0
   395→                break
   396→
   397→    if lowest_name and lowest_score < 100:
   398→        if lowest_kind == "subjective":
   399→            print(
   400→                colorize(
   401→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — re-review to improve",
   402→                    "cyan",
   403→                )
   404→            )
   405→            print()
   406→            return
   407→
   408→        potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
   409→        target_dim = next((d for d in DIMENSIONS if d.name == lowest_name), None)
   410→        if target_dim:
   411→            impact = 0.0
   412→            for det in target_dim.detectors:
   413→                impact = compute_score_impact(
   414→                    {
   415→                        k: {
   416→                            "score": v["score"],
   417→                            "tier": v.get("tier", 3),
   418→                            "detectors": v.get("detectors", {}),
   419→                        }
   420→                        for k, v in dim_scores.items()
   421→                        if "score" in v
   422→                    },
   423→                    potentials,
   424→                    det,
   425→                    lowest_issues,
   426→                )
   427→                if impact > 0:
   428→                    break
   429→
   430→            impact_str = f" for +{impact:.1f} pts" if impact > 0 else ""
   431→            print(
   432→                colorize(
   433→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — "
   434→                    f"fix {lowest_issues} items{impact_str}",
   435→                    "cyan",
   436→                )
   437→            )
   438→            print()
   439→
   440→
   441→def show_subjective_followup(
   442→    state: dict, dim_scores: dict, *, target_strict_score: float
   443→) -> None:
   444→    """Show concrete subjective follow-up commands when applicable."""
   445→    if not dim_scores:
   446→        return
   447→
   448→    subjective = _scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores)
   449→    if not subjective:
   450→        return
   451→
   452→    followup = reporting_dimensions_mod.build_subjective_followup(
   453→        state,
   454→        subjective,
   455→        threshold=target_strict_score,
   456→        max_quality_items=3,
   457→        max_integrity_items=5,
   458→    )
   459→    if print_subjective_followup(followup):
   460→        print()
   461→
   462→
   463→def show_agent_plan(narrative: dict, *, plan: dict | None = None) -> None:
   464→    """Show concise action plan derived from narrative.actions.
   465→
   466→    When a living *plan* is active, renders plan focus/progress instead.
   467→    """
   468→    if plan and (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters")):
   469→        print_agent_plan(
   470→            [],
   471→            plan=plan,
   472→            header="  AGENT PLAN (use `desloppify next` to see your next task):",
   473→        )
   474→        return

   488→

   491→
   492→
   493→def _collect_structural_areas(
   494→    state: dict,
   495→) -> list[tuple[str, list]] | None:
   496→    """Collect T3/T4 structural findings grouped by area.
   497→
   498→    Returns sorted area list, or None if insufficient data to display.
   499→    """
   500→    findings = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   501→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
   502→    )
   503→
   504→    structural = [
   505→        f
   506→        for f in findings.values()
   507→        if f["tier"] in (3, 4) and f["status"] in ("open", "wontfix")
   508→    ]
   509→
   510→    if len(structural) < 5:
   511→        return None
   512→
   513→    areas: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
   514→    for f in structural:
   515→        area = get_area(str(f.get("file", "")))
   516→        areas[area].append(f)
   517→
   518→    if len(areas) < 2:
   519→        return None
   520→
   521→    return sorted(areas.items(), key=lambda x: -sum(f["tier"] for f in x[1]))
   522→
   523→
   524→def _build_area_rows(sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]], *, max_areas: int = 15) -> list[list[str]]:
   525→    """Build table rows from sorted area findings."""
   526→    rows = []
   527→    for area, area_findings in sorted_areas[:max_areas]:
   528→        t3 = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["tier"] == 3)
   529→        t4 = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["tier"] == 4)
   530→        open_count = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["status"] == "open")
   531→        debt_count = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["status"] == "wontfix")
   532→        weight = sum(f["tier"] for f in area_findings)
   533→        rows.append(
   534→            [
   535→                area,
   536→                str(len(area_findings)),
   537→                f"T3:{t3} T4:{t4}",
   538→                str(open_count),
   539→                str(debt_count),
   540→                str(weight),
   541→            ]
   542→        )
   543→    return rows
   544→
   545→
   546→def _render_area_workflow(sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]], *, max_areas: int = 15) -> None:
   547→    """Print the overflow count and workflow instructions."""
   548→    remaining = len(sorted_areas) - max_areas
   549→    if remaining > 0:
   550→        print(colorize(f"\n  ... and {remaining} more areas", "dim"))
   551→
   552→    print(colorize("\n  Workflow:", "dim"))
   553→    print(colorize("    1. desloppify show <area> --status wontfix --top 50", "dim"))
   554→    print(
   555→        colorize(
   556→            "    2. Create tasks/<date>-<area-name>.md with decomposition plan", "dim"
   557→        )
   558→    )
   559→    print(
   560→        colorize("    3. Farm each task doc to a sub-agent for implementation", "dim")
   561→    )
   562→    print()
   563→
   564→
   565→def show_structural_areas(state: dict):
   566→    """Show structural debt grouped by area when T3/T4 debt is significant."""
   567→    sorted_areas = _collect_structural_areas(state)
   568→    if sorted_areas is None:
   569→        return
   570→
   571→    print(colorize("\n  ── Structural Debt by Area ──", "bold"))
   572→    print(
   573→        colorize(
   574→            "  Create a task doc for each area → farm to sub-agents for decomposition",
   575→            "dim",
   576→        )
   577→    )
   578→    print()
   579→
   580→    rows = _build_area_rows(sorted_areas)
   581→    print_table(
   582→        ["Area", "Items", "Tiers", "Open", "Debt", "Weight"], rows, [42, 6, 10, 5, 5, 7]
   583→    )
   584→
   585→    _render_area_workflow(sorted_areas)
   586→
   587→
   588→def show_review_summary(state: dict):
   589→    """Show review findings summary if any exist."""
   590→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   591→    review_open = [
   592→        f
   593→        for f in findings.values()
   594→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   595→    ]
   596→    if not review_open:
   597→        return
   598→    uninvestigated = sum(
   599→        1 for f in review_open if not f.get("detail", {}).get("investigation")
   600→    )
   601→    parts = [f"{len(review_open)} finding{'s' if len(review_open) != 1 else ''} open"]
   602→    if uninvestigated:
   603→        parts.append(f"{uninvestigated} uninvestigated")
   604→    print(colorize(f"  Review: {', '.join(parts)} — `desloppify show review --status open`", "cyan"))
   605→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   606→    if "Test health" in dim_scores:
   607→        print(
   608→            colorize(
   609→                "  Test health tracks coverage + review; review findings track issues found.",
   610→                "dim",
   611→            )
   612→        )
   613→    print()
   614→
   615→

1→"""Compact queue table renderer for ``plan queue``."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.guardrails import print_synthesis_guardrail_info
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize, print_table
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan import compute_new_finding_ids, load_plan
    12→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    13→
    14→
    15→def _truncate(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    16→    if len(text) <= width:
    17→        return text
    18→    return text[: width - 1] + "\u2026"
    19→
    20→
    21→def _cluster_tier_label(item: dict) -> str:
    22→    """Compute a tier label from cluster members, e.g. 'T2' or 'T1-T3'."""
    23→    members = item.get("members", [])
    24→    if not members:
    25→        return ""
    26→    tiers = {int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) for m in members}
    27→    lo, hi = min(tiers), max(tiers)
    28→    if lo == hi:
    29→        return f"T{lo}"
    30→    return f"T{lo}-T{hi}"
    31→
    32→
    33→def _resolve_plan_context(plan: dict, cluster_filter: str | None) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None]:
    34→    plan_data: dict | None = None
    35→    if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("overrides") or plan.get("clusters"):
    36→        plan_data = plan
    37→
    38→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter
    39→    if plan_data and not cluster_filter:
    40→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
    41→        if active_cluster:
    42→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
    43→    return plan_data, effective_cluster
    44→
    45→
    46→def _print_queue_header(
    47→    *,
    48→    items: list[dict],
    49→    tier_counts: dict,
    50→    include_skipped: bool,
    51→    plan: dict,
    52→    plan_data: dict | None,
    53→    new_count: int = 0,
    54→) -> None:
    55→    total = len(items)
    56→    skipped_count = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("plan_skipped"))
    57→    non_skipped = total - skipped_count
    58→    tc_parts = " ".join(f"T{t}:{tier_counts.get(t, 0)}" for t in (1, 2, 3, 4))
    59→    finding_total = sum(tier_counts.get(t, 0) for t in (1, 2, 3, 4))
    60→    new_suffix = f"  ({new_count} new this scan)" if new_count > 0 else ""
    61→    if finding_total != non_skipped:
    62→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {non_skipped} entries  ({finding_total} findings: {tc_parts}){new_suffix}", "bold"))
    63→    else:
    64→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {non_skipped} items  ({tc_parts}){new_suffix}", "bold"))
    65→
    66→    focus = plan.get("active_cluster") if plan_data else None
    67→    if focus:
    68→        print(colorize(f"  Focus: {focus}", "cyan"))
    69→
    70→    if include_skipped or skipped_count != 0:
    71→        return
    72→    plan_skipped_count = len(plan.get("skipped", {})) if plan_data else 0
    73→    if not plan_skipped_count:
    74→        return
    75→    print(
    76→        colorize(
    77→            f"  ({plan_skipped_count} skipped item{'s' if plan_skipped_count != 1 else ''}"
    78→            " hidden — use --include-skipped)",
    79→            "dim",
    80→        )
    81→    )
    82→
    83→
    84→def _queue_display_items(items: list[dict], *, top: int) -> list[dict]:
    85→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
    86→        return items[:top]
    87→    return items
    88→
    89→
    90→def _build_rows(display_items: list[dict], new_ids: set[str] | None = None) -> list[list[str]]:
    91→    rows: list[list[str]] = []
    92→    _new = new_ids or set()
    93→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
    94→        pos = str(idx)
    95→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
    96→
    97→        if kind == "cluster":
    98→            tier_str = _cluster_tier_label(item)
    99→            member_count = item.get("member_count", 0)
   100→            detector = item.get("detector", "cluster")
   101→            new_in_cluster = sum(
   102→                1 for m in item.get("members", [])
   103→                if m.get("id") in _new
   104→            )
   105→            new_tag = f" (+{new_in_cluster} new)" if new_in_cluster else ""
   106→            summary = f"[{member_count} items{new_tag}] {item.get('summary', '')}"
   107→            cluster_name = item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id", ""))
   108→        else:
   109→            tier_val = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   110→            tier_str = f"T{tier_val}"
   111→            detector = item.get("detector", "")
   112→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   113→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   114→            cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "") if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else ""
   115→
   116→        prefix = "* " if item.get("id") in _new else ""
   117→        suffix = " [skip]" if item.get("plan_skipped") else ""
   118→        summary_display = _truncate(prefix + summary, 48) + suffix
   119→        rows.append([pos, tier_str, detector, summary_display, cluster_name])
   120→    return rows
   121→
   122→
   123→def cmd_plan_queue(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   124→    """Render a compact table of all upcoming queue items."""
   125→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   126→    state = runtime.state
   127→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   128→        return
   129→
   130→    top = getattr(args, "top", 30)
   131→    tier_filter = getattr(args, "tier", None)
   132→    cluster_filter = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
   133→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
   134→
   135→    plan = load_plan()
   136→    print_synthesis_guardrail_info(plan=plan, state=state)
   137→    plan_data, effective_cluster = _resolve_plan_context(plan, cluster_filter)
   138→
   139→    queue = build_work_queue(
   140→        state,
   141→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   142→            tier=tier_filter,
   143→            count=None,
   144→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   145→            status="open",
   146→            include_subjective=True,
   147→            plan=plan_data,
   148→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   149→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   150→            collapse_clusters=True,
   151→        ),
   152→    )
   153→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   154→    tier_counts = queue.get("tier_counts", {})
   155→
   156→    sort_by = getattr(args, "sort", "priority")
   157→    all_new_ids: set[str] = queue.get("new_ids", set())
   158→    # Merge review-based new finding IDs (since last synthesis)
   159→    review_new_ids = compute_new_finding_ids(plan, state)
   160→    all_new_ids = all_new_ids | review_new_ids
   161→    item_ids = {it.get("id") for it in items}
   162→    new_ids = all_new_ids & item_ids
   163→
   164→    if sort_by == "recent":
   165→        items = sorted(items, key=lambda it: it.get("first_seen", ""), reverse=True)
   166→
   167→    _print_queue_header(
   168→        items=items,
   169→        tier_counts=tier_counts,
   170→        include_skipped=include_skipped,
   171→        plan=plan,
   172→        plan_data=plan_data,
   173→        new_count=len(new_ids),
   174→    )
   175→
   176→    if not items:
   177→        print(colorize("\n  Queue is empty.", "green"))
   178→        return
   179→
   180→    # Determine which items to show
   181→    display_items = _queue_display_items(items, top=top)
   182→    headers = ["#", "Tier", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster"]
   183→    rows = _build_rows(display_items, new_ids=new_ids)
   184→
   185→    print()
   186→    widths = [4, 4, 12, 50, 16]
   187→    print_table(headers, rows, widths=widths)
   188→
   189→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   190→        remaining = len(items) - top
   191→        print(colorize(
   192→            f"\n  ... and {remaining} more (use --top 0 to show all)", "dim"
   193→        ))
   194→    print()
   195→
   196→
   197→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_queue"]
   198→
```

## Summary: Queue Headline Number Rendering

I've traced through the complete flow of how the "Queue: N items remaining" headline is rendered in both the `status`, `next`, and `plan queue` commands. Here are my findings:

**Function: `plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan=None)` (lines 9-23)**
- Returns an `int` representing the queue count
- Calls `build_work_queue()` with:
  - `status="open"` — filters to open findings only
  - `count=None` — doesn't limit the result count, gets full queue
  - `collapse_clusters=True` — cluster members are collapsed into meta-items (counted as 1)
  - `include_skipped=False` — **explicitly excludes skipped items**
- Returns `result["total"]` from the WorkQueueResult dict

**Function: `print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(plan, queue_remaining)` (lines 33-54)**

  " remaining. Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`."

- Only printed when a plan is active with `plan_start_scores` frozen and there are remaining queue items
- Called from `print_execution_or_reveal()` and `show_score_with_plan_context()`

- Called from `cmd_next()` at line 272, passing:
  - `queue_total` = count computed via `plan_aware_queue_count()`
  - `plan_start_strict` = frozen plan start score

      try:

**Function: `build_work_queue()` (lines 317-455)**

  ```
- The `filtered` list is the result after:
  1. Building all items from `build_finding_items()` (objective findings)
  2. Adding subjective items if `include_subjective=True`
  3. **Excluding skipped items** if `include_skipped=False` (lines 185-186)
  4. Applying plan order via `_apply_plan_order()` (lines 375-382)

---

### **Key Question Answers:**

**What number is shown as the headline "queue count"?**
- The `total` field from `WorkQueueResult`, which is `len(filtered)` after all filtering/collapsing

**How is it computed?**
- Via `plan_aware_queue_count()` which calls `build_work_queue()` with specific options
- The number shown is the **length of the filtered items list after all transformations**

**What does it include/exclude?**
- **Includes:**
  - All open findings (objective: detectors)
  - Subjective dimension items (when `include_subjective=True`)
  - Synthesis-needed synthetic items
  - Auto-cluster meta-items (as single items, not their members)
  
- **Excludes:**
  - Skipped items (temporary, permanent, false_positive) — `include_skipped=False` by default
  - Findings that don't match the tier filter (if tier is specified)
  - Findings outside the scope (if scope is specified)
  - Findings in clusters when cluster drilling is active (filtered in `_apply_plan_order()`)

**Where exactly is the headline text printed?**
1. **Primary:** `desloppify/app/commands/helpers/queue_progress.py:50` in `print_frozen_score_with_queue_context()`
   - Used by both `status` and `next` commands
2. **Alternative:** `desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/render.py:536` in `render_followup_nudges()`
   - Same text, printed in identical circumstances
3. **Plan command:** `desloppify/app/commands/plan/queue_render.py:62-64` shows similar "Queue: N items" but computes differently for the plan view

---

### **Important Implementation Detail:**

The queue count shown in the headline is **plan-aware**:
- Items are reordered per the living plan (via `_apply_plan_order()`)
- Auto-clusters are collapsed (members count as 1)
- Skipped items are excluded from the count
- This means the "Queue: N items remaining" changes as you mark items as done, skip them, or manipulate the plan

> DEVELOPER

Design an implementation for GitHub issue #180: "Queue headline should prioritize plan/cluster actionable count with compact breakdown."

## Problem
The queue headline (`Queue: 1934 items remaining`) shows a single number that mixes leaf findings, subjective items, and doesn't communicate plan-level workload. Users can't tell at a glance what they're actually working through.

## Current architecture (from exploration)

### Data flow:
1. `build_work_queue()` in `engine/_work_queue/core.py` builds the queue:
   - `finding_items` = all open objective findings
   - adds subjective items (some may be `backlog_gated` and suppressed)
   - applies plan order via `_apply_plan_order()`
   - collapses clusters via `_collapse_clusters()`
   - applies interleave guardrail
   - returns `WorkQueueResult` TypedDict with `total: int`, `items`, `tier_counts`, etc.

2. `plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan)` in `app/commands/helpers/queue_progress.py`:
   - Calls `build_work_queue(state, options=QueueBuildOptions(status="open", count=None, plan=plan, collapse_clusters=True, include_skipped=False))`
   - Returns `result["total"]` (a single int)

3. Headline rendering appears in 2 places:
   - `queue_progress.py:print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(plan, queue_remaining)` line 50:
     `f"Queue: {queue_remaining} items remaining. Score will not update until..."`
   - `next_parts/render.py:render_followup_nudges()` line 536:
     Same format, receives `queue_total` parameter

### Callers:
- `status_cmd.py`: calls `plan_aware_queue_count()` → passes to `print_frozen_score_with_queue_context()`
- `next.py`: calls `plan_aware_queue_count()` via `_plan_queue_context()` → passes `queue_total` to `render_followup_nudges()`

### Available data sources:
- `plan["queue_order"]` = list of explicitly ordered item IDs (e.g., 292)
- `plan["skipped"]` = dict of skipped items (e.g., 23)
- `plan["active_cluster"]` = currently focused cluster name
- `plan["clusters"]` = cluster definitions with finding_ids
- `state["findings"]` = all findings with status
- `build_work_queue` result = `total` (after collapse + filter), `items`, `tier_counts`
- During queue building: `finding_items` count, subjective items count, backlog_gated suppressed count

## Proposed output format (from issue)
```
Queue: 292 plan items [leaf: 1934 | open findings: 1942 | skipped: 23 | subjective cards: 15 | suppressed: 0]
```
With cluster focus:
```
Queue: 0 in focus cluster `smart-t1-review` [plan total: 292 | leaf: 1934 | skipped: 23]
```

## Design requirements:
1. Primary number = plan-ordered actionable items (items in queue_order minus skipped)
2. Bracket breakdown with: total queue items, open findings, skipped, subjective, suppressed
3. Focus cluster variant when active
4. When no plan exists, fall back to simple format
5. Must update both rendering locations consistently
6. Keep `plan_aware_queue_count()` backward-compatible (scan_preflight.py and potentially other callers use it)
7. Minimal changes to `build_work_queue` — compute breakdown from available data

## What to design:
1. Data structure for the breakdown
2. Where to compute each number
3. How to thread the breakdown to rendering
4. The exact rendering format
5. How to handle edge cases (no plan, no clusters, etc.)
6. Test strategy

1→"""Unified work-queue selection for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from collections import deque
     7→from typing import TypedDict
     8→
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
    10→    ALL_STATUSES,
    11→    ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    12→    build_subjective_items,
    13→    build_synthesis_item,
    14→    scope_matches,
    15→)
    16→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import (
    17→    build_finding_items,
    18→    choose_fallback_tier,
    19→    group_queue_items,
    20→    item_explain,
    21→    item_sort_key,
    22→    tier_counts,
    23→)
    24→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    25→
    26→
    27→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    28→class QueueBuildOptions:
    29→    """Configuration for queue construction and tier selection behavior."""
    30→
    31→    tier: int | None = None
    32→    count: int | None = 1
    33→    scan_path: str | None = None
    34→    scope: str | None = None
    35→    status: str = "open"
    36→    include_subjective: bool = True
    37→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    38→    chronic: bool = False
    39→    no_tier_fallback: bool = False
    40→    explain: bool = False
    41→    plan: dict | None = None
    42→    include_skipped: bool = False
    43→    cluster: str | None = None
    44→    collapse_clusters: bool = True
    45→
    46→
    47→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    48→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    49→
    50→    items: list[dict]
    51→    total: int
    52→    tier_counts: dict[int, int]
    53→    requested_tier: int | None
    54→    selected_tier: int | None
    55→    fallback_reason: str | None
    56→    available_tiers: list[int]
    57→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    58→
    59→
    60→def _is_subjective_queue_item(item: dict) -> bool:
    61→    """Return True for queue items that represent subjective work."""
    62→    return item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
    63→
    64→
    65→def _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(
    66→    items: list[dict],
    67→    *,
    68→    objective_burst: int = 3,
    69→    subjective_burst: int = 1,
    70→) -> list[dict]:
    71→    """Interleave subjective work with objective work to avoid starvation."""
    72→    if objective_burst < 1 or subjective_burst < 1:
    73→        return items
    74→
    75→    subjective_items = deque(
    76→        item for item in items if _is_subjective_queue_item(item)
    77→    )
    78→    objective_items = deque(
    79→        item for item in items if not _is_subjective_queue_item(item)
    80→    )
    81→    if not subjective_items or not objective_items:
    82→        return items
    83→
    84→    interleaved: list[dict] = []
    85→    while objective_items and subjective_items:
    86→        for _ in range(objective_burst):
    87→            if not objective_items:
    88→                break
    89→            interleaved.append(objective_items.popleft())
    90→        for _ in range(subjective_burst):
    91→            if not subjective_items:
    92→                break
    93→            interleaved.append(subjective_items.popleft())
    94→
    95→    interleaved.extend(objective_items)
    96→    interleaved.extend(subjective_items)
    97→    return interleaved
    98→
    99→
   100→def _new_item_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   101→    """Return finding IDs added in the most recent scan."""
   102→    scan_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
   103→    if not scan_history:
   104→        return set()
   105→    threshold = scan_history[-1].get("timestamp", "")
   106→    if not threshold:
   107→        return set()
   108→    return {
   109→        fid
   110→        for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   111→        if f.get("first_seen", "") >= threshold
   112→    }
   113→
   114→
   115→def _apply_plan_order(
   116→    items: list[dict],
   117→    plan: dict,
   118→    *,
   119→    include_skipped: bool = False,
   120→    cluster: str | None = None,
   121→    new_ids: set[str] | None = None,
   122→) -> list[dict]:
   123→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
   124→
   125→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
   126→    2. Existing remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
   127→    3. New items (from latest scan) sort after existing remaining items.
   128→    4. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
   129→    5. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
   130→    """
   131→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   132→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   133→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
   134→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
   135→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   136→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
   137→
   138→    # Build lookup
   139→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
   140→    for item in items:
   141→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
   142→
   143→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
   144→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
   145→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
   146→        if override.get("description"):
   147→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
   148→        if override.get("note"):
   149→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
   150→        if override.get("cluster"):
   151→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
   152→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   153→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
   154→                "name": cluster_name,
   155→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
   156→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
   157→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
   158→            }
   159→
   160→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   161→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   162→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   163→    for fid in queue_order:
   164→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   165→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   166→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   167→
   168→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   169→    remaining_existing: list[dict] = []
   170→    remaining_new: list[dict] = []
   171→    _new = new_ids or set()
   172→    for item in items:
   173→        item_id = item["id"]
   174→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   175→            continue
   176→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   177→            skipped_items.append(item)
   178→        elif item_id in _new:
   179→            remaining_new.append(item)
   180→        else:
   181→            remaining_existing.append(item)
   182→
   183→    # Assign queue positions — new items after existing ones
   184→    result = ordered + remaining_existing + remaining_new
   185→    if include_skipped:
   186→        result = result + skipped_items
   187→
   188→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   189→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   190→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   191→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   192→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   193→            if skip_entry:
   194→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   195→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   196→                if skip_reason:
   197→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   198→
   199→    # Filter to cluster if requested
   200→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   201→    if effective_cluster:
   202→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   203→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   204→        if cluster_member_ids:
   205→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   206→
   207→    return result
   208→
   209→
   210→def _item_matches_tier(item: dict, tier: int) -> bool:
   211→    """Check if an item (or any of its members for clusters) matches a tier."""
   212→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   213→        return any(
   214→            int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   215→            for m in item.get("members", [])
   216→        )
   217→    return int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   218→
   219→
   220→def _action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   221→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   222→    try:
   223→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   224→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   225→        if meta:
   226→            return meta.action_type
   227→    except ImportError as exc:
   228→        _ = exc
   229→    return "manual_fix"
   230→
   231→
   232→_ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   233→
   234→
   235→def _collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   236→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items."""
   237→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   238→    if not clusters:
   239→        return items
   240→
   241→    # Build mapping: finding_id → auto-cluster name
   242→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   243→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   244→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   245→            continue
   246→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   247→            fid_to_cluster[fid] = name
   248→
   249→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   250→        return items
   251→
   252→    # Collect members for each cluster, preserving order
   253→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   254→    non_cluster_items: list[dict] = []
   255→
   256→    for item in items:
   257→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   258→        if cname:
   259→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   260→        else:
   261→            non_cluster_items.append(item)
   262→
   263→    # Build cluster meta-items
   264→    result: list[dict] = list(non_cluster_items)
   265→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   266→        # Don't collapse singletons — show them as individual findings
   267→        if len(members) < 2:
   268→            result.extend(members)
   269→            continue
   270→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cname, {})
   271→        detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   272→        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   273→        # Derive action_type from the actual cluster action, not just the detector
   274→        if "desloppify fix" in action:
   275→            action_type = "auto_fix"
   276→        elif "desloppify move" in action:
   277→            action_type = "reorganize"
   278→        else:
   279→            action_type = _action_type_for_detector(detector)
   280→            # If detector says auto_fix but cluster has no fix command, it's really refactor
   281→            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
   282→                action_type = "refactor"
   283→
   284→        # Use stored description but patch the count if it differs from visible members
   285→        stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   286→        total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   287→        if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   288→            # Replace the stored count with the visible count
   289→            summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   290→        else:
   291→            summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   292→
   293→        primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   294→        if not primary_command:
   295→            primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cname} --count 10"
   296→
   297→        meta_item: dict = {
   298→            "id": cname,
   299→            "kind": "cluster",
   300→            "action_type": action_type,
   301→            "summary": summary,
   302→            "members": members,
   303→            "member_count": len(members),
   304→            "primary_command": primary_command,
   305→            "cluster_name": cname,
   306→            "cluster_auto": True,
   307→            "confidence": "high",
   308→            "detector": detector,
   309→            "file": "",
   310→        }
   311→        result.append(meta_item)
   312→
   313→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   314→    return result
   315→
   316→
   317→def build_work_queue(
   318→    state: StateModel,
   319→    *,
   320→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   321→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   322→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   323→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   324→
   325→    status = resolved_options.status
   326→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   327→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   328→    try:
   329→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   330→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   331→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   332→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   333→
   334→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   335→        state,
   336→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   337→        status_filter=status,
   338→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   339→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   340→    )
   341→
   342→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   343→    has_objective_backlog = bool(finding_items)
   344→    if (
   345→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   346→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   347→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   348→    ):
   349→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   350→            state,
   351→            state.get("findings", {}),
   352→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   353→        )
   354→        for item in subjective_items:
   355→            if not scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   356→                continue
   357→            # Suppress subjective items whose only action is a review rerun
   358→            # (--force-review-rerun) while objective backlog exists — the
   359→            # review preflight blocks reruns until the backlog drains.
   360→            if has_objective_backlog and item.get("backlog_gated"):
   361→                continue
   362→            all_items.append(item)
   363→
   364→    # Inject synthesis::pending synthetic item when plan requires it
   365→    if resolved_options.plan and status in {"open", "all"}:
   366→        synth_item = build_synthesis_item(resolved_options.plan, state)
   367→        if synth_item is not None:
   368→            all_items.append(synth_item)
   369→
   370→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   371→
   372→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   373→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
   374→    if resolved_options.plan:
   375→        new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   376→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   377→            all_items,
   378→            resolved_options.plan,
   379→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   380→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   381→            new_ids=new_ids,
   382→        )
   383→
   384→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   385→    should_collapse = (
   386→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   387→        and resolved_options.plan
   388→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   389→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   390→    )
   391→    if should_collapse:
   392→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   393→
   394→    should_interleave = (
   395→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   396→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   397→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   398→        and resolved_options.tier is None
   399→        and not resolved_options.scope
   400→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   401→    )
   402→    if should_interleave:
   403→        all_items = _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(all_items)
   404→
   405→    counts = tier_counts(all_items)
   406→
   407→    requested_tier = (
   408→        int(resolved_options.tier) if resolved_options.tier is not None else None
   409→    )
   410→    selected_tier = requested_tier
   411→    fallback_reason = None
   412→    filtered = all_items
   413→
   414→    if requested_tier is not None:
   415→        filtered = [
   416→            item
   417→            for item in all_items
   418→            if _item_matches_tier(item, requested_tier)
   419→        ]
   420→        if not filtered and not resolved_options.no_tier_fallback:
   421→            chosen = choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier, counts)
   422→            if chosen is not None:
   423→                selected_tier = chosen
   424→                filtered = [
   425→                    item
   426→                    for item in all_items
   427→                    if _item_matches_tier(item, chosen)
   428→                ]
   429→                fallback_reason = (
   430→                    f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open -> showing T{chosen} "
   431→                    "(nearest non-empty)."
   432→                )
   433→        elif not filtered:
   434→            fallback_reason = f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open."
   435→
   436→    total = len(filtered)
   437→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   438→        filtered = filtered[: resolved_options.count]
   439→
   440→    if resolved_options.explain:
   441→        for item in filtered:
   442→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   443→
   444→    available_tiers = [tier for tier, value in counts.items() if value > 0]
   445→    return {
   446→        "items": filtered,
   447→        "total": total,
   448→        "tier_counts": counts,
   449→        "requested_tier": requested_tier,
   450→        "selected_tier": selected_tier,
   451→        "fallback_reason": fallback_reason,
   452→        "available_tiers": available_tiers,
   453→        "grouped": group_queue_items(filtered, "item"),
   454→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   455→    }
   456→
   457→
   458→__all__ = [
   459→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   460→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   461→    "WorkQueueResult",
   462→    "build_work_queue",
   463→    "group_queue_items",
   464→]
   465→

1→"""Plan-aware queue progress and frozen score display helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
     6→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     7→
     8→
     9→def plan_aware_queue_count(state: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> int:
    10→    """Count remaining plan-aware queue items (skips excluded, clusters collapsed)."""
    11→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    12→
    13→    result = build_work_queue(
    14→        state,
    15→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    16→            status="open",
    17→            count=None,
    18→            plan=plan,
    19→            collapse_clusters=True,
    20→            include_skipped=False,
    21→        ),
    22→    )
    23→    return result["total"]
    24→
    25→
    26→def get_plan_start_strict(plan: dict | None) -> float | None:
    27→    """Extract the frozen plan-start strict score, or None if unset."""
    28→    if not plan:
    29→        return None
    30→    return plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
    31→
    32→
    33→def print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
    34→    plan: dict,
    35→    queue_remaining: int,
    36→) -> None:
    37→    """Show frozen plan-start score + queue progress."""
    38→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
    39→    strict = scores.get("strict")
    40→    if strict is None:
    41→        return
    42→    print(
    43→        colorize(
    44→            f"\n  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {strict:.1f}/100",
    45→            "cyan",
    46→        )
    47→    )
    48→    print(
    49→        colorize(
    50→            f"  Queue: {queue_remaining} item{'s' if queue_remaining != 1 else ''}"
    51→            " remaining. Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
    52→            "dim",
    53→        )
    54→    )
    55→
    56→
    57→def print_execution_or_reveal(
    58→    state: dict,
    59→    prev,
    60→    plan: dict | None,
    61→) -> None:
    62→    """Context-aware score display: frozen plan-start score or live scores.
    63→
    64→    If a plan has ``plan_start_scores`` and there are queue items remaining,
    65→    show the frozen score + queue progress.  Otherwise delegate to the
    66→    existing live ``print_score_update``.
    67→    """
    68→    if plan and plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict") is not None:
    69→        try:
    70→            remaining = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan)
    71→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    72→            remaining = 0
    73→        if remaining > 0:
    74→            print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(plan, remaining)
    75→            return
    76→
    77→    # No active plan cycle or queue is clear — live scores
    78→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import print_score_update
    79→
    80→    print_score_update(state, prev)
    81→
    82→
    83→def show_score_with_plan_context(state: dict, prev) -> None:
    84→    """Load plan (best-effort) and show frozen or live score context.
    85→
    86→    Encapsulates the common load_plan + PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS + reveal
    87→    choreography so command modules don't each repeat it.
    88→    """
    89→    from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    90→
    91→    try:
    92→        plan = load_plan()
    93→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    94→        plan = None
    95→    print_execution_or_reveal(state, prev, plan)
    96→
    97→
    98→__all__ = [
    99→    "get_plan_start_strict",
   100→    "plan_aware_queue_count",
   101→    "print_execution_or_reveal",
   102→    "print_frozen_score_with_queue_context",
   103→    "show_score_with_plan_context",
   104→]
   105→

1→"""Plan schema types, defaults, and validation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any, Required, TypedDict
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
     8→
     9→PLAN_VERSION = 5
    10→
    11→VALID_SKIP_KINDS = {"temporary", "permanent", "false_positive", "synthesized_out"}
    12→
    13→EPIC_PREFIX = "epic/"
    14→VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS = {
    15→    "delete", "merge", "flatten", "enforce",
    16→    "simplify", "decompose", "extract", "inline",
    17→}
    18→
    19→
    20→class SkipEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    21→    finding_id: Required[str]
    22→    kind: Required[str]  # "temporary" | "permanent" | "false_positive"
    23→    reason: str | None
    24→    note: str | None  # required for permanent (wontfix note)
    25→    attestation: str | None  # required for permanent/false_positive
    26→    created_at: str
    27→    review_after: int | None  # re-surface after N scans (temporary only)
    28→    skipped_at_scan: int  # state.scan_count when skipped
    29→
    30→
    31→class ItemOverride(TypedDict, total=False):
    32→    finding_id: Required[str]
    33→    description: str | None
    34→    note: str | None
    35→    cluster: str | None
    36→    created_at: str
    37→    updated_at: str
    38→
    39→
    40→class Cluster(TypedDict, total=False):
    41→    name: Required[str]
    42→    description: str | None
    43→    finding_ids: list[str]
    44→    created_at: str
    45→    updated_at: str
    46→    auto: bool  # True for auto-generated clusters
    47→    cluster_key: str  # Deterministic grouping key (for regeneration)
    48→    action: str | None  # Primary resolution command/guidance text
    49→    user_modified: bool  # True when user manually edits membership
    50→
    51→
    52→class ExecutionLogEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    53→    timestamp: Required[str]

    55→    finding_ids: list[str]
    56→    cluster_name: str | None
    57→    actor: str  # "user" | "system" | "agent"

    60→
    61→

    63→    original_id: Required[str]
    64→    original_detector: str
    65→    original_file: str
    66→    original_summary: str
    67→    status: str  # "superseded" | "remapped" | "dismissed"
    68→    superseded_at: str
    69→    remapped_to: str | None
    70→    candidates: list[str]

    72→
    73→
    74→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
    75→    version: Required[int]
    76→    created: Required[str]
    77→    updated: Required[str]
    78→    queue_order: list[str]
    79→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
    80→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]

    86→    plan_start_scores: dict  # frozen score snapshot from plan creation cycle
    87→    execution_log: list[ExecutionLogEntry]

    89→
    90→
    91→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
    92→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
    93→    now = utc_now()
    94→    return {
    95→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
    96→        "created": now,
    97→        "updated": now,
    98→        "queue_order": [],

   112→def _ensure_container_types(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   113→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   114→        plan["queue_order"] = []
   115→    if not isinstance(plan.get("deferred"), list):
   116→        plan["deferred"] = []
   117→    if not isinstance(plan.get("skipped"), dict):
   118→        plan["skipped"] = {}
   119→    if not isinstance(plan.get("overrides"), dict):
   120→        plan["overrides"] = {}
   121→    if not isinstance(plan.get("clusters"), dict):
   122→        plan["clusters"] = {}
   123→    if not isinstance(plan.get("superseded"), dict):
   124→        plan["superseded"] = {}
   125→    if not isinstance(plan.get("promoted_ids"), list):
   126→        plan["promoted_ids"] = []
   127→    if not isinstance(plan.get("plan_start_scores"), dict):
   128→        plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   129→    if not isinstance(plan.get("execution_log"), list):
   130→        plan["execution_log"] = []
   131→    if not isinstance(plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta"), dict):
   132→        plan["epic_synthesis_meta"] = {}
   133→
   134→
   135→def _migrate_deferred_to_skipped(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   136→    deferred: list[str] = plan["deferred"]
   137→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   138→    if not deferred:
   139→        return
   140→
   141→    now = utc_now()
   142→    for fid in list(deferred):
   143→        if fid in skipped:
   144→            continue
   145→        skipped[fid] = {
   146→            "finding_id": fid,
   147→            "kind": "temporary",
   148→            "reason": None,
   149→            "note": None,
   150→            "attestation": None,
   151→            "created_at": now,
   152→            "review_after": None,
   153→            "skipped_at_scan": 0,
   154→        }
   155→    deferred.clear()
   156→
   157→
   158→def _normalize_cluster_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   159→    for cluster in plan["clusters"].values():
   160→        if not isinstance(cluster, dict):
   161→            continue
   162→        if not isinstance(cluster.get("finding_ids"), list):
   163→            cluster["finding_ids"] = []
   164→        cluster.setdefault("auto", False)
   165→        cluster.setdefault("cluster_key", "")
   166→        cluster.setdefault("action", None)
   167→        cluster.setdefault("user_modified", False)
   168→
   169→
   170→def _migrate_epics_to_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   171→    """Migrate v3 top-level ``epics`` dict into ``clusters`` (v4 unification)."""
   172→    epics = plan.pop("epics", None)
   173→    if not isinstance(epics, dict) or not epics:
   174→        return
   175→    clusters = plan["clusters"]
   176→    now = utc_now()
   177→    for name, epic in epics.items():
   178→        if not isinstance(epic, dict):
   179→            continue
   180→        if name in clusters:
   181→            continue  # don't overwrite existing cluster
   182→        clusters[name] = {
   183→            "name": name,
   184→            "description": epic.get("thesis", ""),
   185→            "finding_ids": epic.get("finding_ids", []),
   186→            "auto": True,
   187→            "cluster_key": f"epic::{name}",
   188→            "action": f"desloppify plan focus {name}",
   189→            "user_modified": False,
   190→            "created_at": epic.get("created_at", now),
   191→            "updated_at": epic.get("updated_at", now),
   192→            # Epic-specific fields
   193→            "thesis": epic.get("thesis", ""),
   194→            "direction": epic.get("direction", "simplify"),
   195→            "root_cause": epic.get("root_cause", ""),
   196→            "supersedes": epic.get("supersedes", []),
   197→            "dismissed": epic.get("dismissed", []),
   198→            "agent_safe": epic.get("agent_safe", False),
   199→            "dependency_order": epic.get("dependency_order", 999),
   200→            "action_steps": epic.get("action_steps", []),
   201→            "source_clusters": epic.get("source_clusters", []),
   202→            "status": epic.get("status", "pending"),
   203→            "synthesis_version": epic.get("synthesis_version", 0),
   204→        }
   205→
   206→
   207→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   208→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   209→
   210→    Handles migration from v1 (deferred list) to v2 (skipped dict),
   211→    and v3 (top-level epics) to v4 (epics unified into clusters).
   212→    """
   213→    defaults = empty_plan()
   214→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   215→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   216→
   217→    _ensure_container_types(plan)
   218→    _migrate_deferred_to_skipped(plan)
   219→    _migrate_epics_to_clusters(plan)
   220→    _normalize_cluster_defaults(plan)
   221→
   222→
   223→def synthesis_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
   224→    """Return clusters whose name starts with ``EPIC_PREFIX``."""
   225→    return {
   226→        name: cluster
   227→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   228→        if name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   229→    }
   230→
   231→
   232→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   233→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   234→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   235→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   236→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   237→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   238→
   239→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   240→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   241→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   242→    if overlap:
   243→        raise ValueError(
   244→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   245→        )
   246→
   247→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   248→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   249→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   250→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   251→            raise ValueError(
   252→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   253→            )
   254→
   255→
   256→__all__ = [
   257→    "EPIC_PREFIX",
   258→    "ExecutionLogEntry",
   259→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   260→    "Cluster",
   261→    "ItemOverride",
   262→    "PlanModel",
   263→    "SkipEntry",
   264→    "SupersededEntry",
   265→    "VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS",
   266→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",
   267→    "empty_plan",
   268→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   269→    "synthesis_clusters",
   270→    "validate_plan",
   271→]
   272→

    35→
    36→
    37→def _scorecard_subjective(
    38→    state: dict,
    39→    dim_scores: dict,
    40→) -> list[dict]:
    41→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    42→    return next_render_mod.scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
    43→
    44→
    45→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    46→    state: dict,
    47→    dim_scores: dict,
    48→    *,
    49→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    50→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    51→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    52→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    53→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    54→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    55→            continue
    56→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    57→        if strict_val < threshold:
    58→            low.append(
    59→                (
    60→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    61→                    strict_val,
    62→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    63→                )
    64→            )
    65→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    66→    return low
    67→
    68→
    69→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    70→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    71→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    72→    state = runtime.state
    73→    config = runtime.config
    74→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    75→        return
    76→
    77→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    78→    if stale_warning:
    79→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    80→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    81→    if skill_warning:
    82→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    83→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    84→    if config_warning:
    85→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    86→
    87→    print_synthesis_guardrail_info(state=state)
    88→    _get_items(args, state, config)
    89→
    90→
    91→def _resolve_cluster_focus(
    92→    plan_data: dict | None,
    93→    *,
    94→    cluster_arg: str | None,
    95→    scope: str | None,
    96→) -> str | None:
    97→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
    98→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
    99→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
   100→        if active_cluster:
   101→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   102→    return effective_cluster
   103→
   104→
   105→def _build_next_payload(
   106→    *,
   107→    queue: dict,
   108→    items: list[dict],
   109→    state: dict,
   110→    narrative: dict,
   111→    plan_data: dict | None,
   112→) -> dict:
   113→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   114→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
   115→    )
   116→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   117→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   118→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   119→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   120→        state,
   121→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   122→    )
   123→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   124→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   125→    ]
   126→    return payload
   127→
   128→
   129→def _emit_requested_output(
   130→    args,
   131→    payload: dict,
   132→    items: list[dict],
   133→) -> bool:
   134→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   135→    if output_file:
   136→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   137→            output_file,
   138→            payload,
   139→            len(items),
   140→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   141→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   142→        ):
   143→            return True
   144→        raise SystemExit(1)
   145→
   146→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
   147→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):
   148→        return True
   149→    return False
   150→
   151→
   152→def _plan_queue_context(
   153→    *,
   154→    state: dict,
   155→    plan_data: dict | None,
   156→) -> tuple[float | None, int]:
   157→    plan_start_strict = get_plan_start_strict(plan_data)
   158→    if plan_start_strict is None:
   159→        return None, 0
   160→    try:
   161→        queue_total = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan_data)
   162→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   163→        queue_total = 0
   164→    return plan_start_strict, queue_total
   165→
   166→
   167→def _merge_potentials_safe(raw_potentials: dict | None) -> dict | None:
   168→    try:
   169→        return merge_potentials(raw_potentials) or None
   170→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError):
   171→        return raw_potentials or None
   172→
   173→
   174→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
   175→    tier = getattr(args, "tier", None)
   176→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
   177→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
   178→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
   179→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
   180→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
   181→    no_tier_fallback = bool(getattr(args, "no_tier_fallback", False))
   182→    cluster_arg = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
   183→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
   184→
   185→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
   186→
   187→    # Load the living plan
   188→    plan = load_plan()
   189→    plan_data: dict | None = None
   190→    if (
   191→        plan.get("queue_order")
   192→        or plan.get("overrides")
   193→        or plan.get("clusters")
   194→    ):
   195→        plan_data = plan
   196→
   197→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   198→    effective_cluster = _resolve_cluster_focus(
   199→        plan_data,
   200→        cluster_arg=cluster_arg,
   201→        scope=scope,
   202→    )
   203→
   204→    queue = build_work_queue(
   205→        state,
   206→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   207→            tier=tier,
   208→            count=count,
   209→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   210→            scope=scope,
   211→            status=status,
   212→            include_subjective=True,
   213→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   214→            no_tier_fallback=no_tier_fallback,
   215→            explain=explain,
   216→            plan=plan_data,
   217→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   218→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   219→        ),
   220→    )
   221→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   222→
   223→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   224→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   225→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   226→        state,
   227→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next", plan=plan_data),
   228→    )
   229→
   230→    payload = _build_next_payload(
   231→        queue=queue,
   232→        items=items,
   233→        state=state,
   234→        narrative=narrative,
   235→        plan_data=plan_data,
   236→    )
   237→    write_query(payload)
   238→
   239→    if _emit_requested_output(args, payload, items):
   240→        return
   241→
   242→    # Extract frozen plan-start score and queue total for lifecycle display
   243→    plan_start_strict, queue_total = _plan_queue_context(
   244→        state=state,
   245→        plan_data=plan_data,
   246→    )
   247→
   248→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   249→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   250→    if next_render_mod.show_empty_queue(
   251→        queue,
   252→        tier,
   253→        strict_score,
   254→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   255→        target_strict=target_strict,
   256→    ):
   257→        return
   258→
   259→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   260→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   261→        state.get("findings", {}),
   262→        state.get("scan_path"),
   263→    )
   264→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   265→    potentials = _merge_potentials_safe(raw_potentials)
   266→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   267→        items, dim_scores, findings_scoped, group=group, explain=explain,
   268→        potentials=potentials, plan=plan_data,
   269→        cluster_filter=effective_cluster,
   270→    )
   271→    next_render_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   272→    next_render_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   273→        state,
   274→        dim_scores,
   275→        findings_scoped,
   276→        strict_score=strict_score,
   277→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   278→        queue_total=queue_total,
   279→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   280→    )
   281→    print()
   282→
   283→
   284→__all__ = ["_low_subjective_dimensions", "cmd_next"]
   285→
