```
1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    11→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    21→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    22→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    23→)
    24→
    25→
    26→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    27→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    28→    detail = item.get("detail")
    29→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    30→
    31→
    32→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    33→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    34→
    35→
    36→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    37→    detector = item.get("detector")
    38→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    39→        return True
    40→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    41→        return True
    42→    return False
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    47→
    48→
    49→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    50→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    51→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    52→    weight_by_confidence = {
    53→        "high": 1.0,
    54→        "medium": 0.7,
    55→        "low": 0.3,
    56→    }
    57→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    58→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    59→        weight *= 10.0
    60→    return float(weight)
    61→
    62→
    63→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    64→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    65→    if not scope:
    66→        return True
    67→
    68→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    69→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    70→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    71→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    72→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    73→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    74→
    75→    if "*" in scope:
    76→        return any(
    77→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    78→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    if "::" in scope:
    82→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    83→
    84→    lowered = scope.lower()
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    86→        return (
    87→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    88→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    89→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
    93→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    94→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    95→
    96→    return (
    97→        detector == scope
    98→        or filepath == scope
    99→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   100→    )
   101→
   102→
   103→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   104→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   105→
   106→
   107→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   108→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   109→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   110→    target = cleaned.lower()
   111→
   112→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   113→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   114→            return str(dim_key)
   115→    return slugify(cleaned)
   116→
   117→
   118→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   119→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   120→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   121→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   122→    return {
   123→        cleaned.lower(),
   124→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   125→        slugify(cleaned),
   126→        canonical.lower(),
   127→        slugify(canonical),
   128→    }
   129→
   130→
   131→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   132→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   133→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   134→    if not lang:
   135→        return None
   136→
   137→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   138→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   139→        return None
   140→
   141→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   142→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   143→        return None
   144→
   145→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   146→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   147→        return None
   148→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   149→
   150→
   151→def primary_command_for_finding(
   152→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   153→) -> str:
   154→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   155→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   156→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   157→        available_fixers = [
   158→            fixer
   159→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   160→            if supported_fixers is not None and fixer in supported_fixers
   161→        ]
   162→        if available_fixers:
   163→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   164→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   165→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   166→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   167→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   168→    return f'desloppify plan done "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --confirm'
   169→
   170→
   171→def build_synthesis_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> dict | None:
   172→    """Build a synthetic T1 work item for ``synthesis::pending`` if it's in the queue.
   173→
   174→    Returns ``None`` when synthesis is not pending.
   175→    """
   176→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SYNTHESIS_ID
   177→
   178→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   179→        return None
   180→
   181→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   182→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   183→
   184→    open_review_ids = {
   185→        fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   186→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   187→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   188→    }
   189→    synthesized_ids = set(meta.get("synthesized_ids", []))
   190→    new_since = len(open_review_ids - synthesized_ids)
   191→    resolved_since = len(synthesized_ids - open_review_ids)
   192→
   193→    return {
   194→        "id": SYNTHESIS_ID,
   195→        "tier": 1,
   196→        "confidence": "high",
   197→        "detector": "synthesis",
   198→        "file": ".",
   199→        "kind": "synthesis_needed",
   200→        "primary_command": "desloppify plan synthesize",
   201→        "summary": (
   202→            f"Synthesize {len(open_review_ids)} review findings into a coherent plan"
   203→        ),
   204→        "detail": {
   205→            "total_review_findings": len(open_review_ids),
   206→            "new_since_last": new_since,
   207→            "resolved_since_last": resolved_since,
   208→        },
   209→    }
   210→
   211→
   212→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   213→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   214→    if not dim_scores:
   215→        return {}
   216→
   217→    entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   218→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   219→    for entry in entries:
   220→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   221→        if not name:
   222→            continue
   223→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   224→        [REDACTED](name)
   225→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
   226→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   227→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   228→            if not key:
   229→                continue
   230→            aliases.add(key)
   231→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   232→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   233→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   234→        for alias in aliases:
   235→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   236→    return scores
   237→
   238→
   239→def build_subjective_items(
   240→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   241→) -> list[dict]:
   242→    """Create synthetic subjective work items (always tier 4)."""
   243→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   244→    if not dim_scores:
   245→        return []
   246→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   247→
   248→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   249→    if not subjective_entries:
   250→        return []
   251→    unassessed_dims = {
   252→        str(name).strip()
   253→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   254→            dim_scores
   255→        )
   256→    }
   257→
   258→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   259→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   260→    for finding in findings.values():
   261→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   262→            continue
   263→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   264→        if not dim_key:
   265→            continue
   266→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   267→
   268→    items: list[dict] = []
   269→    def _prepare_command(
   270→        cli_keys: list[str],
   271→        *,
   272→        force_review_rerun: bool = False,
   273→    ) -> str:
   274→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   275→        if cli_keys:
   276→            command += " --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   277→        if force_review_rerun:
   278→            command += " --force-review-rerun"
   279→        return command
   280→
   281→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   282→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   283→        if not name:
   284→            continue
   285→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   286→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   287→            continue
   288→
   289→        [REDACTED](name)
   290→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   291→        cli_keys = [
   292→            str(key).strip().lower()
   293→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   294→            if str(key).strip()
   295→        ]
   296→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   297→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   298→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   299→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   300→            name in unassessed_dims
   301→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   302→        )
   303→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   304→        # Items whose only action is --force-review-rerun are gated by the
   305→        # review preflight while objective backlog exists.  Flag them so the
   306→        # queue builder can suppress them until the backlog drains.
   307→        is_backlog_gated = open_review == 0 and not is_unassessed
   308→        # If review findings already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   309→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   310→        if open_review > 0:
   311→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   312→        elif is_unassessed:
   313→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   314→        elif is_stale:
   315→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   316→                cli_keys,
   317→                force_review_rerun=True,
   318→            )
   319→        elif cli_keys:
   320→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   321→                cli_keys,
   322→                force_review_rerun=True,
   323→            )
   324→        else:
   325→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   326→                cli_keys,
   327→                force_review_rerun=True,
   328→            )
   329→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   330→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   331→        items.append(
   332→            {
   333→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   334→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   335→                "file": ".",
   336→                "tier": 4,
   337→                "effective_tier": 4,
   338→                "confidence": "medium",
   339→                "summary": summary,
   340→                "detail": {
   341→                    "dimension_name": name,
   342→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   343→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   344→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   345→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   346→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   347→                },
   348→                "status": "open",
   349→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   350→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   351→                "backlog_gated": is_backlog_gated,
   352→            }
   353→        )
   354→    return items
   355→
   356→
   357→__all__ = [
   358→    "ALL_STATUSES",
   359→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   360→    "build_subjective_items",
   361→    "build_synthesis_item",
   362→    "detail_dict",
   363→    "is_review_finding",
   364→    "is_subjective_finding",
```

```
1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    detail_dict,
     7→    is_review_finding,
     8→    is_subjective_finding,
     9→    primary_command_for_finding,
    10→    review_finding_weight,
    11→    scope_matches,
    12→    slugify,
    13→    status_matches,
    14→    subjective_strict_scores,
    15→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    19→
    20→
    21→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    22→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    23→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    24→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    25→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    26→
    27→
    28→def build_finding_items(
    29→    state: dict,
    30→    *,
    31→    scan_path: str | None,
    32→    status_filter: str,
    33→    scope: str | None,
    34→    chronic: bool,
    35→) -> list[dict]:
    36→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
    37→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
    38→    out: list[dict] = []
    39→
    40→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
    41→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    42→            continue
    43→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
    44→            continue
    45→        if chronic and not (
    46→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
    47→        ):
    48→            continue
    49→
    50→        item = dict(finding)
    51→        item["id"] = finding_id
    52→        item["kind"] = "finding"
    53→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
    54→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
    55→        item["effective_tier"] = (
    56→            4 if item["is_subjective"] else int(finding.get("tier", 3))
    57→        )
    58→        item["review_weight"] = (
    59→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
    60→        )
    61→        subjective_score = None
    62→        if item["is_subjective"]:
    63→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
    64→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
    65→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
    66→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
    67→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
    68→            )
    69→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
    70→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
    71→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
    72→            item,
    73→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
    74→        )
    75→
    76→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
    77→            continue
    78→        out.append(item)
    79→
    80→    return out
    81→
    82→
    83→_CLUSTER_ACTION_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
    84→
    85→
    86→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    87→    if item.get("kind") == "synthesis_needed":
    88→        # Synthesis items always sort first — they block all other work.
    89→        return (-1, 0, 0, item.get("id", ""))
    90→
    91→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    92→        # Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type
    93→        # (auto_fix first, then reorganize, refactor, manual_fix),
    94→        # then bigger clusters first within the same action type.
    95→        action_pri = _CLUSTER_ACTION_PRIORITY.get(
    96→            item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"), 3
    97→        )
    98→        return (
    99→            0,  # All clusters before all individual findings
   100→            action_pri,
   101→            -int(item.get("member_count", 0)),
   102→            item.get("id", ""),
   103→        )
   104→
   105→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   106→        return (
   107→            int(item.get("effective_tier", 4)),
   108→            1,  # Subjective items sort after mechanical items within T4.
   109→            subjective_score_value(item),
   110→            item.get("id", ""),
   111→        )
   112→
   113→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   114→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   115→    return (
   116→        int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))),
   117→        0,
   118→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
   119→        -review_weight,
   120→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   121→        item.get("id", ""),
   122→    )
   123→
   124→
   125→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   126→    effective_tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   127→
   128→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   129→        return {
   130→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   131→            "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   132→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   133→            "policy": (
   134→                "Subjective dimensions are always queued as T4 and do not outrank "
   135→                "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   136→            ),
   137→            "ranking_factors": ["tier asc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   138→        }
   139→
   140→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   141→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   142→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   143→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   144→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   145→    if is_subjective:
   146→        ranking_factors = ["tier fixed to T4", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   147→    elif is_review:
   148→        ranking_factors = [
   149→            "tier asc",
   150→            "confidence asc",
   151→            "review_weight desc",
   152→            "count desc",
   153→            "id asc",
   154→        ]
   155→    else:
   156→        ranking_factors = ["tier asc", "confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   157→    explain = {
   158→        "kind": "finding",
   159→        "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   160→        "confidence": confidence,
   161→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   162→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   163→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   164→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   165→    }
   166→    if is_review:
   167→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   168→    if is_subjective:
   169→        explain["policy"] = (
   170→            "Subjective findings are forced to T4 and do not outrank "
   171→            "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   172→        )
   173→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   174→    return explain
   175→
   176→
   177→def tier_counts(items: list[dict]) -> dict[int, int]:
   178→    counts = {1: 0, 2: 0, 3: 0, 4: 0}
   179→    for item in items:
   180→        if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   181→            # Count each member at its own tier
   182→            for member in item.get("members", []):
   183→                tier = int(member.get("effective_tier", member.get("tier", 3)))
   184→                counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
   185→            continue
   186→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   187→        counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
   188→    return counts
   189→
   190→
   191→def choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier: int, counts: dict[int, int]) -> int | None:
   192→    available = [tier for tier, count in counts.items() if count > 0]
   193→    if not available:
   194→        return None
   195→    return min(available, key=lambda tier: (abs(tier - requested_tier), tier))
   196→
   197→
   198→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   199→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   200→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   201→    for item in items:
   202→        if group == "file":
   203→            key = item.get("file", "")
   204→        elif group == "detector":
   205→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   206→        elif group == "tier":
   207→            key = f"T{int(item.get('effective_tier', item.get('tier', 3)))}"
   208→        elif group == "cluster":
   209→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   210→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   211→        else:
   212→            key = "items"
   213→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   214→    return grouped
   215→
   216→
   217→__all__ = [
   218→    "build_finding_items",
   219→    "choose_fallback_tier",
   220→    "item_explain",
   221→    "item_sort_key",
   222→    "tier_counts",

   224→    "group_queue_items",
```

```
1→"""Scan preflight guard: warn and gate scan when queue has unfinished items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→import sys
     7→
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import plan_aware_queue_count
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    11→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    12→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    13→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan, save_plan
    14→
    15→
    16→def scan_queue_preflight(args) -> None:
    17→    """Warn and gate scan when queue has unfinished items."""
    18→    # CI profile always passes
    19→    if getattr(args, "profile", None) == "ci":
    20→        return
    21→
    22→    # --force-rescan with valid attestation bypasses
    23→    if getattr(args, "force_rescan", False):
    24→        attest = getattr(args, "attest", None) or ""
    25→        if "i understand" not in attest.lower():
    26→            print(
    27→                colorize(
    28→                    '  --force-rescan requires --attest "I understand this is not '
    29→                    "the intended workflow and I am intentionally skipping queue "
    30→                    'completion"',
    31→                    "red",
    32→                ),
    33→                file=sys.stderr,
    34→            )
    35→            sys.exit(1)
    36→        print(
    37→            colorize(
    38→                "  --force-rescan: bypassing queue completion check. "
    39→                "Plan-start score will be reset.",
    40→                "yellow",
    41→            )
    42→        )
    43→        # Clear plan_start_scores
    44→        try:
    45→            plan = load_plan()
    46→            if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    47→                plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
    48→                save_plan(plan)
    49→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
    50→            logging.debug("Plan score cleanup skipped: %s", exc)
    51→        return
    52→
    53→    # No plan = no gate (first scan, or user never uses plan)
    54→    try:
    55→        plan = load_plan()
    56→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    57→        return
    58→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    59→        return  # No active cycle
    60→
    61→    # Count plan-aware remaining items
    62→    try:
    63→        state = state_mod.load_state(state_path(args))
    64→        remaining = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan)
    65→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    66→        return
    67→    if remaining == 0:
    68→        return  # Queue clear, scan allowed
    69→
    70→    # GATE

    79→    print(

    83→        ),

    86→    print(

    93→    print(
```

```
1→"""Post-scan plan workflow nudge.
     2→
     3→Dependency-light module that only imports plan/queue helpers — avoids
     4→the import cycle that previously forced function-level imports in scan.py.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import plan_aware_queue_count
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    12→
    13→
    14→def print_plan_workflow_nudge(state: dict) -> None:
    15→    """Print a queue-count reminder when plan-start scores exist."""
    16→    try:
    17→        plan = load_plan()
    18→        if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    19→            return
    20→        queue_total = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan)
    21→    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
    22→        return
    23→
    24→    if queue_total <= 0:
    25→        return
    26→    print(
    27→        colorize(
    28→            f"  Workflow: {queue_total} queue item{'s' if queue_total != 1 else ''}."
    29→            " Score is frozen until the queue is clear — use `desloppify next` to begin.",
    30→            "dim",
    31→        )
    32→    )
    33→
```

```
165→def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(
   166→    state: state_mod.StateModel, plan: dict[str, object]
   167→) -> bool:
   168→    """Clear plan-start score snapshot once the queue is fully drained."""
   169→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   170→        return False
   171→
   172→    try:
   173→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import plan_aware_queue_count
   174→
   175→        queue_empty = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan) == 0
   176→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   177→        logging.debug("Plan operation skipped: %s", exc)
   178→        return False
   179→    if not queue_empty:
   180→        return False
   181→    state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
   182→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   183→    return True
   184→
```

```
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize, print_table
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan import compute_new_finding_ids, load_plan
    12→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    13→
    14→
    15→def _truncate(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    16→    if len(text) <= width:
    17→        return text
    18→    return text[: width - 1] + "\u2026"
    19→
    20→
    21→def _cluster_tier_label(item: dict) -> str:
    22→    """Compute a tier label from cluster members, e.g. 'T2' or 'T1-T3'."""
    23→    members = item.get("members", [])
    24→    if not members:
    25→        return ""
    26→    tiers = {int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) for m in members}
    27→    lo, hi = min(tiers), max(tiers)
    28→    if lo == hi:
    29→        return f"T{lo}"
    30→    return f"T{lo}-T{hi}"
    31→
    32→
    33→def _resolve_plan_context(plan: dict, cluster_filter: str | None) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None]:
    34→    plan_data: dict | None = None
    35→    if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("overrides") or plan.get("clusters"):
    36→        plan_data = plan
    37→
    38→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter
    39→    if plan_data and not cluster_filter:
    40→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
    41→        if active_cluster:
    42→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
    43→    return plan_data, effective_cluster
    44→
    45→
    46→def _print_queue_header(
    47→    *,
    48→    items: list[dict],
    49→    tier_counts: dict,
    50→    include_skipped: bool,
    51→    plan: dict,
    52→    plan_data: dict | None,
    53→    new_count: int = 0,
    54→) -> None:
    55→    total = len(items)
    56→    skipped_count = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("plan_skipped"))
    57→    non_skipped = total - skipped_count
    58→    tc_parts = " ".join(f"T{t}:{tier_counts.get(t, 0)}" for t in (1, 2, 3, 4))
    59→    finding_total = sum(tier_counts.get(t, 0) for t in (1, 2, 3, 4))
    60→    new_suffix = f"  ({new_count} new this scan)" if new_count > 0 else ""
    61→    if finding_total != non_skipped:
    62→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {non_skipped} entries  ({finding_total} findings: {tc_parts}){new_suffix}", "bold"))
    63→    else:
    64→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {non_skipped} items  ({tc_parts}){new_suffix}", "bold"))
    65→
    66→    focus = plan.get("active_cluster") if plan_data else None
    67→    if focus:
    68→        print(colorize(f"  Focus: {focus}", "cyan"))
    69→
    70→    if include_skipped or skipped_count != 0:
    71→        return
    72→    plan_skipped_count = len(plan.get("skipped", {})) if plan_data else 0
    73→    if not plan_skipped_count:
    74→        return
    75→    print(
    76→        colorize(
    77→            f"  ({plan_skipped_count} skipped item{'s' if plan_skipped_count != 1 else ''}"
    78→            " hidden — use --include-skipped)",
    79→            "dim",
    80→        )
    81→    )
    82→
    83→
    84→def _queue_display_items(items: list[dict], *, top: int) -> list[dict]:
    85→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
    86→        return items[:top]
    87→    return items
    88→
    89→
    90→def _build_rows(display_items: list[dict], new_ids: set[str] | None = None) -> list[list[str]]:
    91→    rows: list[list[str]] = []
    92→    _new = new_ids or set()
    93→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
    94→        pos = str(idx)
    95→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
    96→
    97→        if kind == "cluster":
    98→            tier_str = _cluster_tier_label(item)
    99→            member_count = item.get("member_count", 0)
   100→            detector = item.get("detector", "cluster")
   101→            new_in_cluster = sum(
   102→                1 for m in item.get("members", [])
   103→                if m.get("id") in _new
   104→            )
   105→            new_tag = f" (+{new_in_cluster} new)" if new_in_cluster else ""
   106→            summary = f"[{member_count} items{new_tag}] {item.get('summary', '')}"
   107→            cluster_name = item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id", ""))
   108→        else:
   109→            tier_val = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   110→            tier_str = f"T{tier_val}"
   111→            detector = item.get("detector", "")
   112→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   113→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   114→            cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "") if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else ""
   115→
   116→        prefix = "* " if item.get("id") in _new else ""
   117→        suffix = " [skip]" if item.get("plan_skipped") else ""
   118→        summary_display = _truncate(prefix + summary, 48) + suffix
   119→        rows.append([pos, tier_str, detector, summary_display, cluster_name])
   120→    return rows
   121→
   122→
   123→def cmd_plan_queue(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   124→    """Render a compact table of all upcoming queue items."""
   125→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   126→    state = runtime.state
   127→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   128→        return
   129→
   130→    top = getattr(args, "top", 30)
   131→    tier_filter = getattr(args, "tier", None)
   132→    cluster_filter = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
   133→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
   134→
   135→    plan = load_plan()
   136→    print_synthesis_guardrail_info(plan=plan, state=state)
   137→    plan_data, effective_cluster = _resolve_plan_context(plan, cluster_filter)
   138→
   139→    queue = build_work_queue(
   140→        state,
   141→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   142→            tier=tier_filter,
   143→            count=None,
   144→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   145→            status="open",
   146→            include_subjective=True,
   147→            plan=plan_data,
   148→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   149→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   150→            collapse_clusters=True,
   151→        ),
   152→    )
   153→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   154→    tier_counts = queue.get("tier_counts", {})
   155→
   156→    sort_by = getattr(args, "sort", "priority")
   157→    all_new_ids: set[str] = queue.get("new_ids", set())
   158→    # Merge review-based new finding IDs (since last synthesis)
   159→    review_new_ids = compute_new_finding_ids(plan, state)
   160→    all_new_ids = all_new_ids | review_new_ids
   161→    item_ids = {it.get("id") for it in items}
   162→    new_ids = all_new_ids & item_ids
   163→
   164→    if sort_by == "recent":
   165→        items = sorted(items, key=lambda it: it.get("first_seen", ""), reverse=True)
   166→
   167→    _print_queue_header(
   168→        items=items,
   169→        tier_counts=tier_counts,
   170→        include_skipped=include_skipped,
   171→        plan=plan,
   172→        plan_data=plan_data,
   173→        new_count=len(new_ids),
   174→    )
   175→
   176→    if not items:
   177→        print(colorize("\n  Queue is empty.", "green"))
   178→        return
   179→
   180→    # Determine which items to show
   181→    display_items = _queue_display_items(items, top=top)
   182→    headers = ["#", "Tier", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster"]
   183→    rows = _build_rows(display_items, new_ids=new_ids)
   184→
   185→    print()
   186→    widths = [4, 4, 12, 50, 16]
   187→    print_table(headers, rows, widths=widths)
   188→
   189→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   190→        remaining = len(items) - top
   191→        print(colorize(
   192→            f"\n  ... and {remaining} more (use --top 0 to show all)", "dim"
   193→        ))
   194→    print()
   195→
   196→
   197→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_queue"]
   198→
```

```
     6→def short_finding_id(fid: str) -> str:
     7→    """Extract the 8-char hash suffix from a finding ID for compact display.
     8→
     9→    Finding IDs look like ``review::.::holistic::dim::identifier::abcdef12``.
    10→    Commands accept the hash suffix as a shorthand for the full ID.
    11→    """
    12→    if "::" in fid:
    13→        suffix = fid.rsplit("::", 1)[-1]
    14→        if len(suffix) >= 8:
    15→            return suffix[:8]
    16→    return fid
    17→
```

Now I have a comprehensive understanding of the codebase. Let me present the implementation plan.

---

## Implementation Plan: Queue Headline with Plan/Cluster Breakdown

### 1. Problem Analysis

Currently, the queue headline displays a single opaque number (`Queue: 1934 items remaining`) in these locations:
- **`queue_progress.py:print_frozen_score_with_queue_context()`** (line 50) -- used by `status_cmd.py`
- **`next_parts/render.py:render_followup_nudges()`** (line 536) -- used by `next.py`
- **`scan/plan_nudge.py:print_plan_workflow_nudge()`** (line 28) -- used post-scan
- **`scan/scan_preflight.py:scan_queue_preflight()`** (line 73) -- used as a gate before scan
- **`plan/queue_render.py:_print_queue_header()`** (lines 62-64) -- `plan queue` subcommand (already has its own richer format)

The first four all use `plan_aware_queue_count()` which returns a single `int`. The goal is to provide a compact breakdown showing plan-ordered items, leaf findings, skipped, subjective, and suppressed counts.

### 2. Data Structure: `QueueBreakdown`

Create a new dataclass in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/queue_progress.py`:

```python
from dataclasses import dataclass

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class QueueBreakdown:
    """Compact breakdown of queue composition for headline display."""
    plan_items: int          # items in queue_order (minus skipped)
    queue_total: int         # total after collapse/filter (the current "total")
    open_findings: int       # raw objective finding count (before collapse/plan)
    skipped: int             # len(plan["skipped"])
    subjective_cards: int    # subjective dimension items included in queue
    suppressed: int          # backlog_gated items that were excluded
    focus_cluster: str | None  # active_cluster name, if any
    focus_cluster_count: int   # items in the focused cluster visible in queue
```

**Where each number comes from:**
- `plan_items`: `len([fid for fid in plan["queue_order"] if fid not in plan["skipped"]])` -- count of explicitly plan-ordered non-skipped IDs. This is the primary "actionable" count.
- `queue_total`: `result["total"]` from `build_work_queue()` -- the existing collapsed/filtered total.
- `open_findings`: count of items from `build_finding_items()` only (before subjective additions). This requires a second lightweight count or extracting it during the queue build. The simplest approach: count items in the returned queue that have `kind == "finding"` or `kind == "cluster"` (since clusters represent grouped findings).
- `skipped`: `len(plan.get("skipped", {}))` -- directly from plan.
- `subjective_cards`: count items in the queue with `kind == "subjective_dimension"`.
- `suppressed`: requires knowing how many backlog_gated items were excluded. This is not currently exposed by `build_work_queue`.
- `focus_cluster` / `focus_cluster_count`: from `plan["active_cluster"]` and counting queue items within that cluster.

### 3. Computing the Breakdown

**Option A (preferred): Compute from existing data without modifying `build_work_queue`.**

The breakdown can be computed entirely from:
1. The `plan` dict (for `plan_items`, `skipped`, `focus_cluster`)
2. The `WorkQueueResult` dict (for `queue_total`, tier breakdown)
3. Iterating the returned items to count subjective vs objective

For `suppressed` (backlog_gated count), we need one additional piece of data. Two approaches:

- **3a: Add `suppressed_count` to `WorkQueueResult`** -- Modify `build_work_queue()` to count the backlog-gated items it skips at line 360 and include that count in the result dict. This is minimal and clean.
- **3b: Compute from state externally** -- Build subjective items separately and count backlog_gated ones. This duplicates work.

I recommend **3a**: add a single `suppressed_count: int` field to `WorkQueueResult` and count it at the existing suppression point in `build_work_queue()`.

**New function: `plan_aware_queue_breakdown()`**

```python
def plan_aware_queue_breakdown(
    state: dict,
    plan: dict | None = None,
) -> QueueBreakdown:
    """Compute a detailed queue breakdown for headline display."""
    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue

    result = build_work_queue(
        state,
        options=QueueBuildOptions(
            status="open",
            count=None,
            plan=plan,
            collapse_clusters=True,
            include_skipped=False,
        ),
    )

    queue_total = result["total"]
    items = result.get("items", [])

    # Count subjective items in the final queue
    subjective_cards = sum(
        1 for it in items
        if it.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or it.get("is_subjective")
    )

    # Count open findings = total minus subjective
    open_findings = queue_total - subjective_cards

    # Plan-derived counts
    if plan:
        skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
        plan_ordered = [
            fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
            if fid not in skipped_ids
        ]
        plan_items = len(plan_ordered)
        skipped = len(plan.get("skipped", {}))
        focus_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    else:
        plan_items = 0
        skipped = 0
        focus_cluster = None

    # Focus cluster count
    focus_cluster_count = 0
    if focus_cluster and plan:
        cluster_data = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(focus_cluster, {})
        cluster_fids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
        focus_cluster_count = sum(
            1 for it in items if it.get("id") in cluster_fids
        )

    suppressed = result.get("suppressed_count", 0)

    return QueueBreakdown(
        plan_items=plan_items,
        queue_total=queue_total,
        open_findings=open_findings,
        skipped=skipped,
        subjective_cards=subjective_cards,
        suppressed=suppressed,
        focus_cluster=focus_cluster,
        focus_cluster_count=focus_cluster_count,
    )
```

### 4. Backward Compatibility for `plan_aware_queue_count()`

Keep `plan_aware_queue_count()` unchanged -- it continues to return `int`. Callers that only need a count (scan_preflight, scan_workflow) keep working. Only callers that want the rich headline switch to the new `plan_aware_queue_breakdown()`.

### 5. Rendering Format

**New function: `format_queue_headline()`** in `queue_progress.py`:

```python
def format_queue_headline(breakdown: QueueBreakdown) -> str:
    """Format a compact queue headline string from a breakdown."""
    if breakdown.focus_cluster:
        # Focus cluster variant
        parts = [f"plan total: {breakdown.plan_items}"]
        parts.append(f"leaf: {breakdown.queue_total}")
        if breakdown.skipped:
            parts.append(f"skipped: {breakdown.skipped}")
        bracket = " | ".join(parts)
        return (
            f"Queue: {breakdown.focus_cluster_count} in focus cluster"
            f" `{breakdown.focus_cluster}` [{bracket}]"
        )

    if breakdown.plan_items > 0:
        # Plan-active variant
        parts = []
        parts.append(f"leaf: {breakdown.queue_total}")
        if breakdown.open_findings != breakdown.queue_total:
            parts.append(f"findings: {breakdown.open_findings}")
        if breakdown.skipped:
            parts.append(f"skipped: {breakdown.skipped}")
        if breakdown.subjective_cards:
            parts.append(f"subjective: {breakdown.subjective_cards}")
        if breakdown.suppressed:
            parts.append(f"suppressed: {breakdown.suppressed}")
        bracket = " | ".join(parts)
        return f"Queue: {breakdown.plan_items} plan items [{bracket}]"

    # No plan -- simple fallback
    n = breakdown.queue_total
    return f"Queue: {n} item{'s' if n != 1 else ''} remaining"
```

### 6. Updating Rendering Locations

#### Location 1: `queue_progress.py:print_frozen_score_with_queue_context()`

Change the signature to accept `QueueBreakdown` instead of (or in addition to) `queue_remaining: int`:

```python
def print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
    plan: dict,
    queue_remaining: int | QueueBreakdown,
) -> None:
```

If it receives a `QueueBreakdown`, use `format_queue_headline()`; if it receives an `int`, use the old format. This preserves backward compatibility while allowing callers to opt in.

**Caller update in `status_cmd.py`** (line 117-122): Replace the call to `plan_aware_queue_count()` with `plan_aware_queue_breakdown()` and pass the breakdown object.

**Caller update in `print_execution_or_reveal()`** (line 70): Similarly switch to breakdown.

#### Location 2: `next_parts/render.py:render_followup_nudges()`

Change `queue_total: int = 0` parameter to `queue_breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None = None`.

Use `format_queue_headline()` from `queue_progress.py` for the display. The `queue_total > 10` check at line 591 becomes `breakdown.queue_total > 10` (or a method on the dataclass).

**Caller update in `next.py`** (line 243-280): Replace `_plan_queue_context()` to return `QueueBreakdown | None` instead of `tuple[float | None, int]`. Update `render_followup_nudges()` call.

#### Location 3: `scan/plan_nudge.py:print_plan_workflow_nudge()`

Switch from `plan_aware_queue_count()` to `plan_aware_queue_breakdown()` and use `format_queue_headline()`.

#### Location 4: `scan/scan_preflight.py:scan_queue_preflight()`

This uses `remaining` as a gating number. Keep using `plan_aware_queue_count()` here -- it only needs the int for the `== 0` check and the warning message. Optionally switch to breakdown for a richer warning, but this is lower priority.

#### Location 5: `plan/queue_render.py:_print_queue_header()`

This already has its own richer format. It could adopt `format_queue_headline()` for consistency, or stay independent since `plan queue` is a detailed view.

### 7. Changes to `build_work_queue` in `core.py`

Minimal change: track suppressed (backlog_gated) count.

```python
# In build_work_queue(), around line 344-362:
suppressed_count = 0
# ... in the subjective loop:
if has_objective_backlog and item.get("backlog_gated"):
    suppressed_count += 1
    continue
all_items.append(item)

# ... in the return dict (line 445):
return {
    ...
    "suppressed_count": suppressed_count,
}
```

Also update `WorkQueueResult` TypedDict to include `suppressed_count: int`.

### 8. Edge Cases

| Scenario | Behavior |
|----------|----------|
| No plan exists | `plan_items=0`, falls through to simple format: `Queue: N items remaining` |
| Plan exists, no `queue_order` | `plan_items=0`, same simple fallback |
| Plan with `queue_order` but no clusters | Standard plan format without focus cluster variant |
| Active cluster with 0 visible items | `Queue: 0 in focus cluster \`name\` [plan total: N | ...]` |
| All items skipped | `plan_items=0`, shows `Queue: 0 plan items [leaf: N | skipped: M]` |
| `plan_aware_queue_count` called by old callers | Unchanged behavior, returns `int` |

### 10. Test Strategy

1. **Unit tests for `QueueBreakdown` + `format_queue_headline()`**:
   - No plan: outputs `Queue: N items remaining`
   - Plan active, no focus: outputs `Queue: M plan items [leaf: N | skipped: K | ...]`
   - Plan active with focus cluster: outputs `Queue: X in focus cluster \`name\` [...]`
   - Zero suppressed/subjective omits those fields from bracket
   - Singular/plural handling

2. **Unit tests for `plan_aware_queue_breakdown()`**:
   - Mock `build_work_queue` to return known items; verify breakdown fields computed correctly
   - Verify backward compat: `plan_aware_queue_count()` unchanged

3. **Integration tests for rendering**:
   - `print_frozen_score_with_queue_context()` with `QueueBreakdown` input: verify output contains breakdown terms
   - `render_followup_nudges()` with breakdown: verify headline appears
   - Regression: old `int` path still works for callers not yet migrated

4. **Edge case tests**:
   - Empty plan dict
   - Plan with `queue_order` but all IDs skipped
   - Active cluster with no matching items in queue

2. Add `QueueBreakdown` dataclass and `format_queue_headline()` in `queue_progress.py`
3. Add `plan_aware_queue_breakdown()` in `queue_progress.py`
4. Update `print_frozen_score_with_queue_context()` to accept breakdown
5. Update `status_cmd.py` to use breakdown
6. Update `next.py` and `render_followup_nudges()` to use breakdown
7. Update `plan_nudge.py` to use breakdown
8. Write tests
9. Run full test suite to verify no regressions

```
1→"""Plan-aware queue progress and frozen score display helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
     6→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     7→
     8→
     9→def plan_aware_queue_count(state: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> int:
    10→    """Count remaining plan-aware queue items (skips excluded, clusters collapsed)."""
    11→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    12→
    13→    result = build_work_queue(
    14→        state,
    15→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    16→            status="open",
    17→            count=None,
    18→            plan=plan,
    19→            collapse_clusters=True,
    20→            include_skipped=False,
    21→        ),
    22→    )
    23→    return result["total"]
    24→
    25→
    26→def get_plan_start_strict(plan: dict | None) -> float | None:
    27→    """Extract the frozen plan-start strict score, or None if unset."""
    28→    if not plan:
    29→        return None
    30→    return plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
    31→
    32→
    33→def print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
    34→    plan: dict,
    35→    queue_remaining: int,
    36→) -> None:
    37→    """Show frozen plan-start score + queue progress."""
    38→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
    39→    strict = scores.get("strict")
    40→    if strict is None:
    41→        return
    42→    print(
    43→        colorize(
    44→            f"\n  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {strict:.1f}/100",
    45→            "cyan",
    46→        )
    47→    )
    48→    print(
    49→        colorize(
    50→            f"  Queue: {queue_remaining} item{'s' if queue_remaining != 1 else ''}"
    51→            " remaining. Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
    52→            "dim",
    53→        )
    54→    )
    55→
    56→
    57→def print_execution_or_reveal(
    58→    state: dict,
    59→    prev,
    60→    plan: dict | None,
    61→) -> None:
    62→    """Context-aware score display: frozen plan-start score or live scores.
    63→
    64→    If a plan has ``plan_start_scores`` and there are queue items remaining,
    65→    show the frozen score + queue progress.  Otherwise delegate to the
    66→    existing live ``print_score_update``.
    67→    """
    68→    if plan and plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict") is not None:
    69→        try:
    70→            remaining = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan)
    71→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    72→            remaining = 0
    73→        if remaining > 0:
    74→            print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(plan, remaining)
    75→            return
    76→
    77→    # No active plan cycle or queue is clear — live scores
    78→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import print_score_update
    79→
    80→    print_score_update(state, prev)
    81→
    82→
    83→def show_score_with_plan_context(state: dict, prev) -> None:
    84→    """Load plan (best-effort) and show frozen or live score context.
    85→
    86→    Encapsulates the common load_plan + PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS + reveal
    87→    choreography so command modules don't each repeat it.
    88→    """
    89→    from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    90→
    91→    try:
    92→        plan = load_plan()
    93→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    94→        plan = None
    95→    print_execution_or_reveal(state, prev, plan)
    96→
    97→
    98→__all__ = [
    99→    "get_plan_start_strict",
   100→    "plan_aware_queue_count",
   101→    "print_execution_or_reveal",
   102→    "print_frozen_score_with_queue_context",
   103→    "show_score_with_plan_context",
   104→]
   105→
```

```
1→"""Reusable CLI rendering snippets for command modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→
    10→
    11→def print_agent_plan(
    12→    steps: list[str],
    13→    *,
    14→    next_command: str | None = None,
    15→    header: str = "  AGENT PLAN:",
    16→    plan: dict | None = None,
    17→) -> None:
    18→    """Print a consistent AGENT PLAN block with numbered steps.
    19→
    20→    When a living *plan* is active, renders plan focus/progress instead
    21→    of the narrative-derived steps.
    22→    """
    23→    if plan and (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters")):
    24→        _print_plan_agent_block(plan, header=header)
    25→        return
    26→    if not steps:
    27→        return
    28→    print(colorize(header, "yellow"))
    29→    for idx, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
    30→        print(colorize(f"  {idx}. {step}", "dim"))
    31→    if next_command:
    32→        print(colorize(f"  Next command: `{next_command}`", "dim"))
    33→
    34→
    35→def _print_plan_agent_block(plan: dict, *, header: str = "  AGENT PLAN:") -> None:
    36→    """Render the living plan as the agent plan block."""
    37→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    38→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    39→    skipped = len(plan.get("skipped", {}))
    40→
    41→    print(colorize(header, "yellow"))
    42→    print(colorize(f"  Living plan active: {ordered} ordered, {skipped} skipped.", "dim"))
    43→    if active:
    44→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active, {})
    45→        remaining = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    46→        print(colorize(f"  Focused on: {active} ({remaining} items remaining).", "dim"))
    47→    print(colorize("  Next command: `desloppify next`", "dim"))
    48→    print(colorize("  View plan: `desloppify plan`", "dim"))
    49→
    50→
    51→def print_replacement_groups(
    52→    groups: dict[str, list[tuple[str, str]]],
    53→    *,
    54→    title: str,
    55→    rel_fn: Callable[[str], str] = rel,
    56→) -> None:
    57→    """Print grouped old→new replacement lines by file."""
    58→    if not groups:
    59→        return
    60→    print(colorize(title, "cyan"))
    61→    for filepath, replacements in sorted(groups.items()):
    62→        print(f"    {rel_fn(filepath)}:")
    63→        for old, new in replacements:
    64→            print(f"      {old}  →  {new}")
    65→    print()
    66→
    67→
    68→def print_ranked_actions(
    69→    actions: list[dict], *, limit: int = 3, plan: dict | None = None
    70→) -> bool:
    71→    """Print the highest-impact narrative actions and return True when shown."""
    72→    ranked = sorted(
    73→        [action for action in actions if int(action.get("count", 0)) > 0],
    74→        key=lambda action: (
    75→            -float(action.get("impact", 0.0)),
    76→            -int(action.get("count", 0)),
    77→            int(action.get("priority", 999)),
    78→        ),
    79→    )
    80→    if not ranked:
    81→        return False
    82→    has_plan = plan and (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters"))
    83→    label = "Score context:" if has_plan else "Biggest things impacting score:"
    84→    print(colorize(f"  {label}", "cyan"))
    85→    for action in ranked[:limit]:
    86→        detector = action.get("detector", "unknown")
    87→        count = int(action.get("count", 0))
    88→        cluster_count = action.get("cluster_count")
    89→        if cluster_count:
    90→            print(colorize(f"    - {detector}: {count} open in {cluster_count} cluster(s) — `desloppify next`", "dim"))
    91→        else:
    92→            command = action.get("command", "desloppify next")
    93→            print(colorize(f"    - {detector}: {count} open — `{command}`", "dim"))
    94→    return True
    95→
    96→
    97→__all__ = [
    98→    "print_agent_plan",
    99→    "print_ranked_actions",
   100→    "print_replacement_groups",
   101→]
   102→
```

```
1→"""Compact queue table renderer for ``plan queue``."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.guardrails import print_synthesis_guardrail_info
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize, print_table
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan import compute_new_finding_ids, load_plan
    12→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    13→
    14→
    15→def _truncate(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    16→    if len(text) <= width:
    17→        return text
    18→    return text[: width - 1] + "\u2026"
    19→
    20→
    21→def _cluster_tier_label(item: dict) -> str:
    22→    """Compute a tier label from cluster members, e.g. 'T2' or 'T1-T3'."""
    23→    members = item.get("members", [])
    24→    if not members:
    25→        return ""
    26→    tiers = {int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) for m in members}
    27→    lo, hi = min(tiers), max(tiers)
    28→    if lo == hi:
    29→        return f"T{lo}"
    30→    return f"T{lo}-T{hi}"
    31→
    32→
    33→def _resolve_plan_context(plan: dict, cluster_filter: str | None) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None]:
    34→    plan_data: dict | None = None
    35→    if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("overrides") or plan.get("clusters"):
    36→        plan_data = plan
    37→
    38→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter
    39→    if plan_data and not cluster_filter:
    40→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
    41→        if active_cluster:
    42→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
    43→    return plan_data, effective_cluster
    44→
    45→
    46→def _print_queue_header(
    47→    *,
    48→    items: list[dict],
    49→    tier_counts: dict,
    50→    include_skipped: bool,
    51→    plan: dict,
    52→    plan_data: dict | None,
    53→    new_count: int = 0,
    54→) -> None:
    55→    total = len(items)
    56→    skipped_count = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("plan_skipped"))
    57→    non_skipped = total - skipped_count
    58→    tc_parts = " ".join(f"T{t}:{tier_counts.get(t, 0)}" for t in (1, 2, 3, 4))
    59→    finding_total = sum(tier_counts.get(t, 0) for t in (1, 2, 3, 4))
    60→    new_suffix = f"  ({new_count} new this scan)" if new_count > 0 else ""
    61→    if finding_total != non_skipped:
    62→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {non_skipped} entries  ({finding_total} findings: {tc_parts}){new_suffix}", "bold"))
    63→    else:
    64→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {non_skipped} items  ({tc_parts}){new_suffix}", "bold"))
    65→
    66→    focus = plan.get("active_cluster") if plan_data else None
    67→    if focus:
    68→        print(colorize(f"  Focus: {focus}", "cyan"))
    69→
    70→    if include_skipped or skipped_count != 0:
    71→        return
    72→    plan_skipped_count = len(plan.get("skipped", {})) if plan_data else 0
    73→    if not plan_skipped_count:
    74→        return
    75→    print(
    76→        colorize(
    77→            f"  ({plan_skipped_count} skipped item{'s' if plan_skipped_count != 1 else ''}"
    78→            " hidden — use --include-skipped)",
    79→            "dim",
    80→        )
    81→    )
    82→
    83→
    84→def _queue_display_items(items: list[dict], *, top: int) -> list[dict]:
    85→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
    86→        return items[:top]
    87→    return items
    88→
    89→
    90→def _build_rows(display_items: list[dict], new_ids: set[str] | None = None) -> list[list[str]]:
    91→    rows: list[list[str]] = []
    92→    _new = new_ids or set()
    93→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
    94→        pos = str(idx)
    95→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
    96→
    97→        if kind == "cluster":
    98→            tier_str = _cluster_tier_label(item)
    99→            member_count = item.get("member_count", 0)
   100→            detector = item.get("detector", "cluster")
   101→            new_in_cluster = sum(
   102→                1 for m in item.get("members", [])
   103→                if m.get("id") in _new
   104→            )
   105→            new_tag = f" (+{new_in_cluster} new)" if new_in_cluster else ""
   106→            summary = f"[{member_count} items{new_tag}] {item.get('summary', '')}"
   107→            cluster_name = item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id", ""))
   108→        else:
   109→            tier_val = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   110→            tier_str = f"T{tier_val}"
   111→            detector = item.get("detector", "")
   112→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   113→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   114→            cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "") if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else ""
   115→
   116→        prefix = "* " if item.get("id") in _new else ""
   117→        suffix = " [skip]" if item.get("plan_skipped") else ""
   118→        summary_display = _truncate(prefix + summary, 48) + suffix
   119→        rows.append([pos, tier_str, detector, summary_display, cluster_name])
   120→    return rows
   121→
   122→
   123→def cmd_plan_queue(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   124→    """Render a compact table of all upcoming queue items."""
   125→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   126→    state = runtime.state
   127→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   128→        return
   129→
   130→    top = getattr(args, "top", 30)
   131→    tier_filter = getattr(args, "tier", None)
   132→    cluster_filter = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
   133→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
   134→
   135→    plan = load_plan()
   136→    print_synthesis_guardrail_info(plan=plan, state=state)
   137→    plan_data, effective_cluster = _resolve_plan_context(plan, cluster_filter)
   138→
   139→    queue = build_work_queue(
   140→        state,
   141→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   142→            tier=tier_filter,
   143→            count=None,
   144→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   145→            status="open",
   146→            include_subjective=True,
   147→            plan=plan_data,
   148→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   149→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   150→            collapse_clusters=True,
   151→        ),
   152→    )
   153→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   154→    tier_counts = queue.get("tier_counts", {})
   155→
   156→    sort_by = getattr(args, "sort", "priority")
   157→    all_new_ids: set[str] = queue.get("new_ids", set())
   158→    # Merge review-based new finding IDs (since last synthesis)
   159→    review_new_ids = compute_new_finding_ids(plan, state)
   160→    all_new_ids = all_new_ids | review_new_ids
   161→    item_ids = {it.get("id") for it in items}
   162→    new_ids = all_new_ids & item_ids
   163→
   164→    if sort_by == "recent":
   165→        items = sorted(items, key=lambda it: it.get("first_seen", ""), reverse=True)
   166→
   167→    _print_queue_header(
   168→        items=items,
   169→        tier_counts=tier_counts,
   170→        include_skipped=include_skipped,
   171→        plan=plan,
   172→        plan_data=plan_data,
   173→        new_count=len(new_ids),
   174→    )
   175→
   176→    if not items:
   177→        print(colorize("\n  Queue is empty.", "green"))
   178→        return
   179→
   180→    # Determine which items to show
   181→    display_items = _queue_display_items(items, top=top)
   182→    headers = ["#", "Tier", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster"]
   183→    rows = _build_rows(display_items, new_ids=new_ids)
   184→
   185→    print()
   186→    widths = [4, 4, 12, 50, 16]
   187→    print_table(headers, rows, widths=widths)
   188→
   189→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   190→        remaining = len(items) - top
   191→        print(colorize(
   192→            f"\n  ... and {remaining} more (use --top 0 to show all)", "dim"
   193→        ))
   194→    print()
   195→
   196→
   197→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_queue"]
   198→
```

```
     2→

     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.rendering import print_agent_plan, print_ranked_actions
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.subjective import print_subjective_followup
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.scan import (

    25→from desloppify.core.registry import dimension_action_type
    26→from desloppify.scoring import (

    32→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize, print_table
    33→
    34→
    35→def show_tier_progress_table(by_tier: dict) -> None:
    36→    """Fallback display when dimension scores are unavailable."""
    37→    rows = []
    38→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
    39→        ts = by_tier.get(str(tier_num), {})
    40→        t_open = ts.get("open", 0)
    41→        t_fixed = ts.get("fixed", 0) + ts.get("auto_resolved", 0)
    42→        t_fp = ts.get("false_positive", 0)
    43→        t_wontfix = ts.get("wontfix", 0)
    44→        t_total = sum(ts.values())
    45→        strict_pct = round((t_fixed + t_fp) / t_total * 100) if t_total else 100
    46→        bar_len = 20
    47→        filled = round(strict_pct / 100 * bar_len)
    48→        bar = colorize("█" * filled, "green") + colorize(
    49→            "░" * (bar_len - filled), "dim"
    50→        )
    51→        rows.append(
    52→            [
    53→                f"Tier {tier_num}",
    54→                bar,
    55→                f"{strict_pct}%",
    56→                str(t_open),
    57→                str(t_fixed),
    58→                str(t_wontfix),
    59→            ]
    60→        )
    61→    print_table(
    62→        ["Tier", "Strict Progress", "%", "Open", "Fixed", "Debt"],
    63→        rows,
    64→        [40, 22, 5, 6, 6, 6],
    65→    )
    66→
    67→
    68→def _status_next_command(narrative: dict) -> str:
    69→    actions = narrative.get("actions", [])
    70→    return actions[0]["command"] if actions else "desloppify next --count 20"
    71→
    72→
    73→def write_status_query(
    74→    *,
    75→    state: dict,
    76→    stats: dict,
    77→    by_tier: dict,
    78→    dim_scores: dict,
    79→    scorecard_dims: list[dict],
    80→    subjective_measures: list[dict],
    81→    suppression: dict,
    82→    narrative: dict,
    83→    ignores: list[str],
    84→    overall_score: float | None,
    85→    objective_score: float | None,
    86→    strict_score: float | None,
    87→    verified_strict_score: float | None,
    88→    plan: dict | None = None,
    89→) -> None:
    90→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    91→    open_scope = (
    92→        state_mod.open_scope_breakdown(findings, state.get("scan_path"))
    93→        if isinstance(findings, dict)
    94→        else None
    95→    )
    96→    write_query(
    97→        {
    98→            "command": "status",
    99→            "overall_score": overall_score,
   100→            "objective_score": objective_score,
   101→            "strict_score": strict_score,
   102→            "verified_strict_score": verified_strict_score,
   103→            "dimension_scores": dim_scores,
   104→            "scorecard_dimensions": scorecard_dims,
   105→            "subjective_measures": subjective_measures,
   106→            "stats": stats,
   107→            "scan_count": state.get("scan_count", 0),
   108→            "last_scan": state.get("last_scan"),
   109→            "by_tier": by_tier,
   110→            "ignores": ignores,
   111→            "suppression": suppression,

   116→            "next_command": _status_next_command(narrative),

   118→            **({"plan": {
   119→                "active": True,

   122→                "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
   123→                "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
   124→            }} if plan and (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters") or plan.get("skipped")) else {}),
   125→        }
   126→    )

   129→def show_ignore_summary(ignores: list[str], suppression: dict) -> None:
   130→    """Show ignore list plus suppression accountability from recent scans."""
   131→    print(colorize(f"\n  Ignore list ({len(ignores)}):", "dim"))
   132→    for p in ignores[:10]:
   133→        print(colorize(f"    {p}", "dim"))

   137→    last_pct = float(suppression.get("last_suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   138→
   139→    if last_raw > 0:
   140→        style = "red" if last_pct >= 30 else "yellow" if last_pct >= 10 else "dim"
   141→        print(
   142→            colorize(
   143→                f"  Ignore suppression (last scan): {last_ignored}/{last_raw} findings hidden ({last_pct:.1f}%)",
   144→                style,
   145→            )
   146→        )
   147→    elif suppression.get("recent_scans", 0):
   148→        print(colorize("  Ignore suppression (last scan): 0 findings hidden", "dim"))
   149→
   150→    recent_scans = int(suppression.get("recent_scans", 0) or 0)
   151→    recent_raw = int(suppression.get("recent_raw_findings", 0) or 0)
   152→    if recent_scans > 1 and recent_raw > 0:
   153→        recent_ignored = int(suppression.get("recent_ignored", 0) or 0)
   154→        recent_pct = float(suppression.get("recent_suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   155→        print(
   156→            colorize(
   157→                f"    Recent ({recent_scans} scans): {recent_ignored}/{recent_raw} findings hidden ({recent_pct:.1f}%)",
   158→                "dim",
   159→            )
   160→        )

   163→def _scorecard_subjective_entries(state: dict, dim_scores: dict) -> list[dict]:
   164→    """Return subjective entries aligned to scorecard labels and ordering."""
   165→    return scorecard_subjective_entries(
   166→        state,
   167→        dim_scores=dim_scores,

   170→
   171→def _find_lowest_dimension(
   172→    dim_scores: dict,
   173→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
   174→) -> str | None:
   175→    """Return the name of the dimension with the lowest strict score."""
   176→    lowest_name = None
   177→    lowest_score = 101.0
   178→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   179→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   180→        if not ds:
   181→            continue
   182→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", ds["score"])
   183→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
   184→            lowest_score = strict_val
   185→            lowest_name = dim.name
   186→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   187→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   188→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
   189→            lowest_score = strict_val
   190→            lowest_name = entry.get("name")
   191→    return lowest_name
   192→
   193→
   194→def _open_review_issue_counts(state: dict) -> dict[str, int]:
   195→    """Count open review findings grouped by subjective dimension key."""
   196→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   197→    if not isinstance(findings, dict):
   198→        return {}
   199→
   200→    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
   201→    for finding in findings.values():
   202→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   203→            continue
   204→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   205→            continue
   206→        detail = finding.get("detail", {})
   207→        dimension = ""
   208→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
   209→            dimension = str(detail.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   210→        if not dimension:
   211→            dimension = str(finding.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   212→        if not dimension:
   213→            continue
   214→        counts[dimension] = counts.get(dimension, 0) + 1
   215→    return counts
   216→
   217→
   218→def _render_objective_dimensions(
   219→    dim_scores: dict, *, lowest_name: str | None, bar_len: int
   220→) -> None:
   221→    """Print rows for objective (detector-based) dimensions."""
   222→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   223→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   224→        if not ds:
   225→            continue
   226→        score_val = ds["score"]
   227→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   228→        checks = ds["checks"]
   229→
   230→        bar = dimension_bar(score_val, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
   231→
   232→        focus = colorize(" ←", "yellow") if dim.name == lowest_name else "  "
   233→        checks_str = f"{checks:>7,}"
   234→        action = dimension_action_type(dim.name)
   235→        print(
   236→            f"  {dim.name:<22} {checks_str}  {score_val:5.1f}%  {strict_val:5.1f}%  {bar}  T{dim.tier}  {action}{focus}"
   237→        )
   238→
   239→
   240→def _render_subjective_dimensions(
   241→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
   242→    *,
   243→    lowest_name: str | None,
   244→    bar_len: int,
   245→    review_issue_counts: dict[str, int],
   246→) -> None:
   247→    """Print rows for subjective (review-based) dimensions."""
   248→    if not scorecard_subjective:
   249→        return
   250→    print(
   251→        colorize(
   252→            "  ── Subjective Measures (matches scorecard.png) ──────────────────────",
   253→            "dim",
   254→        )
   255→    )
   256→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   257→        name = str(entry.get("name", "Unknown"))
   258→        score_val = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
   259→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", score_val))
   260→        tier = 4
   261→
   262→        bar = dimension_bar(score_val, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
   263→
   264→        focus = colorize(" ←", "yellow") if name == lowest_name else "  "
   265→        checks_str = f"{'—':>7}"
   266→        stale_tag = colorize(" [stale]", "yellow") if entry.get("stale") else ""
   267→        placeholder_tag = (
   268→            colorize(" [unassessed]", "yellow") if entry.get("placeholder") else ""
   269→        )
   270→        dim_key = str(entry.get("dimension_key", "")).strip()
   271→        cli_keys = [
   272→            str(key).strip()
   273→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   274→            if isinstance(key, str) and str(key).strip()
   275→        ]
   276→        if dim_key:
   277→            issue_count = int(review_issue_counts.get(dim_key, 0))
   278→        elif cli_keys:
   279→            issue_count = int(sum(review_issue_counts.get(key, 0) for key in cli_keys))
   280→        else:
   281→            issue_count = 0
   282→        issue_style = "yellow" if strict_val < 95.0 and issue_count == 0 else "dim"
   283→        issue_tag = colorize(f" [open issues: {issue_count}]", issue_style)
   284→        print(
   285→            f"  {name:<22} {checks_str}  {score_val:5.1f}%  {strict_val:5.1f}%  {bar}  T{tier}  {'review'}{focus}{stale_tag}"
   286→            f"{placeholder_tag}{issue_tag}"
   287→        )
   288→
   289→
   290→def _render_dimension_legend(scorecard_subjective: list[dict], state: dict | None = None) -> None:
   291→    """Print the legend footer and stale-dimension re-review hint."""
   292→    print(
   293→        colorize("  Health = open penalized | Strict = open + wontfix penalized", "dim")
   294→    )
   295→    print(
   296→        colorize(
   297→            "  Action: fix=auto-fixer | move=reorganize | refactor=manual rewrite | manual=review & fix",
   298→            "dim",
   299→        )
   300→    )
   301→    stale_keys = [
   302→        str(e.get("dimension_key"))
   303→        for e in scorecard_subjective
   304→        if e.get("stale") and e.get("dimension_key")
   305→    ]
   306→    if stale_keys:
   307→        n = len(stale_keys)
   308→        dims_arg = ",".join(stale_keys)
   309→        print(
   310→            colorize(
   311→                f"  [stale] = subjective assessment outdated, not an unresolved finding"
   312→                f" — re-review to refresh",
   313→                "yellow",
   314→            )
   315→        )
   316→        print(
   317→            colorize(
   318→                f"  {n} stale dimension{'s' if n != 1 else ''}"
   319→                f": `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {dims_arg} --force-review-rerun`",
   320→                "yellow",
   321→            )
   322→        )
   323→
   324→
   325→def show_dimension_table(state: dict, dim_scores: dict) -> None:
   326→    """Show dimension health table with dual scores and progress bars."""
   327→    print()
   328→    bar_len = 20
   329→    print(
   330→        colorize(
   331→            f"  {'Dimension':<22} {'Checks':>7}  {'Health':>6}  {'Strict':>6}  {'Bar':<{bar_len + 2}} {'Tier'}  {'Action'}",
   332→            "dim",

   334→    )

   336→

   339→    review_issue_counts = _open_review_issue_counts(state)
   340→
   341→    _render_objective_dimensions(dim_scores, lowest_name=lowest_name, bar_len=bar_len)
   342→    _render_subjective_dimensions(
   343→        scorecard_subjective,
   344→        lowest_name=lowest_name,
   345→        bar_len=bar_len,
   346→        review_issue_counts=review_issue_counts,
   347→    )
   348→    _render_dimension_legend(scorecard_subjective, state=state)
   349→    print()
```
