```
   352→def show_focus_suggestion(
   353→    dim_scores: dict, state: dict, *, plan: dict | None = None
   354→) -> None:
   355→    """Show the lowest-scoring dimension as the focus area."""
   356→    # When plan has an active focus cluster, show that instead
   357→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   358→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   359→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name, {})
   360→        remaining = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   361→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   362→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   363→        print(
   364→            colorize(
   365→                f"  Focus: {cluster_name} ({remaining} items remaining){desc_str}",
   366→                "cyan",
   367→            )
   368→        )
   369→        print()
   370→        return
   371→
   372→    scorecard_subjective = _scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores)
   373→    lowest_name = _find_lowest_dimension(dim_scores, scorecard_subjective)
   374→    if not lowest_name:
   375→        return
   376→
   377→    # Determine kind, score, and issue count from the resolved name
   378→    lowest_kind = None
   379→    lowest_score = 101.0
   380→    lowest_issues = 0
   381→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   382→        if dim.name == lowest_name:
   383→            ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   384→            if ds:
   385→                lowest_score = float(ds.get("strict", ds["score"]))
   386→                lowest_kind = "mechanical"
   387→                lowest_issues = int(ds.get("issues", 0))
   388→            break
   389→    else:
   390→        for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   391→            if entry.get("name") == lowest_name:
   392→                lowest_score = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   393→                lowest_kind = "subjective"
   394→                lowest_issues = 0
   395→                break
   396→
   397→    if lowest_name and lowest_score < 100:
   398→        if lowest_kind == "subjective":
   399→            print(
   400→                colorize(
   401→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — re-review to improve",
   402→                    "cyan",
   403→                )
   404→            )
   405→            print()
   406→            return
   407→
   408→        potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
   409→        target_dim = next((d for d in DIMENSIONS if d.name == lowest_name), None)
   410→        if target_dim:
   411→            impact = 0.0
   412→            for det in target_dim.detectors:
   413→                impact = compute_score_impact(
   414→                    {
   415→                        k: {
   416→                            "score": v["score"],
   417→                            "tier": v.get("tier", 3),
   418→                            "detectors": v.get("detectors", {}),
   419→                        }
   420→                        for k, v in dim_scores.items()
   421→                        if "score" in v
   422→                    },
   423→                    potentials,
   424→                    det,
   425→                    lowest_issues,
   426→                )
   427→                if impact > 0:
   428→                    break
   429→
   430→            impact_str = f" for +{impact:.1f} pts" if impact > 0 else ""
   431→            print(
   432→                colorize(
   433→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — "
   434→                    f"fix {lowest_issues} items{impact_str}",
   435→                    "cyan",
   436→                )
   437→            )
   438→            print()
   439→

   493→def _collect_structural_areas(
   494→    state: dict,
   495→) -> list[tuple[str, list]] | None:
   496→    """Collect T3/T4 structural findings grouped by area.
   497→
   498→    Returns sorted area list, or None if insufficient data to display.
   499→    """
   500→    findings = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   501→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
   502→    )
   503→
   504→    structural = [
   505→        f
   506→        for f in findings.values()
   507→        if f["tier"] in (3, 4) and f["status"] in ("open", "wontfix")
   508→    ]
   509→
   510→    if len(structural) < 5:
   511→        return None
   512→
   513→    areas: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
   514→    for f in structural:
   515→        area = get_area(str(f.get("file", "")))
   516→        areas[area].append(f)
   517→
   518→    if len(areas) < 2:
   519→        return None
   520→
   521→    return sorted(areas.items(), key=lambda x: -sum(f["tier"] for f in x[1]))
   522→
   523→
   524→def _build_area_rows(sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]], *, max_areas: int = 15) -> list[list[str]]:
   525→    """Build table rows from sorted area findings."""
   526→    rows = []
   527→    for area, area_findings in sorted_areas[:max_areas]:
   528→        t3 = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["tier"] == 3)
   529→        t4 = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["tier"] == 4)
   530→        open_count = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["status"] == "open")
   531→        debt_count = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["status"] == "wontfix")
   532→        weight = sum(f["tier"] for f in area_findings)
   533→        rows.append(
   534→            [
   535→                area,
   536→                str(len(area_findings)),
   537→                f"T3:{t3} T4:{t4}",
   538→                str(open_count),
   539→                str(debt_count),
   540→                str(weight),
   541→            ]
   542→        )
   543→    return rows
   544→
   545→
   546→def _render_area_workflow(sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]], *, max_areas: int = 15) -> None:
   547→    """Print the overflow count and workflow instructions."""
   548→    remaining = len(sorted_areas) - max_areas
   549→    if remaining > 0:
   550→        print(colorize(f"\n  ... and {remaining} more areas", "dim"))
   551→
   552→    print(colorize("\n  Workflow:", "dim"))
   553→    print(colorize("    1. desloppify show <area> --status wontfix --top 50", "dim"))
   554→    print(
   555→        colorize(
   556→            "    2. Create tasks/<date>-<area-name>.md with decomposition plan", "dim"
   557→        )
   558→    )
   559→    print(
   560→        colorize("    3. Farm each task doc to a sub-agent for implementation", "dim")
   561→    )
   562→    print()
   563→
   564→
   565→def show_structural_areas(state: dict):
   566→    """Show structural debt grouped by area when T3/T4 debt is significant."""
   567→    sorted_areas = _collect_structural_areas(state)
   568→    if sorted_areas is None:
   569→        return
   570→
   571→    print(colorize("\n  ── Structural Debt by Area ──", "bold"))
   572→    print(
   573→        colorize(
   574→            "  Create a task doc for each area → farm to sub-agents for decomposition",
   575→            "dim",
   576→        )
   577→    )
   578→    print()
   579→
   580→    rows = _build_area_rows(sorted_areas)
   581→    print_table(
   582→        ["Area", "Items", "Tiers", "Open", "Debt", "Weight"], rows, [42, 6, 10, 5, 5, 7]
   583→    )
   584→
   585→    _render_area_workflow(sorted_areas)
   586→
   587→
   588→def show_review_summary(state: dict):
   589→    """Show review findings summary if any exist."""
   590→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   591→    review_open = [
   592→        f
   593→        for f in findings.values()
   594→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   595→    ]
   596→    if not review_open:
   597→        return
   598→    uninvestigated = sum(
   599→        1 for f in review_open if not f.get("detail", {}).get("investigation")
   600→    )
   601→    parts = [f"{len(review_open)} finding{'s' if len(review_open) != 1 else ''} open"]
   602→    if uninvestigated:
   603→        parts.append(f"{uninvestigated} uninvestigated")
   604→    print(colorize(f"  Review: {', '.join(parts)} — `desloppify show review --status open`", "cyan"))
   605→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   606→    if "Test health" in dim_scores:
   607→        print(
   608→            colorize(
   609→                "  Test health tracks coverage + review; review findings track issues found.",
   610→                "dim",
   611→            )
   612→        )
   613→    print()
```

```
1→"""Summary-line rendering helpers for the status command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     6→from desloppify.core.output_api import LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD, colorize
     7→
     8→
     9→def score_summary_lines(
    10→    *,
    11→    overall_score: float | None,
    12→    objective_score: float | None,
    13→    strict_score: float | None,
    14→    verified_strict_score: float | None,
    15→    target_strict: float | None = None,
    16→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    17→    """Return formatted top-line score summary rows."""
    18→    if (
    19→        overall_score is not None
    20→        and objective_score is not None
    21→        and strict_score is not None
    22→        and verified_strict_score is not None
    23→    ):
    24→        lines: list[tuple[str, str]] = [
    25→            (
    26→                f"\n  Scores: overall {overall_score:.1f}/100 · "
    27→                f"objective {objective_score:.1f}/100 · "
    28→                f"strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 · "
    29→                f"verified {verified_strict_score:.1f}/100",
    30→                "bold",
    31→            ),
    32→            (
    33→                "  Score guide: overall (lenient) · objective (mechanical only) · "
    34→                "strict (penalizes wontfix) · verified (scan-confirmed only)",
    35→                "dim",
    36→            ),
    37→        ]
    38→        if target_strict is not None:
    39→            gap = round(target_strict - strict_score, 1)
    40→            if gap > 0:
    41→                lines.append((
    42→                    f"  Strict {strict_score:.1f} (target: {target_strict:.1f})"
    43→                    " — run `desloppify next` to find the next improvement",
    44→                    "dim",
    45→                ))
    46→            else:
    47→                lines.append((
    48→                    f"  Strict {strict_score:.1f} — target {target_strict:.1f} reached!",
    49→                    "green",
    50→                ))
    51→        else:
    52→            lines.append(("  Focus on strict — it's your north star.", "dim"))
    53→        return lines
    54→    return [
    55→        ("\n  Scores unavailable", "bold"),
    56→        ("  Run a full scan to compute overall/objective/strict scores.", "yellow"),
    57→    ]
    58→
    59→
    60→def print_scan_metrics(state: dict) -> None:
    61→    """Print aggregate codebase metrics from the last scan."""
    62→    metrics = state.get("codebase_metrics", {})
    63→    total_files = sum(m.get("total_files", 0) for m in metrics.values())
    64→    total_loc = sum(m.get("total_loc", 0) for m in metrics.values())
    65→    total_dirs = sum(m.get("total_directories", 0) for m in metrics.values())
    66→    if total_files:
    67→        loc_str = f"{total_loc:,}" if total_loc < LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD else f"{total_loc // 1000}K"
    68→        print(
    69→            colorize(
    70→                f"  {total_files} files · {loc_str} LOC · {total_dirs} dirs · "
    71→                f"Last scan: {state.get('last_scan', 'never')}",
    72→                "dim",
    73→            )
    74→        )
    75→        return
    76→    print(
    77→        colorize(
    78→            f"  Scans: {state.get('scan_count', 0)} | Last: {state.get('last_scan', 'never')}",
    79→            "dim",
    80→        )
    81→    )
    82→
    83→
    84→def print_scan_completeness(state: dict) -> None:
    85→    """Warn when one or more language scans were partial."""
    86→    completeness = state.get("scan_completeness", {})
    87→    incomplete = [lang for lang, status in completeness.items() if status != "full"]
    88→    if incomplete:
    89→        print(
    90→            colorize(
    91→                f"  * Incomplete scan ({', '.join(incomplete)} — slow phases skipped)",
    92→                "yellow",
    93→            )
    94→        )
    95→
    96→
    97→def print_open_scope_breakdown(state: dict) -> None:
    98→    """Print open counts with explicit in-scope/out-of-scope semantics."""
    99→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   100→    if not isinstance(findings, dict):
   101→        return
   102→
   103→    counts = state_mod.open_scope_breakdown(findings, state.get("scan_path"))
   104→    print(
   105→        colorize(
   106→            "  "
   107→            f"open (in-scope): {counts['in_scope']} · "
   108→            f"open (out-of-scope carried): {counts['out_of_scope']} · "
   109→            f"open (global): {counts['global']}",
   110→            "dim",
   111→        )
   112→    )
   113→
   114→
   115→__all__ = [
   116→    "print_open_scope_breakdown",
   117→    "print_scan_completeness",
   118→    "print_scan_metrics",
   119→    "score_summary_lines",
   120→]
   121→
```

```
   362→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   363→    _render_tier_navigator(queue)
   364→    if not queue.get("fallback_reason"):
   365→        return
   366→    print(colorize(f"  {queue['fallback_reason']}", "yellow"))
   367→    if not explain:
   368→        return
   369→    available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   370→    if available:
   371→        tiers = ", ".join(f"T{tier_num}" for tier_num in available)
   372→        print(colorize(f"  explain: available tiers are {tiers}", "dim"))
   373→
   374→
   375→def show_empty_queue(
   376→    queue: dict,
   377→    tier: int | None,
   378→    strict: float | None,
   379→    *,
   380→    plan_start_strict: float | None = None,
   381→    target_strict: float | None = None,
   382→) -> bool:
   383→    if queue.get("items"):
   384→        return False
   385→    # Show before/after comparison when plan_start_strict is available
   386→    if plan_start_strict is not None and strict is not None:
   387→        delta = round(strict - plan_start_strict, 1)
   388→        delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if abs(delta) >= 0.05 else ""
   389→        print(colorize("\n  Queue cleared!", "green"))
   390→        print(colorize(
   391→            f"  Frozen plan-start: strict {plan_start_strict:.1f} → Live estimate: strict {strict:.1f}{delta_str}",
   392→            "cyan",
   393→        ))
   394→        print(colorize(
   395→            "  Run `desloppify scan` now to finalize and reveal your updated score.",
   396→            "dim",
   397→        ))
   398→    else:
   399→        suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   400→        print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   401→    if tier is not None:
   402→        print(colorize(f"  Requested tier: T{tier}", "dim"))
   403→        available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   404→        if available:
   405→            commands = " | ".join(
   406→                f"desloppify next --tier {tier_num}" for tier_num in available
   407→            )
   408→            print(colorize(f"  Try: {commands}", "dim"))
   409→    return True
   410→
   411→
   412→def _render_compact_item(item: dict, idx: int, total: int) -> None:
   413→    """One-line summary for cluster drill-in items after the first."""
   414→    tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   415→    tag = _effort_tag(item)
   416→    tag_str = f" {tag}" if tag else ""
   417→    fid = item.get("id", "")
   418→    short = fid.rsplit("::", 1)[-1][:8] if "::" in fid else fid
   419→    print(f"  [{idx + 1}/{total}] {_tier_label(tier)}{tag_str} {item.get('summary', '')}")
   420→    print(colorize(f"         {item.get('file', '')}  [{short}]", "dim"))
   421→
   422→
   423→def render_terminal_items(
   424→    items: list[dict],
   425→    dim_scores: dict,
   426→    findings_scoped: dict,
   427→    *,
   428→    group: str,
   429→    explain: bool,
   430→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   431→    plan: dict | None = None,
   432→    cluster_filter: str | None = None,
   433→) -> None:
   434→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   435→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   436→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   437→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   438→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   439→        total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   440→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({len(items)} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   441→
   442→    if group != "item":
   443→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   444→        return
   445→
   446→    # Detect cluster drill-in: multiple items with cluster focus active
   447→    is_cluster_drill = len(items) > 1 and (
   448→        cluster_filter or (plan and plan.get("active_cluster"))
   449→    )
   450→
   451→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   452→        if idx > 0:
   453→            print()
   454→        # Full card for first item, compact for rest in cluster drill-in
   455→        if is_cluster_drill and idx > 0:
   456→            _render_compact_item(item, idx, len(items))
   457→            continue
   458→        queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   459→        if queue_pos and len(items) > 1:
   460→            label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   461→        elif len(items) > 1:
   462→            label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]"
   463→        else:
   464→            pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   465→            label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   466→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   467→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials)
   468→
   469→
   470→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   471→    if len(items) != 1:
   472→        return
   473→    if items[0].get("kind") == "cluster":
   474→        return  # Cluster card already includes resolution hints
   475→    if items[0].get("kind") != "finding":
   476→        return
   477→    item = items[0]
   478→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   479→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   480→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   481→        primary = item.get(
   482→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   483→        )
   484→        print(f"    {primary}")
   485→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   486→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   487→        return
   488→
   489→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   490→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   491→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   492→        print(f"    {primary}")
   493→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   494→    else:
   495→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   496→
   497→    print(
   498→        f'    desloppify plan done "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" --confirm'
   499→    )
   500→    print(
   501→        f'    desloppify plan skip --permanent "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   502→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   503→    )
   504→
   505→
   506→def render_followup_nudges(
   507→    state: dict,
   508→    dim_scores: dict,
   509→    findings_scoped: dict,
   510→    *,
   511→    strict_score: float | None,
   512→    target_strict_score: float,
   513→    queue_total: int = 0,
   514→    plan_start_strict: float | None = None,
   515→) -> None:
   516→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   517→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   518→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   519→        state,
   520→        subjective_entries,
   521→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   522→        max_quality_items=3,
   523→        max_integrity_items=5,
   524→    )
   525→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   526→    # Show frozen plan-start score + queue remaining when in an active cycle
   527→    if queue_total > 0 and plan_start_strict is not None:
   528→        print(
   529→            colorize(
   530→                f"\n  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {plan_start_strict:.1f}/100",
   531→                "cyan",
   532→            )
   533→        )
   534→        print(
   535→            colorize(
   536→                f"  Queue: {queue_total} item{'s' if queue_total != 1 else ''}"
   537→                " remaining. Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   538→                "dim",
   539→            )
   540→        )
   541→    elif strict_score is not None:
   542→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   543→        if gap > 0:
   544→            print(
   545→                colorize(
   546→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   547→                    "cyan",
   548→                )
   549→            )
   550→        else:
   551→            print(
   552→                colorize(
   553→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   554→                    "green",
   555→                )
   556→            )
   557→    # Subjective bottleneck banner
   558→    if strict_score is not None and dim_scores:
   559→        try:
   560→            breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores)
   561→            subjective_drag = sum(
   562→                float(e.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0)
   563→                for e in breakdown.get("entries", [])
   564→                if isinstance(e, dict) and e.get("component") == "subjective"
   565→            )
   566→            mechanical_drag = sum(
   567→                float(e.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0)
   568→                for e in breakdown.get("entries", [])
   569→                if isinstance(e, dict) and e.get("component") != "subjective"
   570→            )
   571→            if subjective_drag > mechanical_drag and subjective_drag > 5.0:
   572→                print(colorize(
   573→                    f"\n  Subjective dimensions are the main bottleneck "
   574→                    f"(-{subjective_drag:.0f} pts vs -{mechanical_drag:.0f} pts mechanical).",
   575→                    "yellow",
   576→                ))
   577→                print(colorize(
   578→                    "  Code fixes alone won't close the gap — run "
   579→                    "`desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` "
   580→                    "to re-score.",
   581→                    "yellow",
   582→                ))
   583→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
   584→            log(f"  subjective bottleneck banner skipped: {exc}")
   585→
   586→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   587→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   588→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   589→
   590→    # Rescan nudge after structural work
   591→    if queue_total > 10:
   592→        print(colorize(
   593→            "\n  Tip: after structural fixes (splitting files, moving code), rescan to "
   594→            "let cascade effects settle: `desloppify scan --path .`",
   595→            "dim",
   596→        ))
   597→
   598→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   599→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   600→        findings_scoped
```

```
1→"""plan command: dispatcher for plan subcommands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.cluster_handlers import cmd_cluster_dispatch
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.move_handlers import cmd_plan_move

    20→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    21→from desloppify.engine.plan import append_log_entry, load_plan, reset_plan, save_plan
    22→
    23→
    24→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    25→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    26→    _plan_output_impl(args)
    27→
    28→
    29→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    30→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    31→    plan = load_plan()
    32→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    33→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    34→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    35→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    36→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    37→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    38→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    39→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    40→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    41→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    42→
    43→    # Count meaningful annotations (descriptions and notes)
    44→    described = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("description"))
    45→    noted = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("note"))
    46→
    47→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    48→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    49→    print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
    50→    if total_skipped:
    51→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
    52→    else:
    53→        print(f"  Skipped:          0")
    54→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    55→    if clusters:
    56→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    57→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    58→            member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    59→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
    60→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    61→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
    62→    if described or noted:
    63→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
    64→    if active:
    65→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    66→    if superseded:
    67→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
    68→
    69→
    70→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    71→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
    72→    plan = load_plan()
    73→    queue_len = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    74→    cluster_count = len(plan.get("clusters", {}))
    75→    reset_plan(plan)
    76→    append_log_entry(
    77→        plan, "reset", actor="user",
    78→        detail={"previous_queue_size": queue_len, "previous_cluster_count": cluster_count},
    79→    )
    80→    save_plan(plan)
    81→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
    82→
    83→
    84→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    85→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
    86→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
    87→
    88→    if plan_action is None:
    89→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
    90→        return
    91→
    92→    if plan_action == "show":
    93→        _cmd_plan_show(args)
    94→        return
    95→
    96→    if plan_action == "queue":
    97→        cmd_plan_queue(args)
    98→        return
    99→
   100→    if plan_action == "reset":
   101→        _cmd_plan_reset(args)
   102→        return
   103→
   104→    if plan_action == "move":
   105→        cmd_plan_move(args)
   106→        return
   107→
   108→    if plan_action in (
   109→        "describe", "note", "focus",
   110→        "skip", "unskip", "reopen", "done",
   111→    ):
   112→        dispatch = {
   113→            "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   114→            "done": cmd_plan_done,
   115→            "note": cmd_plan_note,
   116→            "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   117→            "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   118→            "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   119→            "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   120→        }
   121→        dispatch[plan_action](args)
   122→        return
   123→
   124→    if plan_action == "cluster":
   125→        cmd_cluster_dispatch(args)
   126→        return
   127→
   128→    if plan_action == "synthesize":
   129→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.synthesize_handlers import cmd_plan_synthesize
   130→
   131→        cmd_plan_synthesize(args)
   132→        return
   133→
   134→    print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   135→
   136→
   137→# Backwards-compatible alias
   138→cmd_plan_output = cmd_plan
   139→
   140→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
   141→
```

```
    43→def _print_score_lines(
    44→    *,

    50→    lines: list[str] = []
    51→    if overall_score is not None:
    52→        lines.append(f"Overall score:   {overall_score:.1f}/100")
    53→    if objective_score is not None:
    54→        lines.append(f"Objective score: {objective_score:.1f}/100")
    55→    if strict_score is not None:
    56→        lines.append(f"Strict score:    {strict_score:.1f}/100")
    57→    if verified_score is not None:

    59→    if lines:
    60→        print("\n".join(lines))
    61→    # Score legend — always shown in LLM block so agents understand the scoring model
    62→    print("Score guide:")
    63→    print("  overall  = 40% mechanical + 60% subjective (lenient — ignores wontfix)")
    64→    print("  objective = mechanical detectors only (no subjective review)")
    65→    print("  strict   = like overall, but wontfix counts against you  <-- your north star")
    66→    print("  verified = strict, but only credits scan-verified fixes")

    70→def _split_dimension_scores(
    71→    state: dict,
    72→    dim_scores: dict,

    74→    # Build dimension table from canonical scorecard projection.
    75→    rows = scorecard_projection_mod.scorecard_dimension_rows(
    76→        state, dim_scores=dim_scores
    77→    )
    78→    subjective_name_set = {name.lower() for name in scoring_mod.DISPLAY_NAMES.values()}
    79→    subjective_name_set.update({"elegance", "elegance (combined)"})

    81→    mechanical = [

    87→        )
    88→    ]
    89→    subjective = [
    90→        (name, data)
    91→        for name, data in rows
    92→        if (

    94→            or str(name).strip().lower() in subjective_name_set
    95→        )
    96→    ]
    97→    return mechanical, subjective

   100→def _print_dimension_table(state: dict, dim_scores: dict) -> None:

   129→def _print_drag_summary(dim_scores: dict) -> None:
   130→    """Print the biggest score-drag dimensions so agents know where to focus."""
   131→    if not dim_scores:
   132→        return
   133→    try:
   134→        breakdown = scoring_mod.compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores)
   135→        entries = breakdown.get("entries", [])
   136→        drags = sorted(
   137→            [e for e in entries if isinstance(e, dict) and float(e.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0) > 0.01],
   138→            key=lambda e: -float(e.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0),
   139→        )
   140→        if drags:
   141→            print("Biggest score drags (fixing these dimensions has the most impact):")
   142→            for entry in drags[:5]:
   143→                print(
   144→                    f"  - {entry['name']}: -{float(entry['overall_drag']):.2f} pts "
   145→                    f"(score {float(entry['score']):.1f}%, "
   146→                    f"{float(entry['pool_share'])*100:.1f}% of {entry['pool']} pool)"
   147→                )
   148→            print()
   149→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
   150→        logger.debug("Drag summary skipped: %s", exc)
   151→
   152→
   153→def _print_stats_summary(
   154→    state: dict,
   155→    diff: dict | None,
   156→    *,
   157→    overall_score: float | None,
   158→    strict_score: float | None,
   159→) -> None:
   160→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   161→    if not stats:
   162→        return
   163→
   164→    wontfix = stats.get("wontfix", 0)
   165→    ignored = diff.get("ignored", 0) if diff else 0
   166→    ignore_pats = diff.get("ignore_patterns", 0) if diff else 0
   167→    strict_gap = (
   168→        round((overall_score or 0) - (strict_score or 0), 1)
   169→        if overall_score and strict_score
   170→        else 0

   172→    print(

   181→        )

   213→def _detect_agent_interface() -> str | None:
   214→    """Detect the current agent interface from environment variables."""
   215→    if os.environ.get("CLAUDE_CODE"):
   216→        return "claude"
   217→    if os.environ.get("GEMINI_CLI"):
   218→        return "gemini"

   222→        return "cursor"
   223→    return None
   224→
   225→
   226→def _try_auto_update_skill() -> None:
   227→    """Attempt to auto-install or auto-update the skill document.
   228→
   229→    Best-effort: swallows all exceptions so a network failure or permission
   230→    error never breaks the scan.
   231→    """
   232→    install = skill_docs_mod.find_installed_skill()
   233→
   234→    if install and not install.stale:
   235→        return  # Up to date.
   236→
   237→    try:
   238→        if install:
   239→            interface = resolve_interface(install=install)
   240→        else:
   241→            interface = _detect_agent_interface()
   242→
   243→        if interface:
   244→            update_installed_skill(interface)
   245→    except (ImportError, OSError, RuntimeError, ValueError) as exc:
   246→        logger.debug("Skill auto-update skipped: %s", exc, exc_info=True)
   247→
   248→
   249→def _print_badge_hint(badge_path: Path | None) -> None:
   250→    if not (badge_path and badge_path.exists()):
   251→        return
   252→
   253→    rel_path = badge_path.name if badge_path.parent == PROJECT_ROOT else str(badge_path)
   254→    print(f"A scorecard image was saved to `{rel_path}`.")
   255→    print("Let the user know they can view it, and suggest adding it")
   256→    print(f'to their README: `<img src="{rel_path}" width="100%">`')
   257→
   258→
   259→def _print_llm_summary(
   260→    state: dict,
   261→    badge_path: Path | None,
   262→    narrative: dict | None = None,
   263→    diff: dict | None = None,
   264→):
   265→    """Print a structured summary for LLM consumption.
   266→
   267→    The LLM reads terminal output after running scans. This gives it
   268→    clear instructions on how to present the results to the end user.
   269→    Only shown when running inside an agent (CLAUDE_CODE or DESLOPPIFY_AGENT env).
   270→    """
   271→    if not _is_agent_environment():
   272→        return
   273→
   274→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   275→    scores = _load_scores(state)
   276→
   277→    if (
   278→        scores.overall is None
   279→        and scores.objective is None
   280→        and scores.strict is None
   281→        and scores.verified is None
   282→        and not dim_scores
   283→    ):
   284→        return
   285→
   286→    print("─" * 60)
   287→    print("INSTRUCTIONS FOR LLM")
   288→    print("IMPORTANT: ALWAYS present ALL scores to the user after a scan.")
   289→    print("Show overall health (lenient + strict), ALL dimension scores,")
   290→    print("AND all subjective dimension scores in a markdown table.")
   291→    print("The goal is to maximize strict scores. Never skip the scores.\n")
   292→
   293→    # Check for living plan
   294→    try:
   295→        _plan = load_plan()
   296→        _has_plan = bool(
   297→            _plan.get("queue_order") or _plan.get("clusters")
   298→            or _plan.get("skipped")
   299→        )
   300→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   301→        _plan = {}
   302→        _has_plan = False
   303→
   304→    if _has_plan:
   305→        ordered = len(_plan.get("queue_order", []))
   306→        skipped = len(_plan.get("skipped", {}))
   307→        active = _plan.get("active_cluster")
   308→        print(f"LIVING PLAN ACTIVE: {ordered} ordered, {skipped} skipped.")
   309→        if active:
   310→            cluster = _plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active, {})
   311→            remaining = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   312→            print(f"Focused on: {active} ({remaining} items remaining).")
   313→        print("The plan is the single source of truth for work order.")
   314→        print("Use `desloppify next` which respects the plan.")
   315→        print("Use `desloppify plan` to view and update it.\n")
   316→
   317→    _print_score_lines(
   318→        overall_score=scores.overall,
   319→        objective_score=scores.objective,
   320→        strict_score=scores.strict,
   321→        verified_score=scores.verified,
   322→    )
   323→    _print_dimension_table(state, dim_scores)
   324→    _print_drag_summary(dim_scores)
   325→    _print_stats_summary(
   326→        state,
   327→        diff,
   328→        overall_score=scores.overall,
   329→        strict_score=scores.strict,
   330→    )
   331→    if _has_plan:
   332→        print("\nFollow the living plan: `desloppify next` for your next task,")
   333→        print("`desloppify plan` to view the full queue.")
   334→    else:
   335→        _print_workflow_guide()
   336→    _print_narrative_status(narrative)
   337→    _print_badge_hint(badge_path)
   338→    print("─" * 60)
   339→
   340→
   341→def auto_update_skill() -> None:
   342→    """Auto-install or update the skill document if we detect an agent.
   343→
   344→    Called unconditionally from the scan workflow — not gated on scores.
   345→    """
   346→    if not _is_agent_environment():
   347→        return
   348→
   349→    _try_auto_update_skill()
   350→
```

```
   131→def _print_drag_summary(dim_scores: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   132→    """Print the biggest score-drag dimensions so agents know where to focus."""

   135→    try:
   136→        breakdown = scoring_mod.compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores)

   139→            [e for e in entries if isinstance(e, dict) and float(e.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0) > 0.01],
   140→            key=lambda e: -float(e.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0),
   141→        )
   142→        if drags:
   143→            print("Biggest score drags (fixing these dimensions has the most impact):")
   144→            for entry in drags[:5]:
   145→                print(

   147→                    f"(score {float(entry['score']):.1f}%, "
   148→                    f"{float(entry['pool_share'])*100:.1f}% of {entry['pool']} pool)"

   339→def _load_living_plan_snapshot() -> tuple[dict[str, object], bool]:
   340→    fallback: dict[str, object] = {
   341→        "queue_order": [],
   342→        "clusters": {},
   343→        "skipped": {},
   344→        "active_cluster": None,
   345→    }
   346→    try:

   354→    clusters = loaded_plan.get("clusters")
   355→    skipped = loaded_plan.get("skipped")
   356→    active = loaded_plan.get("active_cluster")
   357→
   358→    snapshot = {
   359→        "queue_order": queue_order if isinstance(queue_order, list) else [],
   360→        "clusters": clusters if isinstance(clusters, dict) else {},
   361→        "skipped": skipped if isinstance(skipped, dict) else {},
   362→        "active_cluster": active if isinstance(active, str) and active else None,
   363→    }
   364→    has_plan = bool(snapshot["queue_order"] or snapshot["clusters"] or snapshot["skipped"])
   365→    return snapshot, has_plan

   377→        print(f"Focused on: {active} ({remaining} items remaining).")
   378→    print("The plan is the single source of truth for work order.")
   379→    print("Use `desloppify next` which respects the plan.")
   380→    print("Use `desloppify plan` to view and update it.\n")
```

```
    37→def _scorecard_subjective(
    38→    state: dict,
    39→    dim_scores: dict,
    40→) -> list[dict]:
    41→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    42→    return next_render_mod.scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
    43→
    44→
    45→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    46→    state: dict,
    47→    dim_scores: dict,
    48→    *,
    49→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    50→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    51→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    52→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    53→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    54→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    55→            continue
    56→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    57→        if strict_val < threshold:
    58→            low.append(
    59→                (
    60→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    61→                    strict_val,
    62→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    63→                )
    64→            )
    65→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    66→    return low
    67→
    68→
    69→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    70→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    71→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    72→    state = runtime.state
    73→    config = runtime.config
    74→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    75→        return
    76→
    77→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    78→    if stale_warning:
    79→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    80→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    81→    if skill_warning:
    82→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    83→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    84→    if config_warning:
    85→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    86→
    87→    print_synthesis_guardrail_info(state=state)
    88→    _get_items(args, state, config)
    89→
    90→
    91→def _resolve_cluster_focus(
    92→    plan_data: dict | None,
    93→    *,
    94→    cluster_arg: str | None,
    95→    scope: str | None,
    96→) -> str | None:
    97→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
    98→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
    99→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
   100→        if active_cluster:
   101→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   102→    return effective_cluster

   106→    *,

   108→    items: list[dict],
   109→    state: dict,

   111→    plan_data: dict | None,

   113→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(

   115→    )
   116→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   117→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   118→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   119→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   120→        state,
   121→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   122→    )
   123→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   124→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   125→    ]
   126→    return payload
   127→
   128→
   129→def _emit_requested_output(
   130→    args,
   131→    payload: dict,
   132→    items: list[dict],
   133→) -> bool:
   134→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   135→    if output_file:
   136→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   137→            output_file,
   138→            payload,
   139→            len(items),
   140→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   141→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   142→        ):
   143→            return True
   144→        raise SystemExit(1)
   145→
   146→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
   147→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):
   148→        return True
   149→    return False
   150→
   151→
   152→def _plan_queue_context(
   153→    *,

   156→) -> tuple[float | None, int]:
   157→    plan_start_strict = get_plan_start_strict(plan_data)
   158→    if plan_start_strict is None:
   159→        return None, 0
   160→    try:

   162→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   163→        queue_total = 0
   164→    return plan_start_strict, queue_total
```

```
1→"""Output helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_write_error
    10→
    11→
    12→def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    13→    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    14→    # Cluster meta-items get their own serialization
    15→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    16→        members_raw = item.get("members", [])
    17→        serialized_members = [serialize_item(m) for m in members_raw]
    18→        return {
    19→            "id": item.get("id"),
    20→            "kind": "cluster",
    21→            "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
    22→            "summary": item.get("summary"),
    23→            "member_count": item.get("member_count", len(serialized_members)),
    24→            "members": serialized_members,
    25→            "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    26→            "cluster_name": item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id")),
    27→            "cluster_auto": item.get("cluster_auto", True),
    28→            "detector": item.get("detector"),
    29→        }
    30→
    31→    serialized: dict[str, Any] = {
    32→        "id": item.get("id"),
    33→        "kind": item.get("kind", "finding"),
    34→        "tier": item.get("tier"),
    35→        "effective_tier": item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier")),
    36→        "confidence": item.get("confidence"),
    37→        "detector": item.get("detector"),
    38→        "file": item.get("file"),
    39→        "summary": item.get("summary"),
    40→        "detail": item.get("detail", {}),
    41→        "status": item.get("status"),
    42→        "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    43→    }
    44→    explain = item.get("explain")
    45→    if explain is not None:
    46→        serialized["explain"] = explain
    47→
    48→    # Plan metadata
    49→    if item.get("queue_position"):
    50→        serialized["queue_position"] = item["queue_position"]
    51→    if item.get("plan_description"):
    52→        serialized["plan_description"] = item["plan_description"]
    53→    if item.get("plan_note"):
    54→        serialized["plan_note"] = item["plan_note"]
    55→    if item.get("plan_cluster"):
    56→        serialized["plan_cluster"] = item["plan_cluster"]
    57→    if item.get("plan_skipped"):
    58→        serialized["plan_skipped"] = True
    59→        serialized["plan_skip_kind"] = item.get("plan_skip_kind", "temporary")
    60→        if item.get("plan_skip_reason"):
    61→            serialized["plan_skip_reason"] = item["plan_skip_reason"]
    62→
    63→    return serialized
    64→
    65→
    66→def build_query_payload(
    67→    queue: Mapping[str, Any],
    68→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    69→    *,
    70→    command: str,
    71→    narrative: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    72→    plan: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    73→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    74→    """Build JSON payload for query.json and non-terminal output modes."""
    75→    serialized = [serialize_item(item) for item in items]
    76→    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
    77→        "command": command,
    78→        "items": serialized,
    79→        "queue": {
    80→            "tier_counts": queue.get("tier_counts", {}),
    81→            "requested_tier": queue.get("requested_tier"),
    82→            "selected_tier": queue.get("selected_tier"),
    83→            "fallback_reason": queue.get("fallback_reason"),
    84→            "available_tiers": queue.get("available_tiers", []),
    85→            "total": queue.get("total", len(items)),
    86→        },
    87→        "narrative": narrative,
    88→    }
    89→
    90→    if plan and (
    91→        plan.get("queue_order")
    92→        or plan.get("skipped")
    93→        or plan.get("clusters")
    94→    ):
    95→        clusters_summary = []
    96→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    97→            member_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    98→            clusters_summary.append({
    99→                "name": name,
   100→                "description": cluster.get("description"),
   101→                "item_count": len(member_ids),
   102→            })
   103→        payload["plan"] = {
   104→            "active": True,
   105→            "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
   106→            "clusters": clusters_summary,
   107→            "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
   108→            "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
   109→            "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
   110→        }
   111→
   112→    return payload
   113→
   114→
   115→def render_markdown(items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
   116→    """Render queue items as markdown."""
   117→    lines = [
   118→        "# Desloppify Next Queue",
   119→        "",
   120→        "| Kind | Tier | Confidence | Summary | Command |",
   121→        "|------|------|------------|---------|---------|",
   122→    ]
   123→    for item in items:
   124→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   125→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   126→        conf = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   127→        summary = item.get("summary", "").replace("|", "\\|")
   128→        command = (item.get("primary_command", "") or "").replace("|", "\\|")
   129→        lines.append(f"| {kind} | T{tier} | {conf} | {summary} | {command} |")
   130→    lines.append("")
   131→    return "\n".join(lines)
   132→
   133→
   134→def write_output_file(
   135→    output_file: str,
   136→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   137→    item_count: int,
   138→    *,
   139→    safe_write_text_fn,
   140→    colorize_fn,
   141→) -> bool:
   142→    """Persist payload to file and print success/failure hints."""
   143→    try:
   144→        safe_write_text_fn(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   145→        print(colorize_fn(f"Wrote {item_count} items to {output_file}", "green"))
   146→    except OSError as exc:
   147→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
   148→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="next output")
   149→        return False
   150→    return True
```

```
    37→
    38→def _show_concerns(state: dict, lang_name: str | None) -> None:
    39→    """Render current design concerns from mechanical signals."""
    40→    concerns = generate_concerns(state, lang_name=lang_name)
    41→    if not concerns:
    42→        print(colorize("  No design concerns detected.", "green"))
    43→        return
    44→
    45→    print(colorize(f"\n  {len(concerns)} design concern(s):\n", "bold"))
    46→    dismissals = state.get("concern_dismissals", {})
    47→
    48→    for i, c in enumerate(concerns, 1):
    49→        print(colorize(f"  {i}. [{c.type}] {c.file}", "cyan"))
    50→        print(f"     {c.summary}")
    51→        for ev in c.evidence:
    52→            print(colorize(f"       - {ev}", "dim"))
    53→        print(colorize(f"     ? {c.question}", "yellow"))
    54→
    55→        # Check if previously dismissed (but resurface due to changed findings).
    56→        prev = dismissals.get(c.fingerprint)
    57→        if isinstance(prev, dict):
    58→            reasoning = prev.get("reasoning", "")
    59→            if reasoning:
    60→                print(colorize(f"     (previously dismissed: {reasoning})", "dim"))
    61→        print()
    62→
    63→
    64→def _print_dimension_score(dim_data: dict, display_name: str) -> None:
    65→    """Print the health/strict score line for a dimension if available."""
    66→    score_val = dim_data.get("score") if isinstance(dim_data, dict) else None
    67→    strict_val = (
    68→        dim_data.get("strict", score_val) if isinstance(dim_data, dict) else None
    69→    )
    70→    if score_val is not None:
    71→        print(
    72→            colorize(
    73→                f"  {display_name}: {score_val:.1f}% health (strict: {strict_val:.1f}%)",
    74→                "bold",
    75→            )
    76→        )
    77→
    78→
    79→def _render_subjective_dimension(
    80→    state: dict,
    81→    config: dict,
    82→    entity,
    83→    pattern_raw: str,
    84→) -> None:
    85→    """Show score + subjective explanation for a subjective dimension."""
    86→    lowered = pattern_raw.strip().lower().replace(" ", "_") if pattern_raw else ""
    87→    dim_data, display_name = _lookup_dimension_score(state, entity.display_name)
    88→    _print_dimension_score(dim_data, display_name)
    89→    print(
    90→        colorize(
    91→            f"  '{pattern_raw.strip()}' is a subjective dimension "
    92→            "— its score comes from design reviews, not code findings.",
    93→            "yellow",
    94→        )
    95→    )
    96→    # Count open review findings tagged with this dimension
    97→    dim_reviews = [
    98→        f
    99→        for f in (state.get("findings") or {}).values()
   100→        if f.get("detector") == "review"
   101→        and f.get("status") == "open"
   102→        and lowered
   103→        in str(f.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")).lower().replace(" ", "_")
   104→    ]
   105→    if dim_reviews:
   106→        print(
   107→            colorize(
   108→                f"  {len(dim_reviews)} open review finding(s). "
   109→                "Run `show review --status open`.",
   110→                "dim",
   111→            )
   112→        )
   113→    show_subjective_followup(
   114→        state,
   115→        target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0),
   116→    )
   117→
   118→
   119→def _render_clean_mechanical_dimension(state: dict, entity) -> None:
   120→    """Show score + 'no open findings' for a mechanical dimension with zero findings."""
   121→    dim_data, display_name = _lookup_dimension_score(state, entity.display_name)
   122→    _print_dimension_score(dim_data, display_name)
   123→    det_list = ", ".join(entity.detectors) if entity.detectors else "none"
   124→    print(
   125→        colorize(
   126→            f"  No open findings for {entity.display_name}. Detectors: {det_list}",
   127→            "green",
   128→        )
   129→    )
   130→
   131→
   132→def _load_dimension_findings(
   133→    state: dict,
   134→    entity,
   135→    status_filter: str,
   136→) -> list[dict]:
   137→    """Load findings for all detectors in a mechanical dimension."""
   138→    all_matches: list[dict] = []
   139→    for detector in entity.detectors:
   140→        matches = load_matches(
   141→            state, scope=detector, status_filter=status_filter, chronic=False
   142→        )
   143→        all_matches.extend(matches)
   144→    # Deduplicate by finding ID
   145→    seen: set[str] = set()
   146→    unique: list[dict] = []
   147→    for item in all_matches:
   148→        fid = item.get("id", "")
   149→        if fid not in seen:
   150→            seen.add(fid)
   151→            unique.append(item)
   152→    return unique
   153→
   154→
   155→def _render_no_matches(entity, pattern, status_filter, narrative, state, config):
   156→    """Handle the no-findings case: subjective views show dashboard, others show hint."""
   157→    print(
   158→        colorize(f"No {status_filter} findings matching: {pattern}", "yellow")
   159→    )
   160→    write_query(
   161→        {
   162→            "command": "show",
   163→            "query": pattern,
   164→            "status_filter": status_filter,
   165→            "total": 0,
   166→            "findings": [],
   167→            "narrative": narrative,
   168→        }
   169→    )
   170→    if entity.kind == "special_view":
   171→        show_subjective_followup(
   172→            state,
   173→            target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0),
   174→        )
   175→    else:
   176→        hint = _detector_names_hint()
   177→        print(
   178→            colorize(
   179→                f"  Try: show <detector>, show <file>, or show subjective. "
   180→                f"Detectors: {hint}",
   181→                "dim",
   182→            )
   183→        )
   184→
   185→
   186→def _render_subjective_views_guide(entity) -> None:
   187→    """Print 'Related views' hint after subjective/subjective_review output."""
   188→    if entity.kind == "special_view" and entity.pattern.strip().lower() in (
   189→        "subjective",
   190→        "subjective_review",
   191→    ):
   192→        print(colorize("  Related views:", "dim"))
   193→        print(colorize("    `show review --status open`            Per-file design review findings", "dim"))
   194→        print(colorize("    `show subjective_review --status open`  Files needing re-review", "dim"))
   195→
   196→
   197→def cmd_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   198→    """Show all findings for a file, directory, detector, or pattern."""
   199→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   200→    state = runtime.state
   201→    config = runtime.config
   202→
   203→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   204→        return
   205→
   206→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
   207→    if stale_warning:
   208→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
   209→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
   210→    if skill_warning:
   211→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
   212→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
   213→    if config_warning:
   214→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
   215→
   216→    print_synthesis_guardrail_info(state=state)
   217→
   218→    pattern_raw = getattr(args, "pattern", "")
   219→    show_code = getattr(args, "code", False)
   220→    chronic = getattr(args, "chronic", False)
   221→    ok, pattern, status_filter, scope = resolve_show_scope(args)
   222→    if not ok or pattern is None:
   223→        return
   224→
   225→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   226→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   227→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   228→        state,
   229→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="show"),
   230→    )
   231→
   232→    # --- Entity resolution: classify what the user asked for ---
   233→    entity = resolve_entity(pattern, state)
   234→
   235→    # Dispatch: concerns special view
   236→    if entity.kind == "special_view" and entity.pattern.strip().lower() == "concerns":
   237→        _show_concerns(state, lang_name)
   238→        return
   239→
   240→    # Dispatch: subjective dimension
   241→    if entity.kind == "dimension" and entity.is_subjective:
   242→        _render_subjective_dimension(state, config, entity, pattern_raw)
   243→        return
   244→
   245→    # Dispatch: mechanical dimension
   246→    if entity.kind == "dimension" and not entity.is_subjective:
   247→        matches = _load_dimension_findings(state, entity, status_filter)
   248→        if not matches:
   249→            _render_clean_mechanical_dimension(state, entity)
   250→            return
```

```
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify import scoring as scoring_mod
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.core import registry as registry_mod
    10→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
    11→    QueueBuildOptions,
    12→    build_work_queue,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    15→
    16→
    17→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    18→class ResolvedEntity:
    19→    """Classifies what the user's show pattern refers to."""
    20→
    21→    kind: str  # "dimension", "special_view", "file_or_pattern"
    22→    pattern: str  # Original user pattern
    23→    display_name: str  # Human-readable label
    24→    detectors: tuple[str, ...] = ()  # Detector names (for dimension kind)
    25→    is_subjective: bool = False  # Whether this is a subjective dimension
    26→
    27→
    28→def _resolve_special_view(pattern: str, lowered: str) -> ResolvedEntity | None:
    29→    if lowered not in ("concerns", "subjective", "subjective_review"):
    30→        return None
    31→    return ResolvedEntity(
    32→        kind="special_view",
    33→        pattern=pattern,
    34→        display_name=pattern,
    35→    )
    36→
    37→
    38→def _resolve_mechanical_dimension(pattern: str, lowered: str) -> ResolvedEntity | None:
    39→    lookup = _build_dimension_lookup()
    40→    detectors = lookup.get(lowered) or lookup.get(pattern.lower())
    41→    if not detectors:
    42→        return None
    43→    dim_display = pattern
    44→    for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
    45→        dim_lowered = dim.name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
    46→        if dim.name.lower() == lowered or dim_lowered == lowered:
    47→            dim_display = dim.name
    48→            break
    49→    return ResolvedEntity(
    50→        kind="dimension",
    51→        pattern=pattern,
    52→        display_name=dim_display,
    53→        detectors=tuple(detectors),
    54→        is_subjective=False,
    55→    )
    56→
    57→
    58→def _resolve_subjective_dimension(
    59→    pattern: str,
    60→    lowered: str,
    61→    state: dict,
    62→) -> ResolvedEntity | None:
    63→    display_name = scoring_mod.DISPLAY_NAMES.get(lowered)
    64→    if not display_name:
    65→        for key in state.get("dimension_scores") or {}:
    66→            if key.lower().replace(" ", "_") == lowered:
    67→                display_name = key
    68→                break
    69→    if not display_name:
    70→        return None
    71→    dim_data, display_name = _lookup_dimension_score(state, display_name)
    72→    is_subj = "subjective_assessment" in (
    73→        dim_data.get("detectors", {}) if isinstance(dim_data, dict) else {}
    74→    )
    75→    return ResolvedEntity(
    76→        kind="dimension",
    77→        pattern=pattern,
    78→        display_name=display_name,
    79→        detectors=(),
    80→        is_subjective=is_subj,
    81→    )
    82→
    83→
    84→def resolve_entity(pattern: str, state: dict) -> ResolvedEntity:
    85→    """Classify a user pattern as a dimension, special view, or passthrough.
    86→
    87→    Resolution priority:
    88→    1. Special views: "concerns", "subjective", "subjective_review"
    89→    2. Mechanical dimension name (from DIMENSIONS)
    90→    3. Subjective dimension name (from DISPLAY_NAMES / dimension_scores)
    91→    4. Everything else: file_or_pattern passthrough
    92→    """
    93→    lowered = pattern.strip().lower().replace(" ", "_")
    94→
    95→    # 1. Special views
    96→    special_view = _resolve_special_view(pattern, lowered)
    97→    if special_view is not None:
    98→        return special_view
    99→
   100→    # 2. Mechanical dimension (via DIMENSIONS list)
```

```
    38→def group_matches_by_file(matches: list[dict]) -> list[tuple[str, list]]:
    39→    """Group findings by file and sort by descending count."""
    40→    by_file: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
```

## SUMMARY TABLE

| Location | File | Line(s) | Numbers Displayed | Source |
|----------|------|---------|-------------------|--------|
| Plan queue table | plan/queue_render.py | 62, 64 | Non-skipped entries, tier counts | build_work_queue() tier_counts |
| Plan show summary | plan/cmd.py | 49, 51, 58, 61 | Ordered items, skipped, clusters, members | plan JSON structure |
| Plan agent block | helpers/rendering.py | 42, 46 | Ordered, skipped, remaining in cluster | plan JSON structure |
| Queue progress | helpers/queue_progress.py | 50 | Queue remaining | plan_aware_queue_count() |
| Next tier navigator | next_parts/render.py | 54-55 | Open per tier | queue.tier_counts |
| Next focused cluster | next_parts/render.py | 440 | Items of total | cluster.finding_ids |
| Next queue nudge | next_parts/render.py | 536 | Queue total | plan_aware_queue_count() |
| Status open breakdown | status_parts/summary.py | 107-109 | In-scope, out-of-scope, global | state_mod.open_scope_breakdown() |
| Status focus suggestion | status_parts/render.py | 365 | Items remaining in cluster | cluster.finding_ids |
| Scan LLM block | scan_reporting_llm.py | 308, 312 | Ordered, skipped, remaining | plan JSON |
| Scan agent context | reporting/agent_context.py | 372, 377 | Ordered, skipped, remaining | plan JSON |
| Show findings list | show/render.py | 103, 141 | Total matches, per-file counts | Filtered findings |
| Status queue context | status_cmd.py | 117 | Queue remaining (frozen score) | plan_aware_queue_count() |
| Query.json (next) | next_parts/output.py | 80, 85, 107-108 | tier_counts, total, ordered, skipped | queue dict & plan JSON |
| Query.json (status) | status_parts/render.py | 121-122 | Ordered, skipped | plan JSON |

All queue counts ultimately derive from:
1. **`plan_aware_queue_count()`** → calls `build_work_queue()` → returns `result["total"]`
2. **`build_work_queue()`** → returns dict with `"total"` (item count) and `"tier_counts"` (per-tier breakdown)
3. **`tier_counts(items)`** function → returns {1: N, 2: N, 3: N, 4: N}
4. **Plan JSON structure** → `queue_order[]`, `skipped{}`, `clusters[].finding_ids[]`
5. **State scope breakdown** → `state_mod.open_scope_breakdown(findings, scan_path)` → in-scope/out-of-scope/global counts
